\makeatletter
\newif\ifspecintwocol \specintwocolfalse
\newif\ifspecresume   \specresumefalse
\newcommand{\StartTwoCol}{%
  \if@twocolumn
    \let\spec@next\@empty
  \else
    \ifspecintwocol
      \let\spec@next\@empty
    \else
      \global\specintwocoltrue
      \def\spec@next{\begin{multicols}{2}}%
    \fi
  \fi
  \spec@next
}
\newcommand{\StopTwoCol}{%
  \if@twocolumn
    \onecolumn
    \global\specintwocolfalse
    \let\spec@next\@empty
  \else
    \ifspecintwocol
      \global\specintwocolfalse
      \def\spec@next{\end{multicols}}%
    \else
      \let\spec@next\@empty
    \fi
  \fi
  \spec@next
}
\newcommand{\BeginConnesPlate}{%
  \if@twocolumn
    \onecolumn
    \global\specresumetrue
    \let\spec@platepre\@empty
  \else
    \ifspecintwocol
      \global\specresumetrue
      \let\spec@platepre\StopTwoCol
    \else
      \global\specresumefalse
      \let\spec@platepre\@empty
    \fi
  \fi
  \spec@platepre
  \par\vspace{4pt}\noindent\textcolor{black!25}{\rule{\textwidth}{0.4pt}}\par\vspace{3pt}%
}
\newcommand{\EndConnesPlate}{%
  \par\vspace{3pt}\noindent\textcolor{black!25}{\rule{\textwidth}{0.4pt}}\par\vspace{4pt}%
  \ifspecresume
    \global\specresumefalse
    \let\spec@platepost\StartTwoCol
  \else
    \let\spec@platepost\@empty
  \fi
  \spec@platepost
}
\newcommand{\MidConnesRule}{%
  \par\vspace{4pt}\noindent\textcolor{black!25}{\rule{\textwidth}{0.4pt}}\par\vspace{4pt}%
}

\title{\vspace{-2.0em}{\fontsize{14.5}{17.4}\selectfont\bfseries\hyphenpenalty=10000\exhyphenpenalty=10000
Stateless Sub-Flit Capability Sanitization, Algebraic Cohort Ratcheting,\\
and Post-Route Formal Verification for Disaggregated CXL~3.0--4.0\\
and UCIe Memory Fabrics\par}\vspace{-0.35em}}

\author{
  \textbf{Eric C. Murphy}\thanks{Correspondence: \href{mailto:eric@spectralsystems.org}{\texttt{eric@spectralsystems.org}}. Open-source Lean~4 formal proofs, cycle-accurate simulators, and post-route silicon receipts: \url{https://github.com/spectralsystems/spectral-cxl-flit-sanitizer} (\texttt{Apache-2.0}; commercial hardware synthesis and silicon implementation rights reserved; all patents pending).}\\
  {\small Spectral Systems LLC}\\
  {\footnotesize\href{mailto:eric@spectralsystems.org}{\texttt{eric@spectralsystems.org}} \;|\; \url{https://github.com/spectralsystems/spectral-cxl-flit-sanitizer}}
}

\date{\vspace{-0.3em}November 2026\vspace{-0.3em}}

\begin{document}

\onecolumn
\maketitle
\thispagestyle{plain}

\vspace{-0.55em}
\begin{center}
\begin{minipage}{0.90\textwidth}
\centering\footnotesize\itshape
``There is no point in using exact methods where there is no clarity in the concepts and the issues to which they are to be applied.'' ---~\upshape John~von~Neumann~(1949).\vspace{0.12em}\\
\itshape
``Information is not a disembodied abstract entity; it is always tied to a physical representation.'' ---~\upshape Rolf~Landauer~(1991)~\cite{landauer1961irreversibility}.
\end{minipage}
\end{center}
\vspace{-0.20em}

\begin{abstract}
Disaggregating High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM2e/HBM3/HBM4) and DDR5 over Compute Express Link (\mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0}~\cite{cxl2025spec}) and Universal Chiplet Interconnect Express (\mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0}~\cite{ucie2025spec30}) is foundational for multi-tenant cloud and AI supercomputing fabrics. Existing hardware isolation mechanisms enforce an acute trade-off among memory capacity, tail latency, and link availability. Stateful permission-table firewalls (\textit{Space-Control}~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol}) carve out $1.5625\%$ of pooled DRAM ($250\text{~GB}$ decimal per $16\text{~TB}$ rack / $256\text{~GiB}$ binary per $16\text{~TiB}$ rack) and incur $32$--$68\text{~ns}$ table-walk stalls on permission-cache misses, while tagged-memory architectures (CHERI / ARM Morello~\cite{watson2015cheri}) impose a $12.5\%$ metadata tax. Conversely, stateless link firewalls that abort unauthorized accesses by poisoning the tail Cyclic Redundancy Check (CRC) of a 512-bit CXL flit phase destroy up to three co-packed innocent 128-bit slots and trigger link-wide Go-Back-N NAK replay storms.

We present a unified algebraic, coding-theoretic, and stochastic framework for Layer-1/2 Zero-Trust sub-flit access control and introduce \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} (\ReleaseVersion), a zero-BRAM, cut-through capability sanitizer for \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} and \mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0}~\cite{cxl2025spec,ucie2025spec30}. The interposer decouples the 14-bit requester $\mathrm{SPID}_i$ onto a prefix-guarded 64-tenant live bitmap and evaluates spatial and temporal capability projections ($\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) = r_i \wedge P_{\mathrm{eff}}$ and $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) = r_i \wedge \neg P_{\mathrm{eff}}$) over $\mathbb{F}_2^{64}$---qualified by the non-zero admissible set $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{adm}} \subset \mathbb{F}_2^{64} \setminus \{\mathbf{0}_{64}\}$---across four parallel 128-bit slots per 512-bit flit phase in a single \StageOneSieveDelayNs\ logic level while maintaining $\eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{slot}} = 99.20\%$ vs.\ $48.90\%$ under CRC poisoning.

We establish six core results. First, a multiscale address decomposition ($\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$~\cite{chen2001multilevel}) combines $J(64,w)$ and $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ codes ($N^T N = 8I_{64}+J_{64}$~\cite{welch1974lower,kautz1964nonrandom}) with a dual-submode Cycle-0 Vieta polynomial-ideal and time-wheel/Steiner tensor sieve over $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ ($P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$ revocations, $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$ lease expiration, and $18,360$--$132,600$ cohorts). Second, spanning-forest permutation-orbit chord clamping with $<4\text{~ns}$ PHY-gated dual-forest failover $(T_0,T_1)$~\cite{tutte1961problem,nashwilliams1961edge}, $+0$-LUT fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} MOESI and 4-tier \texttt{S2M\_DRS} mantissa clamping ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits, $Q = \Omega((\Delta_u/\delta w)^2)$ keyed-dither bound), and the \texttt{spectralc} compiler~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying} translate DOE Genesis \texttt{SKILL.md} / ModCon manifests~\cite{doe2025genesis} into certified \texttt{BAR0} bundles. Third, renewal-reward and Martingale Network Calculus~\cite{poloczek2014martingale} bounds pair with a hitless per-slot dual-epoch grace window, deterministic \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} shootdown closure, a \ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs-LUT per-\texttt{ARID} ordered AXI4 read-scrub responder shim, dual-subflit split-coset tunneling ($8.0\text{~ns}$ recovery, 6 cohorts/flit), and 8-round Davies--Meyer user-space capability attenuation~\cite{daemen1995cipher,birgisson2014macaroons}. Fourth, constant-delay Stage-4 credit release ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$), Tropical $(\max,+) \otimes S_{64}$ pod federation, and 2-share DOM~\cite{gross2016dom} \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} glitch non-completeness are machine-checked across \LeanTheoremTotal\ zero-axiom Lean~4 theorems (\texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm29}: \LeanCoreLoadBearing\ core groups + \LeanStructuralLemmas\ structural lemmas)~\cite{demoura2021lean4} and inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} Miter UNSAT attestation.

On an AMD Virtex UltraScale+ HBM \texttt{xcvu47p-fsvh2892-2L-e} FPGA (Vivado~\VivadoToolVersion\ Post-Route Physical Silicon Sign-Off \& Dual-AFI AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} execution on \texttt{\AwsAgfiId} and 16~GB HBM2 DMA \texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId}), the 2-stage core occupies \CorePhysicalLUTs\ CLB LUTs (\CoreLUTUtilPct; +\ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs\ LUTs scrub shim; \WrapperEnclaveLUTs\ LUTs w/ 4-stage wrapper), \CoreTotalFFs\ FFs, \CoreBRAM\ BRAM, and \CoreDSP\ DSPs at \PostRouteSetupWNS\ WNS ($F_{\max} = \PostRouteFmaxMHz$, \PostRouteDRCViolations\ DRC errors, \PostRouteDynPowerMW\ dynamic power). In the open-source 7nm FinFET ASAP7 predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7}, the standalone core maps to \AsicCoreCells\ standard cells (\AsicCoreGe\ NAND2 GE, \AsicCoreAreaMm) and the placed-and-routed full 4-stage enclave closes at \AsicStandardCells\ standard cells (\AsicEnclaveAreaMm, \AsicSetupWnsPs\ WNS, \AsicStageOneTwoDelayPs\ critical path, \AsicFmaxMhz\ $F_{\max}$, \AsicTotalPowerMw\ total power). Across 10,000 racks ($160\text{~PB}$ dec / $156.25\text{~PiB}$ bin), eliminating the $1.5625\%$ table carve-out reclaims $2,500\text{~TB}$ ($2,560,000\text{~GiB}$), saving \$$37.50\text{M}$--\$$38.40\text{M}$ CapEx and $8.0$--$39.4\text{~MW}$.
\end{abstract}

\StartTwoCol

\section{Introduction}
Compute Express Link (\mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} (and projected \mbox{CXL~5.0})~\cite{cxl2025spec}) and Universal Chiplet Interconnect Express (\mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0}~\cite{ucie2025spec30}) disaggregate High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM2e/HBM3/HBM4) and DDR5 pools across multi-tenant compute nodes and 2.5D/3D chiplet packages~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol}. While CXL fabric switches and Dynamic Capacity Devices (DCD) manage coarse extent allocation over \texttt{CXL.io}, they lack hardware-enforced, sub-flit tenant access control at wire speed on \texttt{CXL.mem} and \texttt{CXL.cache}. Existing isolation architectures face four fundamental bottlenecks:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Capacity and tail-latency tax from stateful permission tables:} \textit{Space-Control}~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol} carves out $1.5625\%$ of pooled DRAM ($250\text{~GB}$/rack) for permission tables and stalls the memory pipeline by $32.0$--$68.0\text{~ns}$ on SRAM permission-cache misses ($+11.0\%$ to $+19.0\%$ throughput loss and $>180\text{~ns}$ P99.99 tail latency on bursty sparse MoE and vLLM PagedAttention KV-cache workloads), while CHERI / ARM Morello~\cite{watson2015cheri} incurs a $12.50\%$ DRAM metadata tax.\footnote{For static batch workloads whose working-set permissions fit entirely in on-die SRAM, miss penalties are modest; however, for sparse MoE, vLLM KV-cache sharing, and serverless microVM IPC, permission-cache thrashing dominates tail latency.}
  \item \textbf{Collateral damage from whole-flit CRC poisoning:} A 512-bit CXL/UCIe flit phase packs four parallel 128-bit protocol slots ($s_0, s_1, s_2, s_3$). Inverting the tail Flit CRC to abort one unauthorized slot destroys up to three co-packed innocent slots and triggers a Go-Back-N NAK replay storm.
  \item \textbf{Control-plane compromise, switch HDM permutations, cable-cut failover, and cyclic Confused-Deputy escalation:} Runtime policy writes over PCIe \texttt{BAR0} MMIO are vulnerable if the software Fabric Manager (FM) is compromised or if a host issues a warm PCIe Function-Level Reset (FLR), while cyclic peer-to-peer topologies ($\beta_1(G) = |E| - |V| + \beta_0 > 0$) with switch Host-Managed Device Memory (HDM) address-remapping permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$ allow deputized nodes to launder elevated permissions unless port hairpinning is blocked and cycle chords are clamped.
  \item \textbf{Protocol deadlocks on read/write completions, hard-HBM scrub routing, and coherency snoops:} Dropping an unauthorized \texttt{M2S\_Req} read, \texttt{M2S\_RwD} write, or returning \texttt{S2M\_DRS} completion hangs the host's Miss Status Holding Register (MSHR) until a Completion Timeout (CTO) triggers a fatal Machine Check (\texttt{\#MC}). Moreover, commercial FPGA HBM hard IP blocks ignore custom \texttt{aruser} bits, and blocking an \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp} snoop response, dropping a snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} writeback, or faulting a \texttt{CXL.cache D2H\_Req RdOwn} request deadlocks the Home Agent.
\end{enumerate}

\noindent\textbf{Bridging Zero Trust to Interconnect Layers 1--2:}
Prevailing Zero Trust Architectures (NIST SP~800-207~\cite{rose2020zta}) enforce micro-segmentation and continuous verification at OSI Layers~3--7, implicitly trusting physical board traces, CXL switch fabrics, and OS/hypervisor Fabric Managers once a host attaches to a shared memory pool. In disaggregated \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0}~\cite{cxl2025spec} and \mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0}~\cite{ucie2025spec30} fabrics---and in autonomous scientific computing platforms such as the U.S.\ Department of Energy (DOE) Genesis Mission and American Science Cloud (\texttt{AmSC})~\cite{doe2025genesis}---a rogue DMA engine, compromised Fabric Manager, or hallucinating AI agent tool call bypasses Layer~3--7 gateways entirely by issuing direct cacheline loads, stores, and coherent snoops at $250\text{~MHz}$--$1.0\text{~GHz}$. Enforcing true hardware Zero Trust at Interconnect Layers~1--2 requires an inline Policy Enforcement Point (PEP) on the memory-controller boundary that verifies every 128-bit sub-flit slot in $O(1)$ gate depth without trusting the flit wire, the host OS/FM, intermediate switches, downstream AI consumers, or the EDA synthesis toolchain~\cite{ncdsmo2020rain}.

\noindent\textbf{Architectural Goal \& Contributions:} Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline} illustrates \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} (\ReleaseVersion), a cut-through capability interposer that enforces bidirectional Layer-1/2 Zero-Trust sub-flit isolation in $O(1)$ gate depth using 0~BRAM and 0~B of DRAM permission tables. By sanitizing individual 128-bit protocol slots in place, the architecture guarantees $100\%$ survival of innocent co-packed slots, zero host MSHR/CTO hangs, zero physical HBM wordline perturbation on scrubbed reads, and zero self-inflicted link NAKs across six unified contributions:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\arabic*)]
  \item \textbf{Multiscale Address Splitting \& Cycle-0 Vieta / Time-Wheel / Tensor Sieve:} Multiscale $\mathrm{HPA}[51:6]$ splitting ($\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$~\cite{chen2001multilevel}) with orthogonal 64-bit coarse/fine masks (\texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x00/0x08} and dual-banked \texttt{0x60\_bank[0:1]/0x68}), $J(64,w)$ and $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ coding, and a dual-submode Cycle-0 Vieta $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ polynomial-ideal and autonomous 64-bucket time-wheel / Steiner tensor sieve (Section~\ref{sec:multiscale});
  \item \textbf{Spanning-Forest Chord Clamping, PHY Failover, Zero-Area \texttt{LUT6\_2} Precision Clamping, \& \texttt{SKILL.md} Certifying Compilation:} Spanning-forest propagation, $S_{64}$ coordinate-orbit chord clamping, PHY-gated dual-forest chord failover $(T_0,T_1)$, fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} \texttt{CXL.cache} MOESI and 4-tier \texttt{S2M\_DRS} mantissa clamps ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits) at $+0$ CLB LUTs with an adaptive $Q = \Omega((\Delta_u/\delta w)^2)$ keyed-dither query lower bound, and the \texttt{spectralc} certifying compiler translating DOE Genesis \texttt{SKILL.md} / ModCon manifests~\cite{doe2025genesis} via submodular $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ slope dispersion packing (Sections~\ref{sec:forest} and~\ref{sec:compiler});
  \item \textbf{Stochastic Queueing Bounds, Grace Window, \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} Horizon, \& Ordered Read-Scrub Shim:} Renewal-reward goodput, Martingale Network Calculus tail bounds~\cite{poloczek2014martingale}, a hitless per-slot dual-epoch grace window, deterministic \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} cache shootdown horizon with snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} coherency closure, and a Per-\texttt{ARID} ordered AXI4 read-scrub responder shim (Sections~\ref{sec:pipeline}--\ref{sec:power});
  \item \textbf{Split-Coset Tunneling, Hermitian AG Scaling, \& Davies--Meyer Capability Attenuation:} Tri-mode integrity with dual-subflit \texttt{LOpt} CRC48 + Tail CRC64 split-coset tunneling, Hermitian $\mathcal{H}_q$ Hasse--Weil bus-widening analysis, a 1-\texttt{LUT4} coprime $\chi_{33}\times\chi_{31}\circ\mathcal{L}_5$ Carter--Wegman MAC mixer~\cite{carter1979universal,daemen1995cipher}, and 8-round Davies--Meyer feed-forward user-space capability attenuation~\cite{birgisson2014macaroons} (Sections~\ref{sec:trimode} and~\ref{sec:formal});
  \item \textbf{Silicon Sign-Off Harness, Tropical Pod Federation, \& Inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} Attestation:} 16nm FPGA (\texttt{xcvu47p} OOC and live AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} Dual-AFI silicon), placed-and-routed open-source 7nm ASAP7 predictive PDK standard-cell CMOS sign-off~\cite{clark2016asap7}, CNCF \texttt{k3s} Kubernetes Admission Webhook HIL, Tropical $(\max,+) \otimes S_{64}$ multi-pod federation, WDDL vs.\ cut-through perimeter dichotomy, and Two-Zone DFARS CNF/LRAT \texttt{ZK-IC3} sequential netlist equivalence (Sections~\ref{sec:fpga} and~\ref{sec:traceability}); and
  \item \textbf{Pure Term-Mode Lean~4 Formal Verification:} Zero-axiom term-mode Lean~4 proofs of all \LeanTheoremTotal\ numbered theorems \texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm29} (\LeanCoreLoadBearing\ core groups + \LeanStructuralLemmas\ structural lemmas, Section~\ref{sec:formal}).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Five-Oracle Verification Funnel and Two-Zone DFARS \texorpdfstring{\texttt{ZK-LEC} / \texttt{ZK-IC3}}{ZK-LEC / ZK-IC3} Attestation}
\label{sec:oracles}
All claims and silicon artifacts are cross-gated by five deterministic oracles (Table~\ref{tab:provenance}):
(1)~\textbf{Oracle~1 (Lean~4 Kernel~\cite{demoura2021lean4}, contrasted with Coq RTL frameworks~\cite{choi2017kami}):} While prior dependent-type hardware verification frameworks such as MIT Kami~\cite{choi2017kami} verify modular Bluespec-style RTL rules in Coq, Oracle~1 machine-checks all \LeanTheoremTotal\ numbered finite-state theorems (\texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm29}: \LeanCoreLoadBearing\ core groups + \LeanStructuralLemmas\ structural lemmas) across \id{formal_lean4/CxlSubflitSanitizerFormal.lean} and \id{formal_lean4/CxlSubflitSanitizerExtensions.lean} in pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 term-mode (\texttt{0 sorry}, \texttt{0 axioms}), while continuous/asymptotic expectations (Theorem~\ref{thm:johnson}(iii), Theorem~\ref{thm:dither_lower_bound}), graph cycle rank $\beta_1 = |E|-|V|+\beta_0$ and submodular packing (Theorems~\ref{thm:forest} and~\ref{thm:submodular_modcon}), renewal-reward/Martingale bounds (Theorems~\ref{thm:goodput} and~\ref{thm:martingale}), BCH/Hermitian distance (Lemma~\ref{lem:bch}, Proposition~\ref{prop:hermitian_ag}), and Schur-complement power (Theorem~\ref{thm:power}) are proved analytically and verified in Oracle~2;
(2)~\textbf{Oracle~2 (Trace \& Martingale Simulator):} Evaluates GAPBS, vLLM KV-cache, DeepSeek/Mixtral MoE, and 100,000-op \texttt{mmap BAR0} loopback traces (\id{sim/benchmark_workload_and_martingale.py});
(3)~\textbf{Oracle~3 (ANSI-C11 \texttt{BAR0} ABI \& TRL-8 Kubernetes HIL):} Verifies prototype PCI-SIG DVSEC header (\texttt{Vendor 0x1E98} simulation ID, experimental \texttt{DVSEC 0x0023}, \texttt{Device 0x4B4C}; configurable to licensee PCI-SIG Vendor ID), all 16 64-bit CSRs (\texttt{0x00}--\texttt{0x78} = $128\text{~B}$: coarse spatial/temporal \texttt{0x00/0x08}, shadow key portals \texttt{0x10/0x18}, epoch/status \texttt{0x20}, credit counters \texttt{0x28/0x30}, telemetry/trap/FIFO/egress \texttt{0x38}--\texttt{0x58}, dual-banked fine cohort/Vieta \texttt{0x60\_bank[0:1]} and 64-bucket time-wheel/temporal mask \texttt{0x68}, 64-tenant live SPID bitmap \texttt{0x70}, and prefix/submode/DMA configuration \texttt{0x78}), and the CNCF \texttt{k3s} \texttt{ValidatingAdmissionWebhook} (\id{TRL8_K8S_OPERATOR_HIL_RECEIPT.json});
(4)~\textbf{Oracle~4 (5-Pillar Auditor \& Two-Tier \texttt{ZK-LEC} / \texttt{ZK-IC3}):} Enforces the Two-Zone DFARS 252.227-7018 / DEAR 952.227-14 air-gap (retaining all \texttt{*.sv}, \texttt{*.edf}, and \texttt{*.dcp} files in Zone~1 \id{01_Private_DFARS_Vault} with 0~\texttt{.sv} files in Zone~2 \id{02_Public_GitHub_Repo}) and verifies the two-tier receipt \id{ZK_LEC_MITER_RECEIPT.json} anchored to $h_{\mathrm{netlist}} = \mathrm{SHA\text{-}256}(\mathrm{EDIF}_{\mathrm{vault}})$: Tier~4a seals in \ReleaseVersion\ exhaustive per-cone CNF/LRAT Miter UNSAT ($\mathcal{M}_k(x) = [\mathrm{EDIF}_{\mathrm{vault},k}(x) \neq \mathrm{Spec}_{\mathrm{Lean4},k}(x)] \equiv 0$ across $100\%$ of $2^{w_k}$ combinational cone states ($w_k \le 24$)), 1-step inductive sequential invariance (\texttt{Init}, \texttt{Consecution}, \texttt{Miter Safety}), and 65,536-cycle sequential FIFO/rollover trace equivalence, while Tier~4b formulates the commit-and-prove VOLE-ZK / STARK relation $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{ZK\text{-}IC3}}$ for external cryptographic wrapping (Theorem~\ref{thm:zk_ic3}, Section~\ref{sec:traceability}); and
(5)~\textbf{Oracle~5 (Vivado~\VivadoToolVersion, Dual-AFI AWS F2 Silicon, \& Open-Source 7nm ASAP7 Predictive PDK Sign-Off):}
Attests post-route \texttt{xcvu47p} silicon closure (\id{vivado_xcvu47p_core_timing_summary.rpt}), placed-and-routed open-source 7nm FinFET ASAP7 predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7} standard-cell CMOS sign-off (\id{TRL6_ASIC_7NM_ASAP7_SIGNOFF.json}), and live silicon execution inside an account-locked private AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} enclave (public \texttt{loadPermission} disabled) across both Baseline BIST/TVLA AFI \texttt{\AwsAgfiId} (\AwsBistSlots{} sub-flit slots, 19,990-trace ISO/IEC~17825 Welch TVLA $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$, \id{STAGE2_AWS_F2_SILICON_RECEIPT.json}) and Production 16~GB HBM2 DMA AFI \texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId} (\AwsLiveBursts{} 512-bit bursts / \AwsLiveSlots{} inline sub-flit slots with $100\%$ HBM2 sentinel survival \texttt{0xAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA}, \id{TRL7_AWS_F2_HBM2_DMA_RECEIPT.json}).

\subsection{Asymptotic Time--Space--Circuit Complexity and Physical Silicon Summary}
\label{sec:complexity_silicon}
A foundational question in computer architecture is whether enforcing fine-grained, page-level memory isolation across a disaggregated memory pool of capacity $M$ bytes fundamentally requires $\Theta(M)$ bits of lookup state and $\Omega(\log M)$ circuit depth on the critical memory-access path. Table~\ref{tab:complexity} contrasts the asymptotic time, space, Boolean circuit-depth, and communication complexity of \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} against stateful DRAM permission-table firewalls (\textit{Space-Control}~\cite{goswami2026spacecontrol}), tagged-memory capability architectures (\textit{CHERI / ARM Morello}~\cite{watson2015cheri}), and stateless tail-CRC poisoners.

By encoding 4-KB page cohorts into constant-weight Johnson $J(64,w)$ codewords, affine-plane $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$ lines, and $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ Vieta annihilator polynomials evaluated against a 128-byte \texttt{BAR0} register file, \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} reduces datapath lookup storage from $\Theta(M)$ to $\Theta(1)$ ($0\text{~bits}$ of BRAM/SRAM and $0\text{~B}$ of DRAM) and collapses access verification from variable-latency cache/tree walks to a constant-depth $\mathrm{AC}^0$ Boolean circuit (a single \StageOneSieveDelayNs\ \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} logic level in Stage~1 followed by a \StageTwoCriticalPathNs\ \texttt{LUT2}/\mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} clamp in Stage~2). Revoking a tenant or 4-KB cohort executes in $O(1)$ time ($1$ \texttt{BAR0} MMIO cycle $= 4.0\text{~ns}$, or $0$ MMIO cycles via autonomous 64-bucket time-wheel expiration), while surgical per-slot \texttt{CXL\_NOP} replacement preserves work-conserving $\eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{slot}} = 1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}}$ goodput ($99.20\%$ vs.\ $48.90\%$ under tail-CRC poisoning at $p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.8\%$). On the read completion path, Stage~2's $+0$-LUT fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} 4-tier mantissa clamp paired with counter-seeded Cycle-0 dither inflates adaptive foundation-model weight-extraction query complexity from $O(\log(\Delta_u/\delta w))$ ($Q_{\mathrm{det}} = 6$) to $\Omega((\Delta_u/\delta w)^2)$ ($Q \ge 192$--$512$). Offline, the \texttt{spectralc} compiler solves submodular slope packing and emits certificates verified fail-closed in $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ time ($O(|V| + |E|)$ linear graph time for fixed $|\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}| \le 72$).

In physical silicon (detailed in Section~\ref{sec:fpga} and Section~\ref{sec:empirical_synthesis}), the 2-stage sanitizer core closes post-route on 16nm AMD Virtex UltraScale+ \texttt{xcvu47p} at \CorePhysicalLUTs\ CLB LUTs (\CoreLUTUtilPct), \CoreTotalFFs\ FFs, \CoreBRAM\ BRAM, and \PostRouteSetupWNS\ WNS ($F_{\max} = \PostRouteFmaxMHz$, \PostRouteDynPowerMW\ dynamic power), verified on live AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} silicon across \AwsBistSlots{} BIST slots (\texttt{\AwsAgfiId}, 19,990-trace Welch TVLA $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$) and \AwsLiveBursts{} 16~GB HBM2 DMA bursts (\texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId}, $100\%$ sentinel survival). In the open-source 7nm FinFET ASAP7 predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7}, the standalone core maps to \AsicCoreCells\ standard cells (\AsicCoreGe\ NAND2 GE, \AsicCoreAreaMm) and the placed-and-routed 4-stage enclave closes at \AsicStandardCells\ standard cells (\AsicEnclaveAreaMm, \AsicStageOneTwoDelayPs\ critical path, \AsicFmaxMhz\ $F_{\max}$, \AsicTotalPowerMw\ total power).

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Standing on Classical Foundations (The 60-Year Mathematical Debt).}\;Very little of the algebraic machinery in this monograph had to be invented from scratch: four parallel 128-bit CXL slots can be sanitized in \CorePhysicalLUTs\ CLB LUTs and 0~BRAM only because Kautz and Singleton (1964)~\cite{kautz1964nonrandom}, Welch and Sidelnikov (1971/1974)~\cite{welch1974lower}, Tutte and Nash-Williams (1961)~\cite{tutte1961problem,nashwilliams1961edge}, Carter and Wegman (1979)~\cite{carter1979universal}, Goguen and Meseguer (1982)~\cite{goguen1982security}, and Daemen (1995)~\cite{daemen1995cipher} established the underlying combinatorial, graph-theoretic, and cellular-automaton invariants decades ago. Our contribution is simply observing that those classical structures map isomorphically onto the dual \texttt{O5}/\texttt{O6} outputs of a physical 6-input FPGA lookup table and a 64-bit $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ shift register at CXL wire speed.

(Architectural Consequence).}\; For five decades, memory-protection architectures have obeyed a linear scaling law: protecting $M$ bytes of physical memory at page or cacheline granularity required $\Theta(M)$ bits of permission tables in DRAM and an on-die SRAM translation cache that stalls the datapath by $32$--$68\text{~ns}$ whenever bursty AI or graph workloads thrash the cache. \section{Background, Wire Extraction, and Zero-Trust Threat Model}

\subsection{CXL~3.0--4.0 and UCIe 512-Bit Flit Phases and Disjoint 128-Bit Slot Wire Extraction}
\label{sec:wire}
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\;Prevailing Zero Trust architectures enforce access control at OSI Layers~3--7, implicitly trusting physical board traces and switch fabrics once a host attaches to a shared memory pool. On a 512-bit CXL.mem or CXL.cache bus operating at $250\text{~MHz}$--$1.0\text{~GHz}$, however, four independent 128-bit protocol slots share every clock phase, and a rogue DMA engine or compromised Fabric Manager bypasses software gateways entirely. Enforcing Zero Trust at Interconnect Layers~1--2 requires extracting and verifying each 128-bit slot on the wire without stalling innocent co-packed neighbors or deadlocking host coherency engines.\par\smallskip

In \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} (and projected \mbox{CXL~5.0}, 64--128~GT/s PAM-4~\cite{cxl2025spec}), \mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0} FDI (up to 64~GT/s die-to-die raw/streaming flit framing and fast emergency sideband failover~\cite{ucie2025spec30}), and 64B-flit scale-up fabrics, internal datapaths process 512-bit (64-byte) flit phases (\texttt{s\_flit\_payload\_i[511:0]}) indexed by \texttt{flit\_phase\_id\_i[1:0]} $\in \{0,1,2,3\}$ at 250~MHz--1.0~GHz. Each 512-bit phase partitions into four 128-bit slots $s_0, s_1, s_2, s_3 \in \mathbb{F}_2^{128}$. In \texttt{CXL.mem}, master-to-subordinate requests use \texttt{M2S\_Req}, \texttt{M2S\_RwD}, and \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp}, while subordinate-to-master completions use \texttt{S2M\_NDR}, \texttt{S2M\_DRS}, and \texttt{S2M\_BISnp}. In \texttt{CXL.cache}, device-to-host coherent requests use \texttt{D2H\_Req} (\texttt{RdShared}, \texttt{RdOwn}, \texttt{RdAny}). Phase~0, $\mathrm{Slot}_0[15:0]$ carries the 16-bit Link-Layer Flit Header ($\mathrm{Slot}_0[3:0]$ credits and $\mathrm{Slot}_0[15:4]$ \texttt{H0..H5} slot formats). Decoupling $\mathrm{SPID}_i[13:0]$ onto \texttt{s\_slot\_spid\_i[55:0]} and per-slot epoch bank selector $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$ yields three strictly disjoint wire formats:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Mode A --- Native CPI/FDI Normalized Slot Layout:} On internal CPI/FDI interfaces, the upstream range/MAC decoder and Cycle-0 Vieta evaluator drive the four authenticated 64-bit capability vectors $r_0,\ldots,r_3 \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ onto \texttt{s\_slot\_cap\_vec\_i[255:0]} and latch the combined coarse/Vieta non-root qualifier onto \texttt{slot\_coarse\_arena\_ok\_i[3:0]}. With bit offset $\delta_0 = 16$ on Phase-0 $\mathrm{Slot}_0$ and $\delta_i = 0$ otherwise ($16 + 80 = 96 \le 128$ bits): (a)~$\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+5:\delta_i]$ (6b) carries channel/opcode/MOESI bits, where \texttt{CXL.cache D2H\_Req} bits $[\delta_i+1:\delta_i]$ encode $x_i[1:0] = \ell_{\mathrm{req}} \in \{\mathrm{I}=\texttt{2'b00}, \mathrm{S}=\texttt{2'b01}, \mathrm{E}=\texttt{2'b10}, \mathrm{M}=\texttt{2'b11}\}$ clamped in Stage~2 to $\ell_{\mathrm{eff}} = \min(x_i, c_i)$ against slot $i$'s 2-bit tier ceiling $c_i[1:0]$; (b)~$\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+21:\delta_i+6]$ (16b) carries the preserved transaction \texttt{Tag\_ID[15:0]}; (c)~$\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+67:\delta_i+22] = \mathrm{HPA}_i[51:6]$ (46b) splits into the 23-bit coarse aperture $\mathrm{HPA}_i[51:29]$, 17-bit fine 4-KB page $\mathrm{HPA}_i[28:12]$, and 6-bit cacheline offset $\mathrm{HPA}_i[11:6]$; and (d)~$\mathrm{Slot}_i[\delta_i+79:\delta_i+68]$ (12b) carries \texttt{LD\_ID\_i[5:0]} and \texttt{Meta\_Tag\_i[5:0]} (encoding lease-expiration bucket $\tau_i = \texttt{Meta\_Tag}_i[5:0] \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$ and epoch selector $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$).
  \item \textbf{Mode B --- Enclave-to-Enclave Paired-MAC (Mode B.1) \& Zero-Slot-Tax Split-Coset Tunneling (Mode B.2):} Mode~B.1 (for non-IDE links) packs $r_i[63:0]$ into $\mathrm{Slot}_i[127:64]$ and $\{\mathrm{LD\_ID}_i[5:0], \mathrm{HPA}_i[51:6], \mathrm{Tag\_ID}_i[7:0], \mathrm{Opcode}_i[3:0]\}$ into $\mathrm{Slot}_i[63:0]$ with a paired 64-bit MAC slot ($<2^{-63}$ forgery). Mode~B.2 (for 96-bit CXL IDE AES-GCM links) imposes $0\%$ slot-capacity tax: when $\texttt{LOpt\_Mode\_En} = 0$, four 8-bit cohort descriptors ($\mathrm{cid}_3 \parallel \cdots \parallel \mathrm{cid}_0$) are encrypted via $\mathrm{Enc32}^{(6)}$ into the $[8,4,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ Tail CRC64 coset (Bytes~242--249, Theorem~\ref{thm:modeb}); when $\texttt{LOpt\_Mode\_En} = 1$, a concatenated $[6,2,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ coset in Phase-1 Sub-Flit~0 CRC48 (Bytes~122--127) carries $(\mathrm{cid}_1, \mathrm{cid}_0)$ alongside four descriptors in Tail CRC64, halving cut-through latency to 2~phases ($8.0\text{~ns}$) and expanding capacity by $+50\%$ to 6~\texttt{cid}s/flit (Theorem~\ref{thm:split_coset}).
  \item \textbf{Mode C --- Pre-Core Zero-Sideband Standard \texttt{CXL.mem} Layout:} For unmodified commercial CXL hosts, the Cycle-0 pre-core unsealer (\PreCoreModeCLUTs~CLB LUTs, \CycleZeroFfs~FFs) decrypts the even-width page slice $2n \in \{24,34,36,40\}$ via $\mathrm{Dec}_{K_{\mathrm{seal}}, T_i}^{(6)}$ (binding $\mathrm{SPID}_i$ into $T_i$), evaluates $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_i)$, the 1-hot time-wheel vector $\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i$, or the Vieta non-root check $(\mathrm{cid}_i[7:0] \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}) \wedge (\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_i[7:0]) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}})$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:vieta}), strips the upper $(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)$ bits $\mathrm{HPA}_i[12+2n-1 : 12+2n-(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ (e.g., $\mathrm{HPA}_i[45:32] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ for $2n=34, c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g=14$) prior to Stage~1, and qualifies automatic 1-cycle $\texttt{CSR\_0x70}[\mathrm{SPID}_i[5:0]] \leftarrow 0$ lockout strictly on architectural \texttt{M2S\_RwD} write guard failures.
  \item \textbf{Per-\texttt{ARID} Ordered Soft-Logic AXI4 Read-Scrub Responder Shim:} Placed immediately upstream of the \texttt{xcvu47p} HBM hard IP slave port (\ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs~CLB LUTs, \ScrubShimFFs~FFs, 0~BRAM), the shim intercepts $\texttt{m\_axi\_aruser\_scrub\_o} = \texttt{1'b1}$ on $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{scrub}} = \mathbf{0}_{46}$, forces $\texttt{m\_hbm\_axi\_arvalid\_o} = \texttt{1'b0}$ (zero physical HBM bank-queue or Bank~0 Row~0 \texttt{ACT}/\texttt{PRE} activity), checks a 16-thread 2-bit per-\texttt{ARID} counter (\id{arid_hbm_inflight_cnt_q[0:15]}) to prevent same-\texttt{ARID} completion reordering, and returns synthetic zeroed completions (\texttt{512'h0}, with \texttt{RRESP = OKAY} in Stealth Mode or CXL \texttt{Poison} in Architectural Fault Mode).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Zero-Trust Threat Model and Five-Boundary Physical Enforcement (NIST SP 800-207, SP 800-193 \& NSA RAIN)}
\label{sec:zta_threat}
Following NIST SP~800-207 (Zero Trust Architecture~\cite{rose2020zta}), NIST SP~800-193 (Platform Firmware Resiliency~\cite{regenscheid2018pfr}), and the NSA National Cross Domain Strategy and Management Office (NCDSMO) Raise-the-Bar \texttt{RAIN} design pattern (\textit{Redundant, Always-Invoked, Independent, Non-Bypassable}~\cite{ncdsmo2020rain}), the interposer resides on the trusted memory-controller/HBM base die with root keys provisioned at cold power-on reset ($\texttt{por\_cold\_rst\_n} = 0$) or via an on-die hardware Root-of-Trust pulse ($\texttt{rot\_epoch\_rekey\_rst\_n} = 0$). Table~\ref{tab:zta_crosswalk} maps the seven Federal/DoD Zero Trust pillars directly to hardware registers, normative SystemVerilog Assertions (\texttt{SVA 1}--\texttt{SVA 10}, Appendix~\ref{app:sva}), and machine-checked Lean~4 proofs across five untrusted boundaries:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Boundary 1 --- Untrusted Flit Wire \& Co-Packed Neighbor Slots:} An adversary may inject unauthorized \texttt{M2S\_RwD} writes, \texttt{M2S\_Req} reads, null capability vectors $r_i = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, modulo-64 aliased $\mathrm{SPID}' = \mathrm{SPID} + 64m$, or replay out-of-order (OOO) DMA cacheline beats co-packed inside the same 512-bit flit phase as three innocent tenant slots. Stage~1--2 enforces surgical per-slot \texttt{CXL\_NOP} / zero replacement, 8-bit port-prefix + 64-tenant live SPID filtering (\texttt{CSR 0x70/0x78}), and 4-channel 64-cacheline OOO DMA replay annihilation (\texttt{thm01}, \texttt{thm06}, \texttt{thm14}).
  \item \textbf{Boundary 2 --- Untrusted Host OS, Hypervisor \& Compromised Fabric Manager:} After initial cold-POR boot, the host OS, hypervisor, and software Fabric Manager (FM) are treated as untrusted. A compromised FM may issue arbitrary PCIe \texttt{BAR0} MMIO writes attempting $0 \rightarrow 1$ privilege re-enablement, flood intra-slope revocations, or trigger a warm PCIe Function-Level Reset ($\texttt{host\_warm\_flr\_rst\_n} = 0$). Hardware isolates all security CSRs from $\texttt{host\_warm\_flr\_rst\_n}$, enforces the unconditional cold-POR bitwise \texttt{AND}-ratchet $M^{(t+1)} = M^{(t)} \wedge w$ (\texttt{thm02}) and Vieta polynomial-ideal root permanence $\mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t)}) \subseteq \mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t+1)})$ (\texttt{thm20}), expires ephemeral leases autonomously via the 64-bucket time-wheel (\texttt{CSR 0x68}, \texttt{thm26}), switches failover forests strictly via hardware PCS / UCIe~3.0 emergency sideband loss-of-signal $p_{\mathrm{phy}} = \texttt{pcs\_link\_up\_i}$~\cite{ucie2025spec30} (\texttt{thm28}), and traps any $0 \rightarrow 1$ tamper attempt in \texttt{CSR 0x48} (\texttt{SVA 1}).
  \item \textbf{Boundary 3 --- Untrusted Multi-Hop CXL Switches \& Federated Pods:} Intermediate switches may form cyclic routing topologies ($\beta_1(G) > 0$), apply arbitrary HDM bit-coordinate permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$, or observe reverse link-credit timing. Hardware enforces port anti-hairpinning, $S_{64}$ coordinate-orbit chord clamping $\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ (\texttt{thm21}), zero-topology-disclosure pod transfer matrices (\texttt{thm12}, Theorem~\ref{thm:tropical_pod}), and constant-delay Stage-4 credit release $\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$ (\texttt{thm08}).
  \item \textbf{Boundary 4 --- Untrusted AI Consumers \& Shared-Weight Inference Partners:} A co-tenant GPU or inference partner may issue \texttt{CXL.cache D2H\_Req RdOwn} requests to corrupt shared weights, issue adaptive \texttt{S2M\_DRS} reads to extract proprietary foundation-model weights, or observe HBM Bank~0 row-buffer contention and multi-query derivation digests. Hardware downgrades $\mathrm{RdOwn} \mapsto \mathrm{RdShared}$ in 1~\mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} (\texttt{thm22}), clamps \texttt{S2M\_DRS} mantissas across 4 precision tiers ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits, \texttt{thm27}, Theorem~\ref{thm:dither_lower_bound}), suppresses $\texttt{m\_hbm\_axi\_arvalid\_o} = 0$ on scrubbed reads via the \ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs-LUT Per-\texttt{ARID} responder shim (\texttt{SVA 9}), and bounds canonical egress leakage in \texttt{CSR 0x58} to $\le \log_2(M_{\mathrm{qry}}+1)$ bits (\texttt{thm04}).
  \item \textbf{Boundary 5 --- Untrusted Compiler, EDA Synthesizer \& Air-Gapped Netlist Boundary:} Neither the offline NP-hard graph/hypergraph heuristics inside \texttt{spectralc} nor the commercial FPGA/ASIC synthesis tool is trusted. Control-plane policy bundles carry detached post-quantum \textbf{NSA CNSA~2.0 / NIST FIPS~204 \texttt{ML-DSA-87}}~\cite{fips204mldsa} signatures verified prior to staging; \texttt{spectralc} emits certificates checked fail-closed in $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ time (\texttt{thm18}, Theorem~\ref{thm:submodular_modcon}); post-route \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}/\texttt{LUT2} configuration SRAM frames are bi-simulated in Lean~4 (\texttt{thm05}, \texttt{thm22}, \texttt{thm27}); and the Zone-1 post-route EDIF netlist is proved equivalent to the public Lean~4 specification across all $t \in \mathbb{N}$ via inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} Miter UNSAT (Theorem~\ref{thm:zk_ic3}).
\end{enumerate}

\noindent\textbf{Unconditional/Quantum-Immune vs.\ Computational \& Physical Preconditions:}
We strictly separate unconditional guarantees from cryptographic and physical assumptions: (a)~Stage-1/2 Boolean subspace monotonicity (\texttt{thm01}--\texttt{thm03}), Vieta polynomial-ideal root permanence (\texttt{thm20}), $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ disjunct non-aliasing (\texttt{thm17}), $S_{64}$ orbit chord clamping (\texttt{thm21}), Stage-4 credit non-interference (\texttt{thm08}), Goguen--Meseguer unwinding (\texttt{thm19}), single-read deterministic \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} mantissa zeroing ($I \le u(c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i)$, \texttt{thm27}, Theorem~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi}(iii)), and intra-page Carter--Wegman MAC injectivity ($P_{\mathrm{coll}} \equiv 0$ when $x_{\mathrm{hi},k} = x_{\mathrm{hi},k'}$, Theorem~\ref{thm:mac}(ii)) hold \textbf{unconditionally and information-theoretically} against unbounded quantum adversaries; (b)~Mode~B.2 split-coset link confidentiality relies on the underlying CXL IDE 96-bit AES-256-GCM link layer, user-space capability attenuation (\texttt{thm29}) and the Cycle-0 keyed-dither stream $\eta^{(q)}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:dither_lower_bound}) rely on the 8-round $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}^{(8)}$ Davies--Meyer PRF and 128-bit $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$ Carter--Wegman MAC ($<2^{-63}$ inter-page forgery, Theorem~\ref{thm:mac}), and detached control-plane bundle authentication relies on NIST FIPS~204 \texttt{ML-DSA-87}~\cite{fips204mldsa}; and (c)~while 2-share DOM \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} masking (\texttt{thm24}) and complementary 1-of-2 fault trees (\texttt{thm16}) provide provable digital glitch non-completeness, physical differential power analysis (\texttt{TVLA}) and laser fault injection immunity depend on post-route routing capacitance symmetry ($C_{\mathrm{true}} \approx C_{\mathrm{comp}}$) and physical \texttt{HD.ISOLATED} SLR fence moats (Proposition~\ref{prop:wddl_dichotomy}).

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch~---~Layer-1/2 Access Control vs.\ Payload Confidentiality \& Cold-POR Root Trust).}\;\textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} is an inline Layer-1/2 spatial and temporal capability sanitizer, not a replacement for link-layer bulk encryption (CXL IDE 96-bit AES-256-GCM) or CPU confidential-compute memory encryption. Moreover, because the host OS and software Fabric Manager are treated as untrusted after boot, the unconditional monotonicity of the \texttt{BAR0} AND-ratchets (\texttt{CSR 0x00..0x70}) and Vieta ideal (\texttt{CSR 0x60}) rests on a strict physical precondition: initial epoch seeds and port prefixes must be provisioned during a trusted cold power-on-reset (\texttt{por\_cold\_rst\_n = 0}) or hardware Root-of-Trust pulse (\texttt{rot\_epoch\_rekey\_rst\_n = 0}) prior to untrusted host execution.

\StopTwoCol
\section{Multiscale Address Splitting, Full 64-Bit Johnson-Scheme Cohort Coding, and Dual-Submode Steiner / Cycle-0 Vieta Polynomial-Ideal Ratcheting}
\label{sec:multiscale}

\subsection{Boolean Subspace Monotonicity and Policy Refinement}
\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Conventional firewalls check permissions using arithmetic range comparators ($A_{\min} \le \mathrm{HPA} \le A_{\max}$), which require deep carry-propagation chains and cannot simultaneously output both the surviving capability and the exact unauthorized excess in a single lookup-table delay. By lifting capabilities into the Boolean ring $(\mathbb{F}_2^{64}, \oplus, \wedge)$, every incoming capability vector $s$ splits orthogonally into its authorized projection $\Pi_P(s) = s \wedge P$ and its violation residual $\Delta_P(s) = s \wedge \neg P$, mapped directly onto the dual \texttt{O5} and \texttt{O6} outputs of a single physical \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitive.

\begin{proposition}[Boolean Subspace Monotonicity and Coarse/Fine Policy Refinement]
\label{prop:monotonic}
Let $(\mathbb{F}_2^{64}, \le, \wedge, \vee)$ carry the bitwise order $a \le b \iff a \wedge \neg b = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, with pass-projection $\Pi_P(s) := s \wedge P$ and violation operator $\Delta_P(s) := s \wedge \neg P$. For capability masks $P_{\mathrm{lo}} \le P_{\mathrm{hi}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ ($P_{\mathrm{lo}} \wedge P_{\mathrm{hi}} = P_{\mathrm{lo}}$) and any incoming capability vector $s \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$, $\mathrm{im}(\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{lo}}}) \subseteq \mathrm{im}(\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{hi}}})$ and:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:monotonic}
\bigl(s \wedge (P_{\mathrm{lo}} \wedge P_{\mathrm{hi}})\bigr) \wedge \neg P_{\mathrm{lo}} = \mathbf{0}_{64},\qquad
s = \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{lo}}}(s) \oplus \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{lo}}}(s).
\end{equation}
If $P_{\mathrm{eff}} = p_s \wedge p_t \le q$, then: (i)~$\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(s) \wedge \neg q = \mathbf{0}_{64}$; (ii)~in coarse mode, $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(s) = \mathbf{0}_{64} \implies s \le q$; and (iii)~under the fine-cohort survival rule $\gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(P) := \{c \in \mathcal{C} : \Pi_P(c) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}\}$, $P_{\mathrm{lo}} \le P_{\mathrm{hi}} \implies \gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(P_{\mathrm{lo}}) \subseteq \gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(P_{\mathrm{hi}}) \subseteq \gamma_{\mathrm{fine}}(q)$.
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:monotonic}]
In $(\mathbb{F}_2, \oplus, \wedge)$, $x \wedge \neg x = 0$ and $(s_j \wedge P_j) \oplus (s_j \wedge \neg P_j) = s_j \wedge (P_j \oplus \neg P_j) = s_j$ in each bit $j \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$. Transitivity of $\le$ gives $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(s) \le P_{\mathrm{eff}} \le q$ and $c \wedge P_{\mathrm{lo}} \le c \wedge P_{\mathrm{hi}} \le c \wedge q$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm04}).
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Two-Level Coarse-Fine Address Splitting (\texorpdfstring{$\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$}{X\_(n+1) = X\_n + W\_n}) and Full 64-Bit Johnson-Scheme \texorpdfstring{$J(64,w)$}{J(64,w)} Cohort Coding}
Adapting Chen--Micchelli--Xu subspace splitting~\cite{chen2001multilevel}, we partition $\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \{\mathrm{HPA}[51:12]\} \cong \mathbb{F}_2^{40}$ via $\mathcal{P}_n(\mathrm{HPA}) = \mathrm{HPA}[51:29]$ and $\mathcal{Q}_n(\mathrm{HPA}) = \mathrm{HPA}[28:12]$ into the direct sum $\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$ of a 23-bit coarse aperture subspace $\mathcal{X}_n$ ($512\text{~MB}$ arenas governed by $P_{\mathrm{coarse}} = p_{s,\mathrm{coarse}} \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{coarse}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ in \texttt{CSR 0x00/0x08}) and a 17-bit fine 4-KB page detail subspace $\mathcal{W}_n$ (governed by dual-banked $P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b]} = p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[b]} \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{fine}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ in \texttt{CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]/0x68}). The 2-bit submode field $\texttt{CSR~0x78}[51:50]$ selects among all four Cycle-0/Stage-1 fine-cohort operating modes: $\texttt{2'b00}$ selects Boolean $J(64,w)$ / $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}$ cohort mode; $\texttt{2'b01}$ selects Vieta Submode~V.1 (Autonomous 64-Bucket Time-Wheel + Vieta Quarantine); $\texttt{2'b10}$ selects Coarse-Only Vieta Annihilator mode ($p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]} = p_{t,\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{1}_{64}, r_i = \mathbf{1}_{64}$, gating fine cohorts solely via Cycle-0 $(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}) \wedge (\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i}) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}})$); and $\texttt{2'b11}$ selects Vieta Submode~V.2 (Steiner/MDS $\otimes$ Vieta Tensor-Product Sieve). In Boolean cohort mode ($\texttt{CSR~0x78}[51:50] = \texttt{2'b00}$), each 4-KB cohort is assigned a constant-weight codeword $c \in J(64,w)$~\cite{macwilliams1977theory} checked via $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]}}(c) == \mathbf{0}_{64}$ on the \texttt{LUT6\_2 O5} rail gated by $\texttt{coarse\_ok}_i$, with software-adaptive weight $w^*(R) = \mathrm{round}((64 \ln 2)/R)$.

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; A naive 64-bit 1-hot bitmap can track at most 64 pages before running out of bits, while a Bloom filter suffers from immediate false-positive aliasing on the very first revocation ($R=1$). By assigning each 4-KB cohort a constant-weight codeword $c \in J(64,w)$ whose pairwise bit overlap is bounded by $\lambda$, an innocent cohort $c'$ loses at most $R\lambda$ of its $w$ ones when $R$ cohorts are revoked. As long as $R\lambda < w$, at least one bit of $c'$ is guaranteed to survive in $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}}(c') \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$, yielding strictly zero false-positive revocations ($P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$) in 0~BRAM.

\begin{theorem}[Multiscale Coarse-Fine Splitting and Full 64-Bit Johnson-Scheme $J(64,w)$ Disjunct Cohort Isolation]
\label{thm:johnson}
\label{thm:multiscale}
Let $\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$ be governed by orthogonal 64-bit masks $P_{\mathrm{coarse}}, P_{\mathrm{fine}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$, where fine 4-KB cohorts are assigned codewords $c \in \mathcal{C} \subseteq J(64,w)$. When $R$ distinct cohorts $c^{(1)},\ldots,c^{(R)} \in \mathcal{C}$ are revoked by setting $P_{\mathrm{fine}} = \neg r_{\mathrm{revoked}}$ with $r_{\mathrm{revoked}} = \bigvee_{m=1}^R c^{(m)}$ of union weight $u(R) := \mathrm{wt}(r_{\mathrm{revoked}}) \le \min(64, Rw)$:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Orthogonal Dual-Rail \texttt{LUT6\_2} Evaluation:} Revoking fine cohorts in $P_{\mathrm{fine}}$ causes zero collateral coarse revocations in $P_{\mathrm{coarse}}$, and both $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{coarse}}}(r_i) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$ (\texttt{O6}) and $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}}(c) == \mathbf{0}_{64}$ (\texttt{O5}) evaluate in 1~\texttt{LUT6\_2} level with 0~BRAM;
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Kautz--Singleton Disjunct Zero-Aliasing Regime ($R \le \lfloor (w-1)/\lambda \rfloor$)~\cite{kautz1964nonrandom}:} For any packing code $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}} \subset J(64,w)$ with maximum pairwise overlap $\lambda := \max_{c \neq c'} |\mathrm{supp}(c) \cap \mathrm{supp}(c')|$, $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}}$ is $R$-disjunct for all $R \le R_{\mathrm{exact}} := \lfloor (w-1)/\lambda \rfloor$, guaranteeing \textbf{strictly zero collateral false-positive revocations} ($P_{\mathrm{alias}}(R) \equiv 0$; for $v=64, w=8, \lambda=1$, $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 7$ and $|\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}}| \le A(64,14,8) = \lfloor \frac{64}{8}\lfloor \frac{63}{7}\rfloor \rfloor = 72$, achieved with equality by $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$; for $\lambda = 2$, $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 3$ across 520 MDS codewords); and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Exact Without-Replacement Hypergeometric Tail Bound over $J(64,w)$ ($R > R_{\mathrm{exact}}$):} Over full $\mathcal{C} = J(64,w)$ (where $\binom{64}{8} = 4,426,165,368 > 2^{32}$, and $\binom{64}{4} = 635,376$ vs.\ $\binom{50}{4} = 230,300$), conditioned on $u(R)$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:hypergeom}
  P_{\mathrm{alias}}^J(R \mid u) = \frac{\binom{u(R)}{w} - R}{\binom{64}{w} - R} < \prod_{j=0}^{w-1} \frac{u(R)-j}{64-j} \le \left(\frac{u(R)}{64}\right)^w,\qquad
  \mathbb{E}\bigl[P_{\mathrm{alias}}^J(R)\bigr] \le \bigl(p_{\mathrm{bit}}(R)\bigr)^w = \Bigl(1 - (1 - w/64)^R\Bigr)^w,
  \end{equation}
  which for $v = 64, w = 8$ gives $P_{\mathrm{alias}}^J(1) \equiv 0$, single-draw collision $\binom{64}{8}^{-1} \approx 2.259 \times 10^{-10}$, $R = 2$ ($u \le 16$) worst-case $(\binom{16}{8} - 2)/(\binom{64}{8} - 2) = 12,868 / 4,426,165,366 \approx 2.91 \times 10^{-6}$, and $R = 5$ expected rate $\le (1 - (7/8)^5)^8 = 0.00316870 \approx 0.317\%$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:johnson}]
Claim~(i) follows from Proposition~\ref{prop:monotonic}. For Claim~(ii), each $c^{(m)}$ shares at most $\lambda$ ones with any $c' \in \mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{pack}} \setminus \{c^{(1)},\ldots,c^{(R)}\}$, so $R \le \lfloor (w-1)/\lambda \rfloor$ revocations cover at most $R\lambda \le w - 1 < w$ ones of $c'$, leaving $\ge 1$ surviving bit in $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}}(c') \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$ ($P_{\mathrm{alias}}(R) \equiv 0$). For Claim~(iii), exactly $\binom{u(R)}{w} - R$ of the $\binom{64}{w} - R$ unrevoked codewords in $J(64,w)$ have support inside $\mathrm{supp}(r_{\mathrm{revoked}})$, giving Eq.~\eqref{eq:hypergeom}, and negative association of without-replacement bit coverage gives $\mathbb{E}[P_{\mathrm{alias}}^J(R)] \le (1 - (1 - w/64)^R)^w$.
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Pre-Core Zero-Sideband Carry-Free \texorpdfstring{$\mathbb{F}_8$}{F8} Steiner \texorpdfstring{$S(2,8,64)$}{S(2,8,64)} and \texorpdfstring{$[8,3,6]_8$}{[8,3,6]8} MDS Cohort Expander (Mode C)}
\label{sec:modec}
Over $\mathbb{F}_8 = \mathrm{GF}(2^3) \cong \mathbb{F}_2[z]/(z^3 + z + 1)$, the 8 elements $x_r \in \{0,\ldots,7\}$ have constant wire-permuted squares $x_r^2 = (0,1,4,5,6,7,2,3)$ and inverses $d^{-1} = d^6 = d \odot d^5$. Partitioning $r_i[63:0]$ into 8 byte lanes $r \in \{0,\ldots,7\}$ utilizes $100\%$ of the 64 bits with zero carry chains.

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Unmodified commercial CXL~3.0--4.0 host processors do not emit a separate 256-bit capability sideband bus; they issue standard \texttt{CXL.mem} requests carrying only a 52-bit Host Physical Address ($\mathrm{HPA}$). To support unmodified hosts without on-chip BRAM tables, Cycle~0 decrypts a compact 8-bit or 10-bit cohort descriptor $\mathrm{cid}_i$ embedded in the upper $\mathrm{HPA}$ bits and expands it combinationally into a 64-bit codeword by evaluating polynomial lines $y_r = a \oplus (b \odot x_r) \oplus (c \odot x_r^2)$ over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_8$. Because two distinct lines in the affine plane $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ intersect in at most $\lambda = 1$ point (and parallel lines of equal slope $b$ intersect in $\lambda = 0$ points), pure XOR/AND logic synthesizes an optimal Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ code with zero lookup tables and zero arithmetic carries, then zeroes the upper $\mathrm{HPA}$ slice bits before Stage~1 so downstream memory controllers never fault.

\begin{theorem}[Pre-Core Carry-Free $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ / MDS Expander and Tweakable Multi-Width Feistel HPA Sealing]
\label{thm:modec}
Let an incoming Mode-C request slot carry $\mathrm{SPID}_i[13:0]$ and a $c_{\mathrm{bits}}$-bit descriptor $\mathrm{cid}_i$ ($c_{\mathrm{bits}} \in \{8,10\}$) in the upper bits of an even-width slice $\mathrm{HPA}_{\mathrm{wire},i}[12 + 2n - 1 : 12]$ across $2n \in \{24, 34, 36, 40\}$ ($n \in \{12, 17, 18, 20\}$). In the 10-bit profile ($\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{0,\ldots,1023\}$), the Cycle-0 expander partitions $\mathrm{cid}_i$ into:
(1)~\textbf{Affine Lines \& Quadratic MDS Cohorts ($\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{0,\ldots,511\}$):} with $c = \mathrm{cid}_i[8:6], b = \mathrm{cid}_i[5:3], a = \mathrm{cid}_i[2:0] \in \mathbb{F}_8$, asserts $\mathrm{fine}_i = 1$ and evaluates in each byte lane $r \in \{0,\ldots,7\}$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:f8_expander}
y_r(a,b,c) := a \oplus (b \odot x_r) \oplus (c \odot x_r^2) \in \mathbb{F}_8,\qquad
\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(a,b,c) := \Bigl[ \bigl(\texttt{8'd1} \ll y_7\bigr) \parallel \cdots \parallel \bigl(\texttt{8'd1} \ll y_0\bigr) \Bigr];
\end{equation}
(2)~\textbf{Vertical Byte Lines ($\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{512,\ldots,519\}$):} asserts $\mathrm{fine}_i = 1$ and drives $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_i) = \texttt{64'hFF} \ll (8 \cdot \mathrm{cid}_i[2:0]) \in J(64,8)$;
(3)~\textbf{64 1-Hot Coarse Arenas ($\mathrm{cid}_i \in \{576,\ldots,639\}$):} asserts $\mathrm{fine}_i = 0$ and drives $r_i = \texttt{64'd1} \ll \mathrm{cid}_i[5:0]$; and
(4)~\textbf{Invalid Codepoints (remaining 440 codes):} drives $r_i = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ ($584/1024 = 73/128$ valid ratio; or $64+128+64 = 256/256$ in the 8-bit profile). Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Steiner $S(2,8,64) \cong \mathrm{AG}(2,8)$, Parallel-Slope Resolvability, \& $[8,3,6]_8$ MDS Bounds:} The $64 + 8 = 72$ linear/vertical codewords construct all $\binom{64}{2}/\binom{8}{2} = 72$ lines of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$, achieving $A(64,14,8) = 72$ with equality ($\lambda = 1 \implies R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 7$ across all 2,556 pairs). Within any fixed parallel slope $b_0 \in \mathbb{F}_8 \cup \{\infty\}$, the 8 lines are pairwise bit-disjoint ($\lambda = 0$ across all 252 intra-slope pairs), whereas inter-slope lines intersect in $\lambda = 1$ bit across all 2,304 pairs. Across all 520 extended $[8,3,6]_8$ MDS cohorts (134,940 pairs), $\lambda \le 2 \implies R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 3$ (whereas $S(3,8,64)$ does not exist since $62/6 \notin \mathbb{Z}$; Lean~4 \id{thm17_gf8_steiner_ag28_slope_uniqueness}, Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2}, Row~12);
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Coprime Feistel Diffusion \& Speculative-Read-Safe Write Lockout:} For all $n \in \{12, 17, 18, 20\}$, $\gcd(7,n) = 1$, achieving full half-word diffusion in 6 two-round stages with $\mathrm{SPID}_i$ bound into $T_i$. Unauthenticated Mode-C.2 probes fail $(\mathrm{guard}_g == \mathbf{0}_g) \wedge \mathrm{cid\_valid}_i$ with probability $1 - \frac{73}{128} 2^{-g}$; on an architectural \texttt{M2S\_RwD} write guard failure ($\texttt{slot\_is\_rwd\_hdr\_i}[i] == 1$), hardware clears $\texttt{CSR\_0x70}[\mathrm{SPID}_i[5:0]] \leftarrow \texttt{1'b0}$ and increments \texttt{CSR 0x48}, while speculative \texttt{M2S\_Req} reads never lock out an innocent VM; and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Canonical HPA Slice Restoration:} Cycle~0 strips $\{\mathrm{cid}_i, \mathrm{guard}_g\} \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ across the upper $(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)$ bits of the sealed slice ($\mathrm{HPA}_i[12+2n-1 : 12+2n-(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$, e.g., $\mathrm{HPA}_i[45:32] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$ for $2n=34, c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g=14$) and evaluates $\texttt{coarse\_ok}_i = (P_{\mathrm{coarse}} \gg \mathrm{arena\_idx}_i) \ \&\ \texttt{1'b1}$ prior to Stage~1, preventing downstream AXI \texttt{DECERR} faults.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:modec}]
Each fine codeword sets one bit in each of the 8 byte lanes (or all 8 bits of one vertical lane), so $\mathrm{wt}(\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}) \equiv 8$. Over $\mathbb{F}_8$, two linear codewords with $b_1 = b_2$ and $a_1 \neq a_2$ have $y_r(a_1,b_1,0) \oplus y_r(a_2,b_1,0) = a_1 \oplus a_2 \neq 0$ for all $r \in \mathbb{F}_8$ ($\lambda = 0$ intra-slope). If $b_1 \neq b_2$, $d := b_1 \oplus b_2 \in \mathbb{F}_8^\times$ has inverse $d^{-1} = d^6$, giving the unique intersection root $x_r = d^{-1} \odot (a_1 \oplus a_2) \in \mathbb{F}_8$ ($\lambda = 1$ inter-slope). Non-zero quadratic differences in $\mathbb{F}_8[x]$ have degree $\le 2$ and thus at most $\lambda = 2$ roots ($R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 3$).
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Distance-Doubling $\mathbb{F}_8^8 \hookrightarrow J(64,8)$ Isometry \& Tight Welch--Sidelnikov Frame Optimality]
\label{cor:frame}
Under the 1-hot byte-lane embedding $\psi_{\text{1-hot}} : \mathbb{F}_8^8 \hookrightarrow J(64,8)$ and the $72 \times 64$ incidence matrix $N$ of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$:
(i)~for all $\mathbf{y}, \mathbf{y}' \in \mathbb{F}_8^8$ with agreement count $\lambda(\mathbf{y},\mathbf{y}')$,
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:isometry_f8}
d_H^{\mathbb{F}_2}\bigl(\psi_{\text{1-hot}}(\mathbf{y}), \psi_{\text{1-hot}}(\mathbf{y}')\bigr) = 2\,d_H^{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathbf{y}, \mathbf{y}') = 2\bigl(8 - \lambda(\mathbf{y}, \mathbf{y}')\bigr),
\end{equation}
and a $t$-of-$8$ survival threshold guarantees $P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}}(t,\lambda) = \lfloor (8 - t)/\lambda \rfloor$; and
(ii)~because every point of $\mathbb{F}_8^2$ lies on $r_{\mathrm{deg}} = 9$ lines and every pair of points lies on $\lambda_{\mathrm{design}} = 1$ line, $N^T N = 8 I_{64} + J_{64}$ with eigenvalues $\mu_0 = 72$ (mult.\ $1$) and $\mu_1 = 8$ (mult.\ $63$), forming a tight two-distance frame in $\mathbb{R}^{64}$ achieving the Welch--Sidelnikov bound~\cite{welch1974lower}.
\end{corollary}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Corollary~\ref{cor:frame}]
Each byte lane $r \in \{0,\ldots,7\}$ contributes binary Hamming distance $0$ if $y_r = y'_r$ and $2$ if $y_r \neq y'_r$, giving Eq.~\eqref{eq:isometry_f8}. In $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$, $(N^T N)_{u,v}$ equals $9$ for $u = v$ and $1$ for $u \neq v$, yielding $N^T N = 8 I_{64} + J_{64}$.
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Dual-Submode Cycle-0 Vieta Polynomial-Ideal \& Time-Wheel / Steiner Tensor Sieve over \texorpdfstring{$\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$}{F\_(2\^8)[Z]}}
\label{sec:vieta}
Any Boolean cover-free family over $v$ bits supporting $N$ cohorts up to $R$ revocations obeys the Erd\H{o}s--Frankl--F\"{u}redi bound $v = \Omega((R^2 / \log R)\log N)$~\cite{erdos1985families,macwilliams1977theory}, and revoking all 8 lines of a single parallel slope $b_0$ in $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ sets $P_{\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$. Furthermore, driving an all-ones vector $r_i = \mathbf{1}_{64}$ with $p_{s,\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{1}_{64}$ into Stage~1's \texttt{O5} check $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}}(\mathbf{1}_{64}) = p_{t,\mathrm{fine}} == \mathbf{0}_{64}$ (as in Coarse-Only Vieta mode $\texttt{CSR~0x78}[51:50] = \texttt{2'b10}$) would fire only when all 64 bits of \texttt{CSR 0x68} are zero---collapsing the 64-bit fine temporal mask into a 1-bit global switch, idling Stage~1's 256 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitives, and burning scarce Vieta roots on routine lease expirations.

To break both the quadratic cover-free barrier and the all-ones temporal degeneracy without modifying a single gate of the \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT Stage-1/2 core, our \textbf{Dual-Submode Cycle-0 Vieta Polynomial-Ideal \& Time-Wheel / Steiner Tensor Sieve} ($\texttt{CSR~0x78}[51:50] \in \{\texttt{2'b01}, \texttt{2'b11}\}$, complementing Boolean mode $\texttt{2'b00}$ and Coarse-Only Vieta mode $\texttt{2'b10}$) over $\mathbb{F}_{2^8} \cong \mathbb{F}_2[z]/(z^8 + z^4 + z^3 + z + 1)$ stores the 8 elementary symmetric Vieta coefficients $(\sigma_1^{(t)}, \ldots, \sigma_8^{(t)}) \in (\mathbb{F}_{2^8})^8$ ($64$ bits) in \texttt{CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]} with 4-bit degree counters $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \in \{0,\ldots,8\}$ (\texttt{CSR 0x20}[11:8]) and saturation lockout bit (\texttt{CSR 0x20}[12]), while pairing Cycle~0's Vieta non-root check with either an autonomous 64-bucket 1-hot time-wheel in \texttt{CSR 0x68} (Submode~V.1, $\texttt{2'b01}$) or the $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}$ Steiner/MDS expander (Submode~V.2, $\texttt{2'b11}$).

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Any Boolean superimposed code over $v=64$ bits faces a fundamental combinatorial wall: Erd\H{o}s--Frankl--F\"{u}redi theory demands $v = \Omega(R^2 \log N)$ bits, and revoking all 8 parallel lines of a single slope $b_0$ in $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ zeroes every bit of $P_{\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$. We break this quadratic barrier by reinterpreting the 64-bit register \texttt{CSR 0x60} not as 64 independent Boolean flags, but as the 8 elementary symmetric coefficients $(\sigma_1,\ldots,\sigma_8) \in (\mathbb{F}_{2^8})^8$ of a degree-8 annihilator polynomial $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) = Z^{8-d_{\mathrm{rev}}} \prod_{j=1}^{d_{\mathrm{rev}}} (Z \oplus \alpha_j)$ over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$. Because a polynomial of degree $d_{\mathrm{rev}} \le 8$ over a field has \emph{at most $d_{\mathrm{rev}}$ roots}, revoking a cohort $\alpha_j$ simply multiplies $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$ by $(Z \oplus \alpha_j)$: every revoked cohort evaluates to $\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha_j) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$, every unrevoked cohort is guaranteed to evaluate to $\Lambda^{(t)}(z) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ ($P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$, even within a single slope), and because $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ has no zero divisors, no software write can ever un-revoke an existing root.

\begin{theorem}[Dual-Submode Cycle-0 Vieta Polynomial-Ideal \& Time-Wheel / Steiner Tensor Sieve over {$\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$}]
\label{thm:vieta}
Let each fine 4-KB cohort carry non-zero descriptor $\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ ($N = 255$). In Vieta modes ($\texttt{CSR 0x78}[50] = 1$), each bank of \texttt{CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]} stores $\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(t)} = (\sigma_1^{(t)}, \ldots, \sigma_8^{(t)}) \in (\mathbb{F}_{2^8})^8$ ($\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(0)} = \mathbf{0}_{64}, d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(0)} = 0$) representing the monic degree-8 annihilator polynomial:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:vieta_poly}
\Lambda^{(t)}(Z) := Z^8 \oplus \bigoplus_{k=1}^8 \sigma_k^{(t)} \odot Z^{8-k}
= Z^{8 - d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}} \odot P^{(t)}(Z), \qquad
P^{(t)}(Z) := \prod_{j=1}^{d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}} \bigl(Z \oplus \alpha_j\bigr).
\end{equation}
When cohort $\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ is written to \texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x60}, hardware evaluates $\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha)$ and updates $(\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{(t+1)}, d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t+1)})$ via:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:vieta_ratchet}
\sigma_k^{(t+1)} :=
\begin{dcases}
\sigma_k^{(t)} \oplus \bigl(\alpha \odot \sigma_{k-1}^{(t)}\bigr), & \text{if } \Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha) \neq 0 \wedge d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} < 8,\\
\sigma_k^{(t)}, & \text{if } \Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha) = 0 \text{ (duplicate)},
\end{dcases}
\end{equation}
with $\sigma_0^{(t)} \equiv 1_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ (locking fail-closed at \texttt{CSR 0x20}[12] if a 9th distinct root arrives before bank rotation). In \textbf{Cycle~0}, for each slot $i \in \{0,1,2,3\}$ with bank $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$, hardware conjoins the explicit non-zero descriptor guard $(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}})$ and registers
\[
\texttt{slot\_coarse\_arena\_ok\_i}[i] \Leftarrow \bigl(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr) \wedge \bigl(\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i}) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr) \wedge \texttt{arena\_ok}_i \wedge \texttt{bank\_valid\_q}[b_i]
\]
onto Stage~1's input (noting that while $Z^{8 - d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}}$ vanishes at $Z=0$ for $0 \le d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \le 7$, conjoining $\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ closes the $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} = 8$ boundary case where $\Lambda^{(t)}(0) = \sigma_8^{(t)} = \prod_{j=1}^8 \alpha_j \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$) and selects:
\begin{itemize}
  \item \textbf{Submode V.1 (Autonomous 64-Bucket Time-Wheel + Vieta Quarantine, $\texttt{CSR 0x78}[51:50] = \texttt{2'b01}$):} Cycle~0 decodes slot $i$'s 6-bit lease-expiration bucket $\tau_i = \texttt{Meta\_Tag}_i[5:0] \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$ into 1-hot $r_i = \texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i \in J(64,1)$ with $p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]} = \mathbf{1}_{64}$;
  \item \textbf{Submode V.2 (Steiner/MDS $\otimes$ Vieta Tensor-Product Sieve, $\texttt{CSR 0x78}[51:50] = \texttt{2'b11}$):} Cycle~0 drives $r_i = \Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{hi},i}) \in J(64,8)$ into Stage~1's 256 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} cells against $P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]} = p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]} \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}$.
\end{itemize}
Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Linear Bit Scaling $v = R \cdot m$, Exact $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(r) \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$, \& Core Timing Invariance:} Over the integral domain $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$, $\Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$ has at most $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \le 8$ roots in $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:vieta_roots}
  \mathrm{Roots}\bigl(\Lambda^{(t)}\bigr) := \bigl\{z \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times : \Lambda^{(t)}(z) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr\}
  = \bigl\{\alpha_1, \ldots, \alpha_{d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)}}\bigr\}.
  \end{equation}
  Thus, for every revocation set $\{\alpha_1,\ldots,\alpha_r\} \subset \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ of size $1 \le r \le 8$ (even within a single slope), $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(r) \equiv 0$ across all 255 cohorts with $v = 8 \times 8 = 64$ bits while keeping the \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT Stage-1/2 core (\StageOneSieveDelayNs\ sieve) $100\%$ unchanged;
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Unconditional Polynomial-Ideal Root Permanence:} Because $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}$ has no zero divisors, the active root factor $P^{(t+1)}(Z) = (Z \oplus \alpha) \odot P^{(t)}(Z)$ generates a descending polynomial-ideal chain $\langle P^{(0)} \rangle \supseteq \cdots \supseteq \langle P^{(t)} \rangle$ in $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$. In the active shift-multiply branch ($\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha) \neq 0 \wedge d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} < 8$, where $\sigma_8^{(t)} = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$), update~\eqref{eq:vieta_ratchet} satisfies $Z \odot \Lambda^{(t+1)}(Z) = (Z \oplus \alpha) \odot \Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$, whereas in the duplicate/saturated branch ($\Lambda^{(t)}(\alpha) = 0 \vee d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} = 8$), $\Lambda^{(t+1)}(Z) = \Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$. Consequently, for every non-zero descriptor $z^* \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ and any $\alpha \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:vieta_monotone}
  \Lambda^{(t)}(z^*) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}} \implies z^* \odot \Lambda^{(t+1)}(z^*) = (z^* \oplus \alpha) \odot \Lambda^{(t)}(z^*) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}} \implies \Lambda^{(t+1)}(z^*) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}},
  \end{equation}
  preventing any compromised FM write from un-revoking a root $z^*$ (Lean~4 \id{thm20_vieta_gf256_ideal_ratchet_monotonic_and_degree_bound}, Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2}, Row~14); and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Exact 64-Bucket Time-Wheel Selectivity ($\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$) \& Tensor Namespace Multiplication ($18,360$ / $132,600$ Cohorts):} In Submode~V.1, Stage~1's \texttt{O5} projection satisfies:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:timewheel_tensor}
  \begin{aligned}
  \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]}}\bigl(\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i\bigr) = \mathbf{0}_{64} &\iff p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}[\tau_i] = 0,\\
  \mathrm{Pass}_{\mathrm{V.2}}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{hi}}, \mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo}}) &\iff \bigl(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo}} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr)
  \wedge \bigl(\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo}}) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}\bigr)
  \wedge \bigl(\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]}}(\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{hi}})) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}\bigr).
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  A monotonic hardware tick ratchets \texttt{CSR 0x68} bit-by-bit to expire ephemeral serverless/KV-cache leases automatically with zero MMIO traffic and zero Vieta degree growth ($\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$), while Submode~V.2 expands the zero-BRAM namespace to $72 \times 255 = 18,360$ Steiner--Vieta cohorts (or $520 \times 255 = 132,600$ MDS--Vieta cohorts; Lean~4 \id{thm26_vieta_timewheel_and_tensor_sieve_selectivity}, Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2}, Row~17).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:vieta}]
Over the UFD $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$, $\Lambda^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}') = (\mathrm{cid}')^{8-r} \prod_{j=1}^r (\mathrm{cid}' \oplus \alpha_j)$ vanishes at $\mathrm{cid}' \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ iff $\mathrm{cid}' = \alpha_j$ for some $j \in \{1,\ldots,r\}$, forcing $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(r) \equiv 0$, while $(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}})$ rejects $\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} = \texttt{8'h00}$ across all $0 \le d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} \le 8$. For $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} < 8$, $\sigma_8^{(t)} = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$, so expanding $Z \odot \Lambda^{(t+1)}(Z) = (Z \oplus \alpha) \odot \Lambda^{(t)}(Z)$ yields the active branch of Eq.~\eqref{eq:vieta_ratchet} and multiplying by $(z^*)^{-1} \in \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ together with $b \odot 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}} = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ implies Eq.~\eqref{eq:vieta_monotone} (while $\Lambda^{(t+1)}(z^*) = \Lambda^{(t)}(z^*) = 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$ holds trivially in the duplicate/saturated branch). In Submode~V.1, $(\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i) \wedge (\mathbf{1}_{64} \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{fine}})$ has bit $j$ equal to $\mathbb{I}(j = \tau_i) \wedge p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}[j]$, which vanishes iff $p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}[j] = 0$ at $j = \tau_i$. In Submode~V.2, gating Stage~1's \texttt{O5} reduction by \texttt{slot\_coarse\_arena\_ok\_i[i]} forms the direct tensor product $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Steiner/MDS}} \times \mathbb{F}_{2^8}^\times$ (\texttt{thm20}, \texttt{thm26}).
\end{proof}
\MidConnesRule
\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
\captionsetup{hypcap=false}
\centering
\IncludeSpectralPlate{fig4_vieta_scaling.pdf}
