\textbf{So What? (Architectural Consequence).}\; Any Boolean bitmap or Bloom filter over 64 bits suffers either from the Erd\H{o}s--Frankl--F\"{u}redi quadratic cover-free limit or from catastrophic wipeout when 8 cohorts in the same affine slope are revoked simultaneously. Figure~\ref{fig:vieta_scaling} demonstrates that lifting the 64-bit \texttt{BAR0} register into an $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ Vieta annihilator polynomial paired with the Stage-1 Steiner/time-wheel sieve achieves strictly zero false-positive revocations ($P_{\mathrm{alias}} \equiv 0$) up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = 8$ arbitrary revocations across $18,360$--$132,600$ cohorts in $0\text{~bits}$ of SRAM, while expiring routine TTL leases autonomously with $\Delta d_{\mathrm{rev}} = 0$ root consumption.}
\label{fig:vieta_scaling}
\end{minipage}
\EndConnesPlate

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch~---~Structured Algebraic Cohorts vs.\ Unbounded Scatter-Gather).}\;Eliminating $\Theta(M)$ DRAM permission tables is not a free lunch: in exchange for 0~BRAM and $0.124\text{~ns}$ Stage-1 evaluation, memory allocations must be grouped into structured algebraic cohorts supporting $R_{\mathrm{exact}}=7$ arbitrary revocations in $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ or $R_{\mathrm{exact}}=8$ in $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ per active bank before triggering a background dual-bank rotation in \texttt{CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]} (or autonomous 64-bucket TTL lease expiration in \texttt{CSR 0x68}). Workloads demanding thousands of ungrouped, arbitrary single-page revocations per microsecond without epoch batching still require conventional stateful MMU page tables.

\section{Spanning-Forest Capability Propagation, Permutation-Orbit Chord Clamping, PHY-Gated Dual-Forest Failover, and Stage-2 \texorpdfstring{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}{LUT6\_2} Precision Clamping}
\label{sec:forest}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; In a multi-hop CXL switch fabric containing routing loops ($\beta_1(G) = |E| - |V| + \beta_0 > 0$), a compromised or deputized node can forward a request around a cycle to launder permissions or trigger an infinite distributed agreement loop. By decomposing the fabric graph offline into a spanning forest $T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ plus $\beta_1$ fundamental cycle chords $e^* \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ and enforcing port anti-hairpinning, every non-backtracking cyclic walk in $G$ is mathematically forced to cross at least one fundamental chord. Placing a single-gate combinational meet ratchet $\Gamma_{e^*}$ on each of those $\beta_1$ chords collapses the all-pairs Kleene-star transitive closure of the cyclic graph identically to the loop-free spanning forest in zero online consensus rounds.

\begin{theorem}[Spanning-Forest Decomposition, Anti-Hairpinning, \& Fundamental-Chord Cycle Interception]
\label{thm:forest}
Let the multi-hop interconnect graph $G = (V, E)$ with incidence matrix $B \in \{-1, 0, +1\}^{|V| \times |E|}$ and $\beta_0$ connected components be decomposed in $O(|E|)$ offline time~\cite{tarjan1983data} via a spanning forest $T_{\mathrm{forest}} \subset E$ into $|V| - \beta_0$ tree branches $e \in T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ and $\beta_1 = \dim \ker(B) = |E| - |V| + \beta_0$ fundamental cycle chords $e^* \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$, with port anti-hairpinning ($\texttt{egress\_port} \neq \texttt{ingress\_port}$) at every switch port. Then: (i)~every chord-avoiding subgraph $S \subseteq T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ has $\beta_1(S) = 0$ and admits zero non-backtracking closed walks of length $\ge 2$; and (ii)~every simple cycle $C \subseteq E$ of length $\ge 3$ intersects $E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ in at least one chord $e^* \in C \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$, so ratcheting the $\beta_1$ chords intercepts $100\%$ of cyclic walks in $G$.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:forest}]
Any $S \subseteq T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ is acyclic, so any closed walk in $S$ must backtrack immediately ($u \rightarrow v \rightarrow u$), which is blocked by $\texttt{egress\_port} \neq \texttt{ingress\_port}$. Any simple cycle $C \subseteq E$ has $\mathbf{1}_C \in \ker(B) \setminus \{\mathbf{0}\}$; since columns of $B$ on $T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ are linearly independent, $\ker(B) \cap \mathbb{R}^{T_{\mathrm{forest}}} = \{\mathbf{0}\}$, forcing $C \cap (E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}) \neq \emptyset$.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Spanning-Forest Zero-Iteration Agreement, Chord-Ratchet Cycle Clamping, \& Dioidal Kleene-Star Tree-Closure Collapse]
\label{thm:agreement}
Let each node and link carry capability tuples in the bounded meet-semilattice $(\mathcal{L}, \preceq, \sqcap)$ with $\mathcal{L} = \mathbb{F}_2^{64} \times D_\perp$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:embedding}), where $\tau_1 \sqcap \tau_2 := [(r_1 \wedge r_2) \parallel (\mathrm{SPID}_1 \wedge_{\mathrm{flat}} \mathrm{SPID}_2)]$ and $\alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v) = x_v \sqcap m_{v,e}$. Define the consistency operator $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0$ and edge-admissible set $\Gamma_{\mathrm{adm}}^1(G)$ by:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:cons}
(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0 x)_v := x_v \sqcap \underset{e=(u,v) \in E}{\bigsqcap} \bigl(\alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_u) \sqcap \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v)\bigr),\qquad
\Gamma_{\mathrm{adm}}^0(G) := \mathrm{Fix}(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0), \qquad \Gamma_{\mathrm{adm}}^1(G) := \delta_{\mathrm{edge}}^0\bigl(\Gamma_{\mathrm{adm}}^0(G)\bigr),
\end{equation}
where $(\delta_{\mathrm{edge}}^0 x)_e := \alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_u) \sqcap \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v)$. If tree branches $e \in T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ satisfy $x_u = \alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_u) = \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v) = x_v$ and every fundamental chord $e^* = (u,v) \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ instantiates the combinational meet ratchet across $C(e^*) \subset T_{\mathrm{forest}} \cup \{e^*\}$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:chord_ratchet}
\tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{out}} := \tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{in}} \sqcap \Gamma_{e^*},\qquad
\Gamma_{e^*} := \alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e^*}^*(x_u) \sqcap \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e^*}^*(x_v) \sqcap \underset{w \in C(e^*)}{\bigsqcap} x_w,
\end{equation}
then: (i)~tree-consistent vertex assignments are stationary under $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0$ in 0~online consensus rounds; (ii)~over $\mathcal{L}$, the meet operators $\{\mu_a(\tau) = \tau \sqcap a\}$ factor through $(\mathbb{B}^{\beta_1}, \vee)$, clamping any closed walk $W$ traversing chords $\{e_1^*,\ldots,e_r^*\}$ to $\tau_W^{\mathrm{out}} \preceq \bigsqcap_{q=1}^r \Gamma_{e_q^*}$ in 1~gate delay per chord; and (iii)~over the dioid $\mathcal{D}_{64} = (\mathbb{F}_2^{64}, \vee, \wedge, \mathbf{0}_{64}, \mathbf{1}_{64})$, the all-pairs Kleene-star closure across $G$ collapses identically to the spanning-forest closure:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:kleene_collapse}
\bigl(A_{G,\mathrm{ratcheted}}\bigr)_{a,b}^* := \bigvee_{k=0}^{|V|-1} \bigl(A_{G,\mathrm{ratcheted}}^k\bigr)_{a,b}
\equiv \bigl(A_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}\bigr)_{a,b}^* = \underset{w \in P_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}(a,b)}{\bigsqcap} x_w.
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:agreement}]
Tree agreement gives $(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0 x)_v = x_v$, proving Claim~(i). Associativity, commutativity, and idempotence of $\sqcap$ prove Claim~(ii). Replacing any chord $e^* = (u,v)$ in $W$ by $P_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}(u,v) \subset C(e^*)$ yields a tree path dominating $\Gamma_{e^*}$, so absorption $X \vee Y = X$ for $Y \preceq X$ in $\mathcal{D}_{64}$ proves Eq.~\eqref{eq:kleene_collapse}.
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Orthogonal Pass/Violation Bitwise Split, Non-Zero Capability Check, and Monotonic \texorpdfstring{\texttt{BAR0}}{BAR0} Ratcheting}

\BeginConnesPlate
\begin{corollary}[Orthogonal Pass/Violation Bitwise Split, Non-Zero Capability Sieve, \& Cold-Reset-Isolated AND-Ratchet]
\label{cor:ratchet}
For active mask $P_{\mathrm{eff}} := p_s \wedge p_t \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$, pass projection $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) := r_i \wedge P_{\mathrm{eff}}$, violation operator $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) := r_i \wedge \neg(p_s \wedge p_t)$, and admissible non-zero set
\[
\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{adm}} := \bigl\{(r_i, \mathrm{SPID}_i) :\; r_i \neq \mathbf{0}_{64},\; \mathrm{SPID}_i \neq 0,\;
\mathrm{SPID}_i[13:6] == \texttt{port\_spid\_prefix},\;
\texttt{CSR\_0x70}[\mathrm{SPID}_i[5:0]] == 1\bigr\}:
\]
(i)~$\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}^2 = \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ and $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}^2 = \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}$ decompose $\mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ into the direct sum:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:direct_sum}
\mathbb{F}_2^{64} = \ker(\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}) \oplus \mathrm{im}(\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}),\qquad
r_i = \Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i) \oplus \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i),
\end{equation}
while intersecting with $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{adm}}$ rejects $r_i = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ and aliased tenant IDs; and
(ii)~enforcing $M^{(t+1)} := M^{(t)} \wedge w$ on \texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x00, 0x08, 0x60, 0x68, 0x70} while isolating security CSRs from \texttt{host\_warm\_flr\_rst\_n} guarantees $(M^{(t)} \wedge w) \wedge \neg M^{(t)} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, preventing $0 \rightarrow 1$ escalation across warm FLRs and incrementing \texttt{CSR 0x48} whenever $(w \wedge \neg M^{(t)}) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$.
\end{corollary}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Corollary~\ref{cor:ratchet}]
In $\mathbb{F}_2^{64}$, $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} + \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = \mathrm{Id}$ and $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} \circ \Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}} = 0$, proving Eq.~\eqref{eq:direct_sum}; Claim~(ii) follows from Proposition~\ref{prop:monotonic} (Lean~4 \texttt{thm01}, \texttt{thm02}).
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Permutation-Orbit Chord Clamping, PHY-Gated Dual-Forest Failover, Fractured \texorpdfstring{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}{LUT6\_2} MOESI \& Graduated Mantissa Clamping, and Keyed-Dither Multi-Query Lower Bounds}
\label{sec:sheaf_moesi}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Three practical challenges arise in multi-tenant AI clusters: (1)~intermediate CXL switches may apply HDM address-remapping permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$ whose group order reaches Landau's $g(64) = \mathrm{lcm}(8,3,5,7,11,13,17) = 2,042,040$, seemingly requiring millions of iterations to stabilize around a cycle; (2)~a physical cable cut on a primary spanning-tree link requires sub-4-ns failover without allowing software MMIO writes to re-enable revoked bits; and (3)~in shared-HBM foundation-model inference, co-tenant GPUs need approximate read access (\texttt{BF12}/\texttt{E8M0}) without being able to extract exact \texttt{BF16} weights or issue coherent \texttt{RdOwn} requests to corrupt shared weights. Theorem~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi} resolves all three at $+0$ CLB LUTs: every individual bit coordinate has an orbit of length $L_j \le 64$ in $S_{64}$; pre-certified dual forests $(T_0,T_1)$ switch in $<4\text{~ns}$ directly from the hardware PCS loss-of-signal wire $p_{\mathrm{phy}}$; and the 3 unused inputs of Stage~2's existing \texttt{LUT2} gates are fractured into 5-shared-input \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitives that simultaneously downgrade $\mathrm{RdOwn}\mapsto\mathrm{RdShared}$ and clamp \texttt{S2M\_DRS} floating-point mantissas across 4 precision tiers ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits).

\begin{theorem}[Permutation-Orbit Chord Clamping, PHY-Gated Dual-Forest Failover, \& Fractured \texttt{LUT6\_2} MOESI and Graduated Mantissa Clamping]
\label{thm:sheaf_moesi}
Let $G = (V, E)$ carry switch HDM address-remapping permutations $\pi_e \in S_{64}$ ($\pi_{e^{-1}} = \pi_e^{-1}$) with root-to-vertex tree gauge $\Pi_v := \pi_{e_m} \circ \cdots \circ \pi_{e_1} \in S_{64}$ along $P_{T_{\mathrm{forest}}}(r_0, v)$. For each chord $e^* = (u,v) \in E \setminus T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ with cycle permutation $H_{C(e^*)} := \Pi_v^{-1} \circ \pi_{e^*} \circ \Pi_u \in S_{64}$, although $\mathrm{ord}(H_{C(e^*)})$ in $S_{64}$ can reach Landau's $g(64) = \mathrm{lcm}(8,3,5,7,11,13,17) = 2,042,040$, \textbf{every individual bit-coordinate orbit $\{H_{C(e^*)}^k(j)\}_{k \ge 0} \subseteq \{0,\ldots,63\}$ has cardinality $L_j \le 64$}. Define the coordinate-orbit-invariant chord mask:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:holonomy_orbit}
\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}} := \bigwedge_{k=0}^{63} H_{C(e^*)}^k\bigl(\Gamma_{e^*}\bigr) \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64},\qquad
\tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{out}} := \pi_{e^*}\bigl(\tau_{e^*}^{\mathrm{in}}\bigr) \sqcap \Pi_v\bigl(\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}\bigr).
\end{equation}
To provide sub-4-ns link failover under optical/electrical cable cuts without permitting $0 \rightarrow 1$ software MMIO writes (Corollary~\ref{cor:ratchet}(ii)), \texttt{spectralc} pre-certifies two maximally edge-disjoint spanning forests $(T_0, T_1)$~\cite{tutte1961problem,nashwilliams1961edge} with cold-POR AND-ratcheted orbit-clamped chord registers $(\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(0)}, \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)})$ multiplexed strictly by the hardware-only Physical Coding Sublayer (PCS) / UCIe~3.0 emergency sideband loss-of-signal wire $p_{\mathrm{phy}} = \texttt{pcs\_link\_up\_i} \in \{0,1\}$~\cite{ucie2025spec30}:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:phy_failover}
\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{active}}(p_{\mathrm{phy}}) := p_{\mathrm{phy}}\ ?\ \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(0)} : \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)}.
\end{equation}
Furthermore, in Stage~2, let $c_i[1:0] \in \{\texttt{2'b00},\texttt{2'b01},\texttt{2'b10},\texttt{2'b11}\}$ denote slot $i$'s 2-bit MOESI/precision tier ceiling (distinct from cohort codewords $c \in J(64,w)$). Let 4 of the 256 dual-LUT CLB sites clamp \texttt{CXL.cache D2H\_Req} MOESI state $x_i[1:0] \in \{\mathrm{I}=\texttt{2'b00} < \mathrm{S}=\texttt{2'b01} < \mathrm{E}=\texttt{2'b10} < \mathrm{M}=\texttt{2'b11}\}$ against $c_i[1:0]$ via fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} $\texttt{INIT} = \texttt{64'hCC00CC00\_A2E0A2E0}$ ($\texttt{I5}=\texttt{1'b1},\allowbreak \texttt{I4}=\texttt{1'b0},\allowbreak \texttt{I3}=c_i[1],\allowbreak \texttt{I2}=c_i[0],\allowbreak \texttt{I1}=x_i[1],\allowbreak \texttt{I0}=x_i[0]$), and let the remaining 252 \texttt{S2M\_DRS} read-payload CLB sites be configured as pin-locked 5-shared-input fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitives ($\texttt{I5}=\texttt{1'b1},\allowbreak \texttt{I4}=c_i[1],\allowbreak \texttt{I3}=c_i[0],\allowbreak \texttt{I2}=\mathrm{viol}_i,\allowbreak \texttt{I1}=f_{2m+1},\allowbreak \texttt{I0}=f_{2m}$, address $a = 16c_{i,1} + 8c_{i,0} + 4\mathrm{viol}_i + 2f_{2m+1} + f_{2m} \in \{0,\ldots,31\}$). Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{0-LUT Wire-Gauge Orbit Invariance \& Sub-4-ns PHY-Gated Dual-Forest Failover:} Since $L_j \le 64$, $H_{C(e^*)}(\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}) \equiv \Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ and $\tilde{\mu}_{e^*}^m(r) = H_{C(e^*)}^m(r) \wedge \Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}} \le \Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ on the first chord traversal ($m = 1$) at 0~LUT wire-gauge cost. When a primary branch $e_{\mathrm{cut}} \in T_0$ fails ($p_{\mathrm{phy}} : 1 \rightarrow 0$), hardware switches to $\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)}$ in $< 4.0$~ns with zero MMIO privilege escalation ($\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{active}}(p_{\mathrm{phy}}, \Gamma_0 \wedge w_0, \Gamma_1 \wedge w_1) \wedge \neg\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{active}}(p_{\mathrm{phy}}, \Gamma_0, \Gamma_1) = \mathbf{0}_{64}$) and collapses the Kleene-star closure over $G \setminus \{e_{\mathrm{cut}}\}$ identically to $(A_{T_1})^*$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm21}, \id{thm28_phy_gated_dual_forest_failover_safety}, Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2}, Rows~15 and~19);
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Single-\texttt{LUT6\_2} 2-Bit \texttt{CXL.cache} MOESI Clamp at $+0$ CLB LUTs:} $\texttt{INIT} = \texttt{64'hCC00CC00\_A2E0A2E0}$ evaluates $\texttt{O6} = c_i[1] \wedge x_i[1] = \ell_{\mathrm{eff}}[1]$ and $\texttt{O5} = \ell_{\mathrm{eff}}[0] = \min(x_i, c_i)[0]$ across all 16 $(c_i, x_i)$ states in 1~LUT delay at $+0$ CLB LUTs, enforcing \texttt{CXL\_NOP} ($c_i=\texttt{00} \implies \texttt{00}$), pass-through ($c_i=\texttt{11} \implies x_i$), and coherent $\mathrm{RdOwn} \mapsto \mathrm{RdShared}$ downgrade ($x_i \in \{\texttt{10},\texttt{11}\}, c_i=\texttt{01} \implies \texttt{01}$; Lean~4 \texttt{thm22}); and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Zero-Area Stage-2 Fractured \texttt{LUT6\_2} 4-Tier Graduated Mantissa Clamping \& Shannon Rate-Distortion Bound:} Across all 32 input states of $(c_i[1:0], \mathrm{viol}_i, f_{2m+1}, f_{2m})$ at $+0$ CLB LUTs and $+0$ logic levels, the exact 64-bit \texttt{INIT} masks:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:graduated_init}
  \begin{aligned}
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{SE}} &= \texttt{64'h0C0C0C0C\_0A0A0A0A}\;(\neg\mathrm{viol}_i\text{ hdr pass}), &
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{T1}} &= \texttt{64'h0C0C0C00\_0A0A0A00}\;(\neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge c_i \ge \texttt{2'b01}),\\
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Mid}} &= \texttt{64'h0C0C0000\_0A0A0000}\;(\neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge c_i \ge \texttt{2'b10}), &
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Low}} &= \texttt{64'h0C000000\_0A000000}\;(\neg\mathrm{viol}_i \wedge c_i == \texttt{2'b11}),\\
  \texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{BF16}[7:6]} &= \texttt{64'h0C0C0C00\_0A0A0000}, & &
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  partition each 16-bit \texttt{BF16} read-payload lane ($1\mathrm{s} + 8\mathrm{e} + 7\mathrm{m}$, bits $[15:0]$) across 8 fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} primitives: $4\times\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{T1}}$ on Sign/Exponent bits $[15:8]$, $1\times\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{BF16}[7:6]}$ straddling the byte boundary (passing Exponent bit~7 on \texttt{O6} when $c_i \ge \texttt{2'b01}$ while clamping Mantissa bit~6 on \texttt{O5} when $c_i < \texttt{2'b10}$), $1\times\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Mid}}$ on Mid-Mantissa bits $[5:4]$, and $2\times\texttt{INIT}_{\mathrm{Low}}$ on Low-Mantissa bits $[3:0]$. This enforces four exact precision tiers: $c_i = \texttt{2'b00}$ (or $\mathrm{viol}_i = 1$) yields $u = 0$ unmasked bits ($\texttt{16'h0000}$ full scrub); $c_i = \texttt{2'b01}$ yields $u = 9$ unmasked bits ($1\mathrm{s} + 8\mathrm{e} = \texttt{E8M0}$ shared-exponent coarse scale, clamping Mid $[6:4]$ and Low $[3:0]$ to $0$); $c_i = \texttt{2'b10}$ yields $u = 12$ unmasked bits ($1\mathrm{s} + 8\mathrm{e} + 3\mathrm{m} = \texttt{BF12}$ approximate inference read, clamping only Low $[3:0]$ to $0$); and $c_i = \texttt{2'b11}$ yields $u = 16$ unmasked bits (bit-exact \texttt{BF16}). For any exact weight $W_{\mathrm{exact}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{16}$ and observed output $Y_{\mathrm{obs}} = \mathcal{Q}_{c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i}(W_{\mathrm{exact}})$ keeping $u(c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i) \in \{0, 9, 12, 16\}$ unmasked bits/element, Shannon's data-processing inequality bounds weight-extraction mutual information for any post-processor $\hat{W}$ by:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:shannon_mantissa}
  I\bigl(W_{\mathrm{exact}}; \hat{W} \;\big|\; c_i, \mathrm{viol}_i\bigr) \le I\bigl(W_{\mathrm{exact}}; Y_{\mathrm{obs}} \;\big|\; c_i, \mathrm{viol}_i\bigr)
  \le \log_2\bigl|\mathrm{im}(\mathcal{Q}_{c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i})\bigr| = u(c_i, \mathrm{viol}_i),
  \end{equation}
  defeating shared-HBM LLM weight extraction while preserving partner inference utility (Lean~4 \id{thm27_lut6_2_graduated_mantissa_clamp_bisimulation}, Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2}, Row~18).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi}]
Each coordinate orbit $O_j = \{H_{C(e^*)}^{-k}(j) : k \ge 0\} \subseteq \{0,\ldots,63\}$ has size $L_j \le 64$, so $H_{C(e^*)}(\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}) = \Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$. Under $p_{\mathrm{phy}} = 0$, $e_{\mathrm{cut}}$ carries zero flits and every chord $e \in E \setminus T_1$ is clamped by $\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)} \preceq P_{T_1}$, so $P_{T_1} \vee (r \wedge \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{orb},(1)}) = P_{T_1}$ (\texttt{thm28}). For Claim~(ii), at $a = 4c_i + x_i \in \{0,\ldots,15\}$, $\texttt{16'hCC00}$ has 1s at $\{10,11,14,15\}$ and $\texttt{16'hA2E0}$ has 1s at $\{5,6,7,9,13,15\}$, matching $\min(x_i, c_i)$ (\texttt{thm22}). For Claim~(iii), within each 8-bit tier byte of \texttt{INIT}, the upper nibble ($\mathrm{viol}_i = 1$) is $\texttt{4'h0}$ and the lower nibble ($\mathrm{viol}_i = 0$) is $\texttt{4'hC}$ ($\texttt{1100}_2$, selecting $f_{2m+1}$ on \texttt{O6}) or $\texttt{4'hA}$ ($\texttt{1010}_2$, selecting $f_{2m}$ on \texttt{O5}) iff tier $c_i$ is enabled, establishing Eq.~\eqref{eq:graduated_init} (\texttt{thm27}); $|\mathrm{im}(\mathcal{Q}_{c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i})| = 2^{u(c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i)}$ with $u(c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i) \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:shannon_mantissa}.
\end{proof}
\MidConnesRule
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Deterministic mantissa truncation limits a single read to $u \in \{9,12\}$ bits, but if an adversary can inject controlled activation perturbations $\xi^{(q)}$ prior to quantization, binary search across the deterministic rounding threshold localizes an unknown significand $w \in [0,\Delta_u)$ to precision $\delta w$ in just $Q_{\mathrm{det}} = \lceil\log_2(\Delta_u/\delta w)\rceil$ queries ($Q_{\mathrm{det}} = 6$ at $64\times$ resolution). By adding an unsigned pseudorandom dither $\eta^{(q)} \sim \mathrm{Unif}[0,\Delta_u)$ (equivalently, a centered dither $\tilde{\eta}^{(q)} \sim \mathrm{Unif}[-\Delta_u/2,\Delta_u/2)$ plus a $+\Delta_u/2$ rounding offset) generated per read from the counter-seeded Cycle-0 $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}$ stream prior to Stage-2 floor truncation $\mathcal{Q}_{c_i}(z) = \Delta_u \lfloor z/\Delta_u \rfloor$, each read becomes an unbiased Bernoulli coin flip whose parameter $p(w)$ varies smoothly with $w$. Le~Cam's two-point method and Pinsker's inequality then force any adaptive adversary to average over $\Omega((\Delta_u/\delta w)^2)$ independent CXL reads ($Q \ge 192$--$512$), while legitimate high-dimensional dot products ($d_{\mathrm{model}} \ge 4,096$) self-average the zero-mean quantization residual $Y^{(q)} - (w+\xi^{(q)})$ at $O(1/\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{model}}})$.

\begin{theorem}[Adaptive $Q$-Query Sample-Complexity Lower Bound under Keyed Dithered Rate-Distortion]
\label{thm:dither_lower_bound}
While the frozen $+0$-LUT Stage-2 fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} clamp $\mathcal{Q}_{c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi}(iii)) enforces a strict static single-read mutual-information ceiling $I(W_{\mathrm{exact}}; Y_{\mathrm{obs}}) \le u(c_i,\mathrm{viol}_i)$ across all four tiers ($u = 0$ clamps all 16 bits to $\texttt{16'h0000}$ with $I \equiv 0$ and $Q = \infty$, while $u = 16$ is unquantized \texttt{BF16}), consider the non-trivial quantized inference tiers $u \in \{9, 12\}$ within a fixed \texttt{BF16} exponent bin of mantissa-ULP quantization step size $\Delta_u := 2^{16 - u} \in \{128, 16\}$. Suppose an adaptive model-extraction adversary controls an activation perturbation $\xi^{(q)}$ prior to quantization and seeks to localize a target significand parameter $w \in [0, \Delta_u)$ to precision $\delta w \in (0, \Delta_u/4]$ across $Q$ adaptive queries:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Deterministic Boundary Dichotomy:} Under unkeyed deterministic truncation $Y^{(q)} = \Delta_u \lfloor (w + \xi^{(q)})/\Delta_u \rfloor$, if the adversary cannot perturb $w$ ($\xi^{(q)} \equiv 0$), $Q$ repeated CXL reads return the identical bitvector $Y^{(1)} = \cdots = Y^{(Q)}$ ($0$ additional bits); however, if the adversary can adaptively sweep $\xi^{(q)}$, binary search across the step boundary localizes $w$ to accuracy $\delta w$ in $Q_{\mathrm{det}} = \lceil \log_2(\Delta_u / \delta w) \rceil$ queries; and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Quadratic Sample-Complexity Wall under Keyed Stochastic Dither:} In the modular responder-side Cycle-0 pre-clamping extension (outside the frozen \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT core), injecting an ephemeral unsigned pseudorandom dither $\eta^{(q)} \sim \mathrm{Unif}[0, \Delta_u)$ (equivalently, $\eta^{(q)} = \Delta_u/2 + \tilde{\eta}^{(q)}$ with centered $\tilde{\eta}^{(q)} \sim \mathrm{Unif}[-\Delta_u/2, \Delta_u/2)$) derived per read $q$ from the existing zero-BRAM $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}$ PRF stream seeded with a monotonic per-read counter $q_{\mathrm{cnt}}$ (so repeated reads to an identical \texttt{HPA} never reuse a static dither sample, with the 1-bit carry out of bit $15-u$ propagating into bit $16-u$---mantissa bit~4 for $u=12$ or exponent LSB bit~7 for $u=9$, guarded only at $\texttt{exp} = \texttt{8'hFE}$ so finite values never overflow to $\mathrm{Inf}/\mathrm{NaN}$ and sign bit~15 is never altered) prior to Stage-2 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} floor truncation $\mathcal{Q}_{c_i}(z) = \Delta_u \lfloor z/\Delta_u \rfloor$ yields unbiased randomized rounding $Y^{(q)} = \mathcal{Q}_{c_i}(w + \xi^{(q)} + \eta^{(q)})$ with $\mathbb{E}[Y^{(q)} \mid w, \xi^{(q)}] = w + \xi^{(q)}$ (so inner-product activations across model dimension $d_{\mathrm{model}} \ge 4,096$ average out the zero-mean rounding error at $O(1/\sqrt{d_{\mathrm{model}}})$ without degrading LLM inference perplexity). For any two hypotheses $w_0, w_1$ with $|w_1 - w_0| = \delta w$ in the interior $\bigl[\frac{\Delta_u}{4}, \frac{3\Delta_u}{4}\bigr]$ (or globally under smoothed triangular dither $\eta_1^{(q)} + \eta_2^{(q)}$), the single-query Kullback--Leibler divergence satisfies $D_{\mathrm{KL}}\bigl(P_{w_0}^{(q)} \,\|\, P_{w_1}^{(q)}\bigr) \le \frac{16}{3}(\delta w / \Delta_u)^2$ globally on $[1/4, 3/4]$ and $\le 2(\delta w/\Delta_u)^2$ locally around the bin midpoint $p_0 = 1/2$. By the chain rule for adaptive $Q$-query transcripts $Y^{1:Q}$ and Le~Cam's two-point method, any estimator $\hat{w}(Y^{1:Q})$ achieving $\sup_w \mathbb{P}_w(|\hat{w} - w| \ge \delta w/2) \le p_{\mathrm{err}} < 1/2$ requires:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:lecam_dither}
  D_{\mathrm{KL}}\bigl(P_{w_0}^{1:Q} \,\|\, P_{w_1}^{1:Q}\bigr) \le \frac{16}{3} Q \left(\frac{\delta w}{\Delta_u}\right)^2,\qquad
  Q \ge \frac{3(1 - 2p_{\mathrm{err}})^2}{8}\left(\frac{\Delta_u}{\delta w}\right)^2 = \Omega\left(\left(\frac{\Delta_u}{\delta w}\right)^2\right),
  \end{equation}
  inflating adaptive extraction query complexity from $O(\log(\Delta_u/\delta w))$ to $\Omega((\Delta_u/\delta w)^2)$ (e.g., at $u=9$ \texttt{E8M0}, $\Delta_u = 128, \delta w = 2$, and target divergence $D_{\mathrm{KL}}^{\mathrm{tot}} \ge 1/4$, i.e., $(1-2p_{\mathrm{err}})^2 = 1/8$: $Q_{\mathrm{det}} = 6$ vs.\ $Q \ge 192$ under the worst-case $[1/4,3/4]$ constant $3/8$ and $Q \ge (1 - 2p_{\mathrm{err}})^2(\Delta_u/\delta w)^2 = 512$ at the bin midpoint $p_0 = 1/2$).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:dither_lower_bound}]
Under deterministic rounding, bisecting $[0, \Delta_u)$ via $\xi^{(q)}$ halves the uncertainty interval per query, taking $\lceil \log_2(\Delta_u/\delta w) \rceil$ steps. Under unsigned uniform dither $\eta^{(q)} \sim \mathrm{Unif}[0, \Delta_u)$ applied prior to floor truncation $\mathcal{Q}_{c_i}(z) = \Delta_u \lfloor z/\Delta_u \rfloor$, $Y^{(q)} \in \{k\Delta_u, (k+1)\Delta_u\}$ is an unbiased Bernoulli trial with parameter $p(w) = \{ (w + \xi^{(q)})/\Delta_u \} \in [1/4, 3/4]$ and $\mathbb{E}[Y^{(q)} \mid w, \xi^{(q)}] = k\Delta_u(1 - p(w)) + (k+1)\Delta_u p(w) = w + \xi^{(q)}$, where $\min_{p \in [1/4,3/4]} p(1-p) = 3/16$. The chi-square bound on KL divergence gives:
\[
D_{\mathrm{KL}}\bigl(\mathrm{Bern}(p_0)\,\|\,\mathrm{Bern}(p_1)\bigr) \le \chi^2\bigl(\mathrm{Bern}(p_0)\,\|\,\mathrm{Bern}(p_1)\bigr)
= \frac{(p_0 - p_1)^2}{p_1(1 - p_1)} \le \frac{16}{3}\left(\frac{\delta w}{\Delta_u}\right)^2,
\]
and $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{Bern}(p_0)\,\|\,\mathrm{Bern}(p_1)) \approx \frac{(p_0 - p_1)^2}{2 p_0(1 - p_0)} = 2(\delta w/\Delta_u)^2$ at the midpoint $p_0 = 1/2$. By adaptive KL tensorization:
\[
D_{\mathrm{KL}}\bigl(P_{w_0}^{1:Q} \,\|\, P_{w_1}^{1:Q}\bigr) = \sum_{q=1}^Q \mathbb{E}_{P_{w_0}}\Bigl[D_{\mathrm{KL}}\bigl(P_{w_0}^{(q)}(\cdot \mid Y^{1:q-1}) \;\big\|\; P_{w_1}^{(q)}(\cdot \mid Y^{1:q-1})\bigr)\Bigr],
\]
and applying Le~Cam's minimax two-point bound with Pinsker's inequality:
\[
1 - 2p_{\mathrm{err}} \le \mathrm{TV}\bigl(P_{w_0}^{1:Q}, P_{w_1}^{1:Q}\bigr) \le \sqrt{\frac{1}{2} D_{\mathrm{KL}}\bigl(P_{w_0}^{1:Q} \,\|\, P_{w_1}^{1:Q}\bigr)}
\]
yields $(1 - 2p_{\mathrm{err}})^2 \le \frac{8}{3} Q (\delta w/\Delta_u)^2$ globally on $[1/4,3/4]$ (and $(1 - 2p_{\mathrm{err}})^2 \le Q (\delta w/\Delta_u)^2$ at $p_0 = 1/2$), establishing Eq.~\eqref{eq:lecam_dither}.
\end{proof}
\MidConnesRule

\textbf{So What? (Architectural Consequence).}\; Traditional memory protection is binary (all-or-nothing page mapping) and vulnerable to both power side channels and adaptive boundary-search weight extraction. Figure~\ref{fig:tvla_dither} shows that fracturing Stage~2's existing \texttt{LUT2} gates into 5-shared-input \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} cells enforces hardware rate-distortion clamping ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits) at $+0$ CLB LUTs, while counter-seeded $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}$ dither inflates adaptive weight extraction from $Q_{\mathrm{det}} = 6$ to $Q \ge 192$--$512$ reads and 2-share DOM \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} masking flattens differential power leakage to $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$ across 19,990 live silicon traces.}
\label{fig:tvla_dither}
\end{minipage}
\EndConnesPlate

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch~---~Rate-Distortion Deterrence \& Dual-Forest Resilience vs.\ Semantic Encryption \& Multi-Cut Faults).}\;Two exact structural boundaries govern Section~\ref{sec:forest}. First, for non-zero shared-weight inference tiers $u \in \{9,12\}$, Stage~2's fractured \texttt{LUT6\_2} mantissa clamp paired with counter-seeded $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}$ dither enforces an information-theoretic bandwidth-amplification barrier ($\Omega((\Delta_u/\delta w)^2)$, inflating weight localization from $Q_{\mathrm{det}}=6$ to $Q \ge 192\text{--}512$ CXL reads) rather than full cryptographic semantic security: an adversary permitted to issue millions of unthrottled read queries to the same weight address could still asymptotically average out the dither unless Stage~2 is paired with control-plane query-budget rotation. Second, while PHY-gated dual-forest failover $(T_0,T_1)$ absorbs any single link cut in $<4.0\text{~ns}$ via \texttt{pcs\_link\_up\_i}, simultaneous multi-link cuts that disconnect both $T_0$ and $T_1$ fail closed until \texttt{spectralc} stages a fresh certified spanning forest.

\section{Cut-Through Flit Sanitization, Renewal-Reward Goodput, and Tri-Mode Coding}
\label{sec:pipeline}

\subsection{Go-Back-N Renewal-Reward Goodput vs.\ Locked-Epoch Drain Scheduling}
\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; A 512-bit CXL~3.0--4.0 flit phase co-packs $m=4$ independent 128-bit slots from different tenants or threads. Stateless link firewalls that abort an unauthorized access by inverting the tail Flit CRC force the receiver to discard the entire 512-bit flit and trigger a link-wide Go-Back-N NAK replay of all $N_{\mathrm{pipe}} = 32$ in-flight flits. Consequently, an unauthorized slot rate of just $p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.8\%$ poisons $1 - 0.992^4 = 3.16\%$ of all flits and collapses link goodput to $48.90\%$. Surgical Stage-2 replacement of only the offending 128-bit slot with a valid \texttt{CXL\_NOP} (or zeroed \texttt{S2M\_DRS}/\texttt{M2S\_Req} scrub target $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{scrub}}$) followed by clean Stage-3 CRC regeneration eliminates link replays entirely ($N_{\mathrm{replay}} \equiv 0$), preserving $100\%$ of co-packed innocent slots and achieving work-conserving $\eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{slot}} = 1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 99.20\%$ goodput.

\begin{theorem}[Go-Back-N Renewal-Reward Goodput Collapse and Monotone Locked-Epoch Utilization Bound]
\label{thm:goodput}
Consider a \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} link packing $m = 4$ slots per flit phase with Go-Back-N replay pipe depth $N_{\mathrm{pipe}} \ge 1$ flits under unauthorized slot rate $p_{\mathrm{bad}} \in [0, 1)$.
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Whole-Flit CRC Poisoning Collapse vs.\ Surgical Per-Slot Work Conservation:} Whole-flit CRC poisoning aborts any flit with $\ge 1$ bad slot ($q_{\mathrm{flit}} = 1 - (1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}})^m$). Under fresh-flit replacement where each NAK expends $N_{\mathrm{pipe}}$ cycles, renewal-reward theory gives:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:goodput}
  \eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{CRC}}(p_{\mathrm{bad}}) = \frac{(1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}})^m}{1 + (N_{\mathrm{pipe}} - 1)\bigl(1 - (1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}})^m\bigr)},\qquad
  \eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{slot}}(p_{\mathrm{bad}}) = 1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}},
  \end{equation}
  so at $m = 4, N_{\mathrm{pipe}} = 32, p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.8\%$, legacy goodput collapses to $\eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{CRC}}(0.008) = 48.90\%$, whereas \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} maintains $\eta_{\mathrm{goodput}}^{\mathrm{slot}}(0.008) = 99.20\%$ ($N_{\mathrm{replay}} \equiv 0$).
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Monotone Locked-Epoch Reconfiguration Bound~\cite{azar2009buffer,azar2013metric,azar2026lossless}:} For colored packet streams $\sigma$ with deadlines and minimum laxity $L = \min_i\{d_i - r_i\}$~\cite{azar2009buffer} along a monotone epoch schedule $G_{\mathrm{active}}$ with metric-space bank transition drain cost $1 \le \mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) \le \frac{\delta_{\max}^2}{9} L$~\cite{azar2013metric}, lossless batching of epoch transitions at $K = \lceil \sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) L} \rceil$ cycles~\cite{azar2026lossless} guarantees:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:azar_vardi}
  \mathrm{ALG}(\sigma) \ge \left(1 - \frac{3\sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}})}}{\sqrt{L}}\right) \mathrm{OPT}(\sigma)
  \ge (1 - \delta_{\max})\mathrm{OPT}(\sigma) > 0.
  \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:goodput}]
By the renewal-reward theorem, $\mathbb{E}[R_{\mathrm{cyc}}] / (m\,\mathbb{E}[T_{\mathrm{cyc}}]) = (1 - q_{\mathrm{flit}}) / [1 + (N_{\mathrm{pipe}} - 1)q_{\mathrm{flit}}]$, yielding $0.992^4 / [1 + 31(1 - 0.992^4)] = 0.4890424$ vs.\ $1 - p_{\mathrm{bad}} = 0.9920$. For Claim~(ii), setting $x = \sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) L} \ge 1$ gives $x \le K < 2x$, so colored-packet deadline rounding~\cite{azar2009buffer} forfeits $\le K/L \le 2\sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}})/L}$ and metric-space epoch draining~\cite{azar2013metric,azar2026lossless} expends $\le \mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}})/K \le \sqrt{\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}})/L}$.
\end{proof}

\subsection{Tri-Mode Deployment and Dual-Subflit Split-Coset Tunneling (\texorpdfstring{\texttt{LOpt} CRC48 + Tail CRC64}{LOpt CRC48 + Tail CRC64})}
\label{sec:trimode}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; On CXL~3.0--4.0 links with 96-bit IDE AES-GCM enabled, every payload slot is fully occupied, leaving zero free payload bits for capability tags. However, a 256-byte CXL flit carries an 8-byte Tail CRC64 (Bytes~242--249) protected by three 3-way interleaved Reed--Solomon RS-FEC parity blocks (Bytes~250--255), plus a 6-byte Sub-Flit~0 CRC48 (Bytes~122--127) in \texttt{LOpt} low-latency mode. By injecting encrypted cohort descriptors into an $[8,4,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ Reed--Solomon coset of Tail CRC64 (and a $[6,2,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ coset of Sub-Flit~0 CRC48) while pre-folding the induced parity differential through $\tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)}$ into Bytes~250--255, Theorems~\ref{thm:modeb} and~\ref{thm:split_coset} tunnel up to 6 cohort descriptors per flit at $0\%$ slot-capacity tax, $0\%$ outer RS-FEC margin loss, $8.0\text{~ns}$ Phase-1 cut-through recovery, and $d_{\mathrm{super}} = 5$ miscorrection detection.

\begin{theorem}[Inline Cut-Through Clamping, Modulo-3 $\mathrm{GF}(2^8)$ Differential Synthesis, \& Mode B.1/B.2 DLP-Synchronized Coset Tagging]
\label{thm:modeb}
Let an inline Mode-B interposer stream the 4~phases of a 256-byte flit $F = [M \parallel C \parallel E] \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^{256}$ (preserving Slot~0 header bytes $0,\ldots,15$ and DLP bytes $236,\ldots,241$) while injecting $\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{out}} = G_{\mathrm{tag}}\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{rat}} \oplus \mathbf{s}_{\mathrm{seq}} \in \mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{tag}} \oplus \mathbf{s}_{\mathrm{seq}} \subset \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^8$ for an $[8,4,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ Reed--Solomon coset subcode $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{tag}}$ ($H_{\mathrm{tag}}G_{\mathrm{tag}} = \mathbf{0}, G_{\mathrm{tag}}^+ G_{\mathrm{tag}} = I_4$). Using a 1-logic-level (\mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}}/\texttt{LUT2}), zero-BRAM combinational header recognizer $\Phi_{\mathrm{hdr}}$ (a hardware-layer analogue of software separation-logic framing used in formally verified CBOR/CDDL binary message parsers~\cite{ramananandro2025secure}) to gate $\Delta M_{\mathrm{data}} = \Delta M_{\mathrm{raw}} \wedge \overline{M_{\mathrm{hdr}}}$ and pre-composing the folded generator matrix
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:h_tilde_def}
\tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)} = H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)} \mathbb{I}(i \equiv m \bmod 3)
\oplus \bigoplus_{\substack{j=0 \\ (242+j) \equiv m \pmod 3}}^7 H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(242+j)} H_{\mathrm{CRC},j}^{(i)}
\end{equation}
over the 220 non-DLP byte differentials $\delta_i := \Delta M_{\mathrm{data}}[i] \oplus \Delta C_{i-122}^{\mathrm{sub0}} \mathbb{I}(122 \le i \le 127) \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)$ ($i \in \{16,\ldots,235\}$):
\begin{align}
\Delta C_j^{\mathrm{total}} &= \left(\bigoplus_{i=16}^{235} H_{\mathrm{CRC},j}^{(i)} \cdot \delta_i\right) \oplus \tau_{\mathrm{out},j}, \qquad j \in \{0,\ldots,7\}, \label{eq:delta_crc}\\
\Delta E_m^{\mathrm{total}} &= \left(\bigoplus_{i=16}^{235} \tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)} \cdot \delta_i\right)
\oplus \left(\bigoplus_{\substack{j=0 \\ (242+j) \equiv m \pmod 3}}^7 H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(242+j)} \cdot \tau_{\mathrm{out},j}\right), \qquad m \in \{0,1,2\}, \label{eq:delta_fec}
\end{align}
and XORing $\Delta C^{\mathrm{total}}$ into Bytes~242--249 and $\Delta E^{\mathrm{total}}$ into Bytes~250--255 guarantees: (i)~$(\Delta M_{\mathrm{data}} \wedge M_{\mathrm{hdr}}) \equiv \mathbf{0}$ preserves all routing headers and DLP bytes; (ii)~$S_{\mathrm{FEC},m}(F') = \mathbf{0} \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^2$ for all $m \in \{0,1,2\}$ ($0\%$ FEC margin loss); and (iii)~the receiver evaluates $S = S_{\mathrm{CRC}}(\hat{M},\hat{C}) \oplus \mathbf{s}_{\mathrm{seq}}$, checks $H_{\mathrm{tag}} S = \mathbf{0}$ ($d_{\mathrm{super}} = 5$, rejecting $100\%$ of weight-3 outer RS-FEC miscorrections; $2^{-32}$ burst bound under binary CRC-64), and recovers $\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{rat}} = G_{\mathrm{tag}}^+ S$ in 1~cycle.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:modeb}]
By $\mathbb{F}_2$-linearity of polynomial division over $\mathrm{GF}(2^8)$ and affine preset cancellation $c_0 \oplus c_0 = \mathbf{0}$ (Lemma~\ref{lem:folding}):
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:crc_cancel}
S_{\mathrm{CRC}}(M', C') = \left(\mathrm{CRC}(M) \oplus \bigoplus_{i=16}^{235} H_{\mathrm{CRC}}^{(i)} \cdot \delta_i\right)
\oplus \left(C \oplus \bigoplus_{i=16}^{235} H_{\mathrm{CRC}}^{(i)} \cdot \delta_i \oplus \boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{out}}\right) = \boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{out}}.
\end{equation}
Lemmas~\ref{lem:folding} and~\ref{lem:bch} yield $S_{\mathrm{FEC},m}(F') = \mathbf{0}$ and $H_{\mathrm{tag}} H_{\mathrm{CRC}} E_{\mathrm{mis}} \neq \mathbf{0}$ for all $1 \le \mathrm{wt}(E_{\mathrm{mis}}) \le 4$.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Dual-Subflit Concatenated Split-Coset Tunneling (\texttt{LOpt} CRC48 + Tail CRC64) for Mode B.2]
\label{thm:split_coset}
In 256-byte \texttt{LOpt} flit mode ($\texttt{LOpt\_Mode\_En} == 1$), instantiating a $[6,2,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ Reed--Solomon coset subcode $\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{out}}^{(0)} = G_{\mathrm{tag}}^{(0)}\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{rat}}^{(0)} \oplus \mathbf{s}_{\mathrm{seq}}^{(0)} \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^6$ in Sub-Flit~0 CRC48 (Bytes~122--127, carrying $\mathrm{cid}_1 \parallel \mathrm{cid}_0$) alongside the $[8,4,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ coset $\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{out}}^{(1)}$ in Tail CRC64 (Bytes~242--249, carrying $\mathrm{cid}_5 \parallel \cdots \parallel \mathrm{cid}_2$) by setting at $i = 122 + r$ ($r \in \{0,\ldots,5\}$) in Eqs.~\eqref{eq:delta_crc}--\eqref{eq:delta_fec}:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:split_coset_delta}
\delta_{122+r} := \Delta M_{\mathrm{data}}[122+r] \oplus \Delta C_r^{\mathrm{sub0}} \oplus \tau_{\mathrm{out},r}^{(0)} \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8),
\end{equation}
guarantees: (i)~Phase-1 recovery of $(\mathrm{cid}_1, \mathrm{cid}_0)$ in 2~phases ($8.0\text{~ns}$ at 250~MHz) and $+50\%$ zero-slot-tax capacity (6~\texttt{cid}s/flit); and (ii)~exact compensation of $\boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{out}}^{(0)}$ in Tail CRC64 and RS-FEC parity so $S_{\mathrm{FEC},m}(F') \equiv \mathbf{0}$ and $d_{\mathrm{super}}^{(0)} = d_{\mathrm{super}}^{(1)} = 5$.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:split_coset}]
Substituting $\delta_{122+r} = \Delta C_r^{\mathrm{sub0}} \oplus \tau_{\mathrm{out},r}^{(0)}$ into Eqs.~\eqref{eq:delta_crc}--\eqref{eq:delta_fec} cancels $\tau_{\mathrm{out},r}^{(0)}$ in $S_{\mathrm{CRC64}}(\hat{M},\hat{C}) = \boldsymbol{\tau}_{\mathrm{out}}^{(1)}$ and folds $\delta_{122+r}$ through $\tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(122+r)}$ to preserve $S_{\mathrm{FEC},m}(F') = \mathbf{0}$, while $H_{\mathrm{tag}}^{(0)}$ checks 4 consecutive roots of $\mathrm{CRC48}$ ($d_{\mathrm{super}}^{(0)} = 5$).
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\StartTwoCol
\subsection{Cut-Through Pipeline, Square-Root-of-Rotation Diffusion, and 16-CSR \texorpdfstring{\texttt{BAR0}}{BAR0} ABI}
\textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} executes across a 2-stage core (\id{cxl_subflit_slot_sanitizer_core}) preceded by Cycle~0 and wrapped in a 4-stage enclave (\id{cxl_subflit_sanitizer_enclave_top}, Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline}; normative assertions in Appendix~\ref{app:sva}, Listing~\ref{fig:sva}):
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Cycle 0 \& Stage 1 Sieve (\StageOneSieveDelayNs\ on FPGA / \AsicStageOneTwoDelayPs\ in 7nm ASAP7):} Cycle~0 selects bank $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$, evaluates $\Phi_{\mathbb{F}_8}(\mathrm{cid}_i)$, the 1-hot time-wheel vector $\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_i$, or $(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}) \wedge (\Lambda_{b_i}^{(t)}(\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i}) \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}})$, strips $\mathrm{HPA}_i[12+2n-1 : 12+2n-(c_{\mathrm{bits}}+g)] \leftarrow \mathbf{0}$, and latches \texttt{slot\_coarse\_arena\_ok\_i[i]}. Stage~1's 256 pin-locked \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} cells evaluate $\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i)$ (\texttt{O5}) and $\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{eff}}}(r_i)$ (\texttt{O6}) alongside the \texttt{CSR 0x70/0x78} SPID check and 2-bit MOESI/precision tier ceiling $c_i[1:0]$, latching the \CoreStageOneFFs\ Stage-1 FFs.
  \item \textbf{Stage 2 Multiplexing (\StageTwoCriticalPathNs: \StageTwoLogicDelayNs\ logic + \StageTwoRouteDelayNs\ route), Fractured \texttt{LUT6\_2} MOESI \& Mantissa Clamps, \& Group-Ring Diffusion:} Across \CoreStageTwoFFs\ Stage-2 endpoints (\CoreTotalFFs\ total FFs, with 8-way replicated \texttt{s1\_slot\_viol\_rep\_q[7:0]} per slot to bound fanout to 16 loads/FF and trap single-FF SEU disparity), Stage~2 zeroes unauthorized payload lanes, updates $\mathrm{Slot}_0[15:4]$'s \texttt{H0..H5} table, clamps \texttt{CXL.cache} MOESI states via 4 fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} sites ($\texttt{INIT = 64'hCC00CC00\_A2E0A2E0}$), clamps \texttt{S2M\_DRS} floating-point mantissa lanes across 4 precision tiers ($u \in \{0,9,12,16\}$ bits) via 252 fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} sites (Eq.~\eqref{eq:graduated_init}, $+0$ CLB LUTs), and preserves the 22-bit \texttt{Opcode}/\texttt{Tag\_ID} slice on $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{scrub}}$ and \texttt{S2M\_DRS}/\texttt{S2M\_NDR} headers. For the 3-tap circulant operator $\mathcal{L}_a(m) := m \oplus \mathrm{RotL}_a(m) \oplus \mathrm{RotL}_{32}(m)$ in $\mathbb{F}_2[C_{64}] \cong \mathbb{F}_2[z]/(z+1)^{64}$ (odd $a \in \{1,\ldots,31\}$), $(1 + z^a + z^{32})^2 = 1 + z^{2a} + z^{64} \equiv z^{2a} \pmod{z^{64}+1}$ yields $\mathcal{L}_a(\mathcal{L}_a(m)) \equiv \mathrm{RotL}_{2a}(m)$ with 1-step inverse $\mathcal{L}_a^{-1}(y) = \mathrm{RotR}_{2a}(\mathcal{L}_a(y))$ (\texttt{thm10}, \texttt{thm23}).
  \item \textbf{Stages 3--4 --- Coset Framing, Per-\texttt{ARID} Scrub Shim, \& \texttt{WSTRB} Gating:} Stage~3 computes $\mathrm{CRC}_{64}(F_{\mathrm{san}})$ and Stage~4 applies the Hamming-distance isometry $\Phi_\psi(c) = c \oplus \psi_{\mathrm{mask}}$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:isometry}
  d_H\bigl(\Phi_\psi(x), \Phi_\psi(y)\bigr) = \mathrm{wt}\bigl((x \oplus \psi) \oplus (y \oplus \psi)\bigr) = d_H(x,y),
  \end{equation}
  aligning in Cycle~4 with \id{m_hbm_wstrb_c4_o}$[16i{+}15:16i] = \texttt{16'h0000}$, the Per-\texttt{ARID} Scrub Shim, PHY-gated $(T_0,T_1)$ chord failover, and Stage-4 credit release \id{m_pcie_crd_pulse_c4_o}$[i]$.
\end{enumerate}

\noindent\textbf{16-CSR (128-Byte) \texttt{BAR0} ABI (\texttt{0x00}--\texttt{0x78}):}
Includes atomic $2\times 32\text{-bit}$ write staging and tear-free 64-bit read shadowing across: \texttt{0x00/0x08} ($p_{s,\mathrm{coarse}}, p_{t,\mathrm{coarse}}$), \texttt{0x10/0x18} (inactive-bank 128b key portals / dual-forest chord masks), \texttt{0x20} (epoch control, \texttt{bank\_valid[1:0]}, Bellman popcount status, Vieta degree $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{[0:1]}[3:0]$ + lockout bit, and Stealth \texttt{128'h0} vs.\ CXL \texttt{Poison} fault-mode bit \texttt{CSR~0x20[13]}), \texttt{0x28/0x30} (ingress/egress credit counters), \texttt{0x38} (sanitized slot counter), \texttt{0x40} (self-NAK counter $\equiv 0$), \texttt{0x48} (cold-POR tamper trap counter), \texttt{0x50} (32-slot \texttt{M2S\_RwD} FIFO status), \texttt{0x58} (canonical egress digest $\mathcal{N}(\Sigma,\pi)$ / goal $\Sigma$), \texttt{0x60/0x68} (dual-banked $p_{s,\mathrm{fine}}^{[0:1]}$ or Vieta $\boldsymbol{\sigma}^{[0:1]}$, plus 64-bucket time-wheel/temporal mask $p_{t,\mathrm{fine}}$ in \texttt{0x68}), \texttt{0x70} (64-tenant live SPID bitmap), and \texttt{0x78} (write-once SPID prefix, 2-bit fine-cohort submode $\texttt{CSR~0x78[51:50]} \in \{\texttt{2'b00}, \texttt{2'b01}, \texttt{2'b10}, \texttt{2'b11}\}$, MOESI/mantissa tier config, Bellman threshold $u^*$, and 4-channel OOO DMA burst portal).

Synthesized, placed, and routed in AMD Vivado~\VivadoToolVersion\ on \texttt{xcvu47p-fsvh2892-2L-e}, the 2-stage core (\id{cxl_subflit_slot_sanitizer_core}) packs \CoreLogicalLUTs\ logical LUTs (\CoreStageOneLUTSixTwo\ Stage-1 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} sites, \CoreStageTwoLUTTwo\ Stage-2 \texttt{LUT2}/\mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} gates, and \CoreControlFIFOAndDualRailLUTs\ control/FIFO LUTs) into \CorePhysicalLUTs\ physical CLB LUTs (\CoreLUTUtilPct, \CoreOccupiedCLBs\ CLBs) and \CoreTotalFFs\ FDCE FFs, closing at \PostRouteSetupWNS\ setup WNS (\StageTwoCriticalPathNs\ datapath = \StageTwoLogicDelayNs\ logic + \StageTwoRouteDelayNs\ route, $T_{\min} = \CoreTminNs \implies F_{\max} = \PostRouteFmaxMHz$, \PostRouteHoldWHS\ hold WHS, \PostRouteDRCViolations\ DRC errors, \PostRouteDynPowerMW\ dynamic power; Tables~\ref{tab:comparison}--\ref{tab:vivado} and Figure~\ref{fig:silicon_ppa}). Because normalized CPI/FDI links (Mode~A) and IDE split-coset links (Mode~B.2) deliver pre-decoded capability vectors, only the \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT Stage-1/2 core (plus \ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs\ LUTs and \ScrubShimFFs\ FFs at $+2.099\text{~ns}$ WNS for the Per-\texttt{ARID} scrub shim) sits on the universal datapath; the \PreCoreModeCLUTs-LUT (\CycleZeroFfs-FF) Cycle-0 unsealer is instantiated only on raw Mode-C or Vieta/Steiner tensor ports, and even the maximal 4-stage enclave top (\WrapperEnclaveLUTs\ LUTs, \WrapperEnclaveFFs\ FFs) occupies just \WrapperLUTUtilPct\ of \texttt{xcvu47p} with \CoreBRAM\ BRAM. In the open-source 7nm FinFET ASAP7 predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7}, the standalone 2-stage core occupies \AsicCoreCells\ standard cells (\AsicCoreGe\ NAND2 GE, \AsicCoreAreaMm, $0$ SRAM), while the placed-and-routed full 4-stage enclave (\id{cxl_subflit_sanitizer_enclave_top}) closes at \AsicStandardCells\ routed standard cells (\AsicEnclaveAreaUm\ = \AsicEnclaveAreaMm\ inside a $180.0 \times 180.0\text{~}\mu\text{m}$ 1,460-pin ring, $0$ TritonRoute DRC violations, \AsicSetupWnsPs\ setup WNS, \AsicStageOneTwoDelayPs\ critical path $\implies \AsicFmaxMhz$ $F_{\max}$, \AsicTotalPowerMw\ total power; \id{reports_public/TRL6_ASIC_7NM_ASAP7_SIGNOFF.json}). Because \mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0}~\cite{cxl2025spec} and \mbox{UCIe~1.1--3.0}~\cite{ucie2025spec30} hard-IP PHY/Link controllers terminate PAM-4/NRZ serialization (including 64~GT/s UCIe~3.0 raw/streaming flit framing and fast emergency sideband failover signaling onto $p_{\mathrm{phy}} = \texttt{pcs\_link\_up\_i}$ for $(T_0,T_1)$ dual-forest switching in \texttt{thm28}) upstream of the 512-bit CPI/FDI bus, live 16nm silicon execution inside an account-locked private AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} enclave (public \texttt{loadPermission} disabled) directly exercises this exact post-PHY 512-bit @ $250\text{~MHz}$ ($128\text{~Gbps}$) flit interface across both Baseline BIST/TVLA AFI \texttt{\AwsAgfiId} (\texttt{afi-07282de69eecfd279}, $+1.842\text{~ns}$ WNS, \AwsBistSlots{} sub-flit slots tested, 400,000 rogue attacks scrubbed, $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$ across 19,990 Welch TVLA traces) and Production 16~GB HBM2 DMA AFI \texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId} (\texttt{afi-0f2ddd7dc87d81193}, $+0.512\text{~ns}$ WNS, \AwsLiveBursts{} 512-bit bursts / 50,128 HBM flits / \AwsLiveSlots{} inline sub-flit slots), achieving zero self-inflicted link NAKs (\texttt{CSR~0x40 == 0}), zero PCIe CTOs, $100\%$ allied HBM2 writes (\texttt{0x5555555555555555}), and $100\%$ HBM2 sentinel preservation (\texttt{0xAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA}; \id{reports_public/TRL7_AWS_F2_HBM2_DMA_RECEIPT.json}).
Six protocol corner cases are closed in hardware:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Multi-Slot \texttt{M2S\_RwD} Rollovers via Non-Destructive 32-Slot Shift FIFO:} Stage~1 evaluates per-slot violation (where $|\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{coarse}}}(r_i)| := (\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{coarse}}}(r_i) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64})$ denotes 64-bit reduction-OR) as shown in Eq.~\eqref{eq:viol_full} below. Each \texttt{M2S\_RwD} header pushes 4 copies of $\neg\mathrm{hdr\_cap\_viol}[i]$ into the 32-slot shift FIFO $Q_{\mathrm{RwD}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{32}$ ($C_{\mathrm{RwD,max}} = 8$ credits). On $\texttt{fifo\_overflow\_w}$ ($\texttt{rem\_pending\_w} + \texttt{pushed\_slots\_w} > 32$), hardware executes a \textbf{non-destructive overflow clamp}, preserving \id{rwd_slot_auth_fifo_q} $\Leftarrow$ \id{fifo_after_pop_w} so already-admitted innocent write beats complete $100\%$ intact while rejecting only the overflowing incoming headers.
  \item \textbf{Starvation-Free CDC:} Active masks update from 2-FF synchronized \texttt{BAR0} shadow registers at each 256B flit boundary (\texttt{s\_flit\_valid\_i \&\& flit\_phase\_id\_i == 2'b00}).
  \item \textbf{Temporal Cache-Coherency Closure (\texttt{S2M\_BISnp} + Snoop-Solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} vs.\ \texttt{MemWr} Separation):} When a cohort is revoked in \texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x60}, a control-plane \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} (\texttt{SnpInv}/\texttt{SnpCleanInv}) walker flushes remote L1/L2/LLC caches to bound post-revocation SRAM residency. In Stage~1 (Eq.~\eqref{eq:viol_full}), hardware distinguishes snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} writebacks ($\texttt{slot\_is\_snoop\_evict\_i}[i] = 1$, validated against an outstanding Home-Agent snoop tag) and \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp} completions ($\texttt{slot\_is\_bi\_rsp\_i}[i] = 1$) from unsolicited host-initiated \texttt{M2S\_RwD} \texttt{MemWr} commands, allowing legitimate pre-revocation dirty state to drain cleanly ($\mathrm{viol}[i] = 0$) while blocking post-revocation \texttt{MemWr} writes (\texttt{thm07}, \texttt{thm22}, \id{thm29_snoop_dirty_evict_and_macaroon_attenuation}, Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2}, Row~20).
  \item \textbf{Pin-Locked OOC SLR0 Floorplanning:} Enforces \texttt{CONTAIN\_ROUTING 1}, \texttt{LOCK\_PINS}, and \texttt{DONT\_TOUCH}.
  \item \textbf{SEU Resilience \& 2-Share DOM Glitch Immunity (Mode D):} Using \CoreBRAM\ BRAM and \CoreLUTRAM\ LUTRAM~\cite{koch2021fabulous} with 8-way replicated violation FFs traps single-FF upsets; Mode~D adds 2-share DOM~\cite{gross2016dom} \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} masking (\texttt{thm24}), validated in hardware via 19,990-trace ISO/IEC~17825 Welch's $t$-test (\texttt{TVLA} $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$) on live AWS F2 silicon (\texttt{\AwsAgfiId}).
  \item \textbf{Per-\texttt{ARID} Ordered Soft-Logic AXI4 Read-Scrub Responder Shim \& Selectable CXL \texttt{Poison} Mode:} Forces $\texttt{m\_hbm\_axi\_arvalid\_o} = \texttt{1'b0}$ on $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{scrub}} = \mathbf{0}_{46}$, waits until $\id{arid_hbm_inflight_cnt_q}[\texttt{ARID}[3:0]] == 0$ before returning synthetic zeroed completions (\texttt{512'h0}), and toggles via \texttt{CSR~0x20[13]} between Stealth Scrub Mode (\texttt{128'h0}) and Architectural Fault Mode (setting CXL \texttt{Poison} / \texttt{MemData\_Invalid} for clean application \texttt{SIGBUS} recovery).
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{So What? (Architectural Consequence).}\; Security enclaves that rely on multi-megabit on-die SRAM permission caches or complex tag-walking state machines cannot fit inside area- and power-constrained HBM3e/HBM4 base dies or UCIe chiplet shims. Figure~\ref{fig:silicon_ppa} demonstrates that the entire 2-stage sanitizer core occupies just \CorePhysicalLUTs\ CLB LUTs (\CoreLUTUtilPct\ of \texttt{xcvu47p}, \PostRouteDynPowerMW) in 16nm FPGA and \AsicCoreCells\ standard cells (\AsicCoreAreaMm, \AsicTotalPowerMw\ for the full \AsicStandardCells-cell 4-stage enclave at \AsicFmaxMhz) in 7nm ASAP7 CMOS with \CoreBRAM\ BRAM---small enough to replicate across every HBM pseudo-channel at negligible silicon cost.}
\label{fig:silicon_ppa}
