\MidConnesRule
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Revoking a cohort in \texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x60} blocks new forward \texttt{M2S\_Req}/\texttt{M2S\_RwD} requests in 1~clock cycle ($4.0\text{~ns}$), but a remote host may still hold pre-revocation cachelines in its local L1/L2/LLC hierarchy. If the firewall blindly blocks returning \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp} snoop responses or snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} writebacks while the Home Agent flushes those lines via \texttt{S2M\_BISnp}, the CXL fabric deadlocks immediately. Equation~\eqref{eq:viol_full} and Proposition~\ref{prop:bisnp_shootdown} prove that explicitly exempting snoop responses and authenticated snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} drains ($\mathrm{birsp}_i \vee \mathrm{snoop\_evict}_i = 1$) while blocking unsolicited host \texttt{MemWr} requests makes the channel wait-for graph strictly acyclic ($\beta_1(\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{wait}}) = 0$) and bounds post-revocation cache residency to $\le 248$--$500\text{~ns}$.

\noindent\textbf{Stage-1 Per-Slot Violation Equation and Deterministic \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} Shootdown Horizon:}
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:viol_full}
\begin{aligned}
\mathrm{spid\_ok}_i &:= (\mathrm{SPID}_i \neq \mathbf{0}_{14}) \wedge (\mathrm{SPID}_i[13:6] == \texttt{pfx})
\wedge \texttt{CSR\_0x70}[\mathrm{SPID}_i[5:0]] \wedge \texttt{bank\_valid\_q}[b_i],\\
\mathrm{hdr\_chk}_i &:= \mathrm{hdr}_i \wedge \neg\mathrm{birsp}_i \wedge \neg\mathrm{snoop\_evict}_i,\\
\mathrm{viol}[i] &= \Bigl(\mathrm{hdr\_chk}_i \wedge \bigl((r_i == \mathbf{0}_{64}) \vee \neg\mathrm{spid\_ok}_i
\vee (\mathrm{fine}_i \ ? \ (\neg\mathrm{coarse\_ok}_i \vee \bigl(\Pi_{P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[b_i]}}(r_i) == \mathbf{0}_{64}\bigr)) : |\Delta_{P_{\mathrm{coarse}}}(r_i)|)\bigr)\Bigr)\\
&\quad\vee (\mathrm{rwd\_dat}_i \wedge \neg\mathrm{rwd\_ok}_i) \vee (\mathrm{drs\_dat}_i \wedge \mathrm{drs\_viol}_i).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}

\begin{proposition}[Deterministic Post-Revocation Cache Residency \& Deadlock-Free \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} Shootdown Horizon]
\label{prop:bisnp_shootdown}
Suppose a fine cohort $\alpha$ is revoked in \texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x60} at time $t_0$ (blocking new forward \texttt{M2S\_Req}/\texttt{M2S\_RwD} requests in 1~cycle $= 4.0\text{~ns}$ at $250\text{~MHz}$), while an adjunct Home-Agent invalidation walker (reusing the CXL~3.0 Home Agent's standard architectural snoop-filter directory---mandated for any coherent \texttt{CXL.cache}/\texttt{S2M\_BISnp} memory controller independently of security, adding zero security directory SRAM to the \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT datapath core) drains $K_{\mathrm{dir}} \ge 1$ snoop-directory entries (of which $0 \le K_{\mathrm{dirty}} \le K_{\mathrm{dir}}$ reside in Modified $\mathrm{M}$ state requiring 5-slot \texttt{DirtyEvict} writebacks: 1~\texttt{M2S\_RwD} header $+$ 4~data slots at $R_{\mathrm{wb}} = 4/5 = 0.8\text{~lines/cycle}$) by issuing credit-paced \texttt{S2M\_BISnp(SnpInv)} probes at rate $R_{\mathrm{snp}} \in \{1,2,4\}$ slots/cycle over a link with round-trip time $\mathrm{RTT}_{\mathrm{CXL}}$ and host snoop-filter lookup latency $T_{\mathrm{snp\_lookup}}$. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Deterministic Worst-Case SRAM Zeroization Window:} Remote L1/L2/LLC cache residency of cohort $\alpha$ terminates deterministically within:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:bisnp_horizon}
  \Delta T_{\mathrm{res}}\bigl(K_{\mathrm{dir}}, K_{\mathrm{dirty}}\bigr) \le \max\left(\left\lceil \frac{K_{\mathrm{dir}}}{R_{\mathrm{snp}}} \right\rceil, \left\lceil \frac{5 K_{\mathrm{dirty}}}{4} \right\rceil\right) t_{\mathrm{clk}}
  + T_{\mathrm{snp\_lookup}} + \mathrm{RTT}_{\mathrm{CXL}},
  \end{equation}
  which for $K_{\mathrm{dir}} = 64$ lines ($4\text{~KB}$ page), $R_{\mathrm{snp}} = 2\text{~slots/cycle}$, $t_{\mathrm{clk}} = 4.0\text{~ns}$, and $\mathrm{RTT}_{\mathrm{CXL}} = 120.0\text{~ns}$ yields $\Delta T_{\mathrm{res}} \le 32 \times 4.0 + 120.0 = 248.0\text{~ns}$ for clean ($\mathrm{S}/\mathrm{E}$) pages ($K_{\mathrm{dirty}} = 0, T_{\mathrm{snp\_lookup}} = 0$) and $\Delta T_{\mathrm{res}} \le 80 \times 4.0 + 60.0 + 120.0 = 500.0\text{~ns}$ for a worst-case all-dirty 64-line page flush ($K_{\mathrm{dirty}} = 64, T_{\mathrm{snp\_lookup}} = 60.0\text{~ns}$); and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Acyclic Channel Dependency \& Deadlock Freedom:} Because \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} drains on the subordinate-to-master snoop channel and Stage~1 unconditionally bypasses returning \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp} completions and snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} writebacks ($\mathrm{viol}_i = 0$ when $\mathrm{birsp}_i \vee \mathrm{snoop\_evict}_i = 1$, \texttt{SVA 4}, \texttt{thm07}, \texttt{thm29}) without waiting on blocked forward \texttt{M2S\_Req}/\texttt{M2S\_RwD} traffic, the channel wait-for graph $\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{wait}}$ is strictly acyclic ($\beta_1(\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{wait}}) = 0$), guaranteeing zero protocol deadlocks.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:bisnp_shootdown}]
Injecting $K_{\mathrm{dir}}$ snoop probes takes $\lceil K_{\mathrm{dir}}/R_{\mathrm{snp}} \rceil$ cycles while draining $K_{\mathrm{dirty}}$ 5-slot \texttt{DirtyEvict} writebacks across 4 slots/cycle takes $\lceil 5 K_{\mathrm{dirty}}/4 \rceil$ cycles; adding $T_{\mathrm{snp\_lookup}} + \mathrm{RTT}_{\mathrm{CXL}}$ proves Eq.~\eqref{eq:bisnp_horizon}. In $\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{wait}}$, \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} depends only on sink-unblocked \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp}/\texttt{DirtyEvict} drains ($\mathrm{byp}_i = 1$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:pipeline_transfer}), forming a 2-step DAG $\texttt{S2M\_BISnp} \rightarrow \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp/DirtyEvict} \rightarrow \text{Sink}$ with zero cycles.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Two-Sided Goguen--Meseguer Unwinding and Digital Protocol Non-Interference~\cite{goguen1982security}]
\label{cor:unwinding}
On the joint state space $\mathcal{S} = \Sigma_{\mathrm{datapath}} \times \Sigma_{\mathrm{telemetry}} \times \mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{HBM}}$, define $S = (\sigma_{\mathrm{dp}}, \sigma_{\mathrm{tel}}, \mathfrak{M}) \sim_L (\sigma'_{\mathrm{dp}}, \sigma'_{\mathrm{tel}}, \mathfrak{M}') = S'$ iff $\sigma_{\mathrm{dp}} = \sigma'_{\mathrm{dp}}$ and $\mathfrak{M}[a] = \mathfrak{M}'[a]$ for all $a \in \mathrm{supp}(P_{\mathrm{eff}})$. In Stealth Scrub Mode across all $2^{512}$ flits: (i)~$\mathrm{Step}(S, F) \sim_L \mathrm{Step}(S', F)$ and $\mathrm{Out}_{\mathrm{tenant}}(S, F) \equiv \mathrm{Out}_{\mathrm{tenant}}(S', F)$ whenever $S \sim_L S'$, forcing $I(\mathrm{HBM}_{\mathrm{protected}}; \mathrm{Egress}_{\mathrm{tenant}}) \equiv 0$ bits with zero physical HBM row-buffer eviction (\texttt{m\_hbm\_axi\_arvalid\_o == 0}), zero same-\texttt{ARID} reordering, and zero host CTOs; (ii)~no FM write or warm FLR can expand $M^{(t)}$ or shrink $\mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t)})$; and (iii)~all \texttt{M2S\_BIRsp} snoops, snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} writebacks, and clamped \texttt{CXL.cache} requests complete without deadlock.
\end{corollary}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Corollary~\ref{cor:unwinding}]
Unauthorized writes ($\mathrm{viol}_i = 1$) are replaced by \texttt{CXL\_NOP} with $\texttt{WSTRB}_i = \texttt{16'h0000}$ (Local Respect), and unauthorized reads de-assert \texttt{m\_hbm\_axi\_arvalid\_o} and return deterministic \texttt{128'h0} completions (Step Consistency), yielding $I(\mathrm{HBM}_{\mathrm{protected}}; \mathrm{Egress}_{\mathrm{tenant}}) \equiv 0$ bits by the Goguen--Meseguer unwinding theorem~\cite{goguen1982security}.
\end{proof}
\global\specresumefalse\EndConnesPlate

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch~---~Post-PHY Digital CPI/FDI Closure \& Home-Agent Directory Reuse vs.\ Full-Custom Analog SerDes).}\;While our 16nm \texttt{xcvu47p} Dual-AFI runs on AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} (\texttt{agfi-03b742e2a9cd2c0f2} across 800,000 BIST slots and 16~GB HBM2 \texttt{agfi-08becf3b40b169674} across 25,000 DMA bursts) and placed-and-routed 7nm \texttt{ASAP7} predictive PDK CMOS sign-off ($693\text{~MHz}$) exercise the exact 512-bit post-PHY CPI/FDI digital flit interface, they do not substitute for full-custom 64--128~GT/s PAM-4 analog SerDes silicon: characterizing analog clock-data-recovery (CDR) jitter interaction, multi-switch fabric contention, and heavy-ion single-event latchup requires physical test-chip tapeout (Section~\ref{sec:roadmap}). Likewise, Proposition~\ref{prop:bisnp_shootdown}'s deterministic \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} cache shootdown bound ($\le 248\text{--}500\text{~ns}$) relies on the CXL~3.0 Home Agent's standard architectural snoop-filter directory rather than duplicating directory SRAM inside the \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT datapath core.

\section{Switching Power Bounds, Martingale Queueing Tail-Latency Analysis, and TCO Audit}
\label{sec:power}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Every unauthorized memory burst that penetrates past the ingress port of a disaggregated CXL switch fabric forces interior crossbar switches, retimers, and HBM bank queues to charge and discharge parasitic wire capacitances across multiple hops before eventually being aborted. By modelling the interconnect as a grounded-Dirichlet resistor network with Schur complement $S_\Gamma$, Theorem~\ref{thm:power} establishes from Thomson's principle and Spielman--Srivastava effective-resistance leverage scores $\ell_e$ that clamping unauthorized slots to \texttt{CXL\_NOP} ($\Delta B_a[n] \leftarrow 0$) at Stage~2 ingress eliminates interior switching dissipation proportionally to the Dirichlet conductance $h_{\mathrm{Dir}}^2(S_\Gamma)$ and effective-resistance leverage of the spanned chords.

\begin{theorem}[Schur-Reduced Boundary Coupling Power, Maximum-Leverage Spanning Forest, and Cluster Power Decomposition]
\label{thm:power}
Let the grounded-Dirichlet Graph Laplacian $\tilde{L}_G = L_G + G_{V_{\mathrm{SS}}} \succ 0$ of an interconnect switching network be partitioned into boundary ports $B$ and interior nodes $S$ with Schur complement $S_\Gamma = \tilde{L}_{B,B} - \tilde{L}_{B,S} \tilde{L}_{S,S}^{-1} \tilde{L}_{S,B} \succ 0$, maximum grounded boundary degree $d_{\max}$, Dirichlet conductance $h_{\mathrm{Dir}}(S_\Gamma)$, effective-resistance leverage scores~\cite{spielman2011graph}~$\ell_e = w_e^\Gamma (\delta_a - \delta_b)^T S_\Gamma^{-1}(\delta_a - \delta_b) = \Pr[e \in T_{\mathrm{rand}}] \in (0, 1]$, maximum chord resistance $R_{\max}$, and switching energy $E_{\mathrm{sw}} = \frac{1}{2} C_{\mathrm{unit}} V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2$~\cite{landauer1961irreversibility,thornton2026renesis}. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Grounded Spatial Coupling Lower Bound:} For any boundary excitation $\Delta B[n] \in \{0,1\}^{|B|}$ with chord differential $\Delta H_e[n] = (\Delta B_a[n] - \Delta B_b[n])^2$ (where Stage-2 ingress \texttt{CXL\_NOP} clamping zeroes $\Delta B_a[n] \leftarrow 0$ on blocked bursts):
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:schur_power}
  \dot{Q}_{\mathrm{interior}}[n] \ge E_{\mathrm{sw}} f_{\mathrm{clk}} \Delta B[n]^T S_\Gamma \Delta B[n]
  \ge E_{\mathrm{sw}} f_{\mathrm{clk}} \max\Biggl\{ \frac{h_{\mathrm{Dir}}^2(S_\Gamma)}{2 d_{\max}} \|\Delta B[n]\|_2^2,\;
  \frac{1}{R_{\max}} \sum_{\substack{e=(a,b) \in E(S_\Gamma)}} \ell_e \Delta H_e[n] + \sum_{a \in B} g_{\mathrm{SS},a} \Delta B_a[n]^2 \Biggr\};
  \end{equation}
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Cluster-Scale Power Decomposition:} Across $N_{\mathrm{racks}}$ racks with pooled DRAM $M_{\mathrm{rack,GB}}$, table carve-out $f_{\mathrm{meta}}$, DRAM refresh power $p_{\mathrm{DRAM}}$, fleet IT load $P_{\mathrm{IT,fleet}}$, stall fraction $\eta_{\mathrm{stall}}$ at wait-state factor $\alpha_{\mathrm{wait}}$, and facility $\mathrm{PUE}$:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:grid_power}
  \Delta P_{\mathrm{grid}} = \underbrace{\mathrm{PUE} \cdot N_{\mathrm{racks}}\bigl(f_{\mathrm{meta}} M_{\mathrm{rack,GB}} p_{\mathrm{DRAM}} - P_{\mathrm{core}}\bigr)}_{\!\Delta P_{\mathrm{DRAM,pass}}\text{ (load-indep.)}} \!+\! \underbrace{\mathrm{PUE} \cdot P_{\mathrm{IT,fleet}}\bigl(\eta_{\mathrm{stall}} \alpha_{\mathrm{wait}} + \eta_{\mathrm{SRAM}}\bigr)}_{\Delta P_{\mathrm{stall,active}}\text{ (load-dep.)}}.
  \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:power}]
Block LDU factorization gives $(\tilde{L}_G^{-1})_{B,B} = S_\Gamma^{-1}$, and Thomson's principle yields $\dot{Q}_{\mathrm{interior}}[n] \ge E_{\mathrm{sw}} f_{\mathrm{clk}} \Delta B[n]^T S_\Gamma \Delta B[n]$. Using $w_e^\Gamma \ge \ell_e / R_{\max}$~\cite{spielman2011graph} and the coarea inequality $h_{\mathrm{Dir}}(S_\Gamma)\|x\|_2^2 \le \sqrt{x^T S_\Gamma x}\sqrt{2d_{\max}}\|x\|_2$~\cite{kamal2026cheeger} yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:schur_power}.
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Martingale Stochastic Network Calculus Tail Bounds, Hitless Dual-Epoch Grace Window, and Cluster TCO Audit}
\label{sec:tco}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; In stateful permission-table firewalls, an SRAM permission-cache miss rate $m_{\mathrm{perm}} \in [5.2\%, 14.1\%]$ injects random $32$--$68\text{~ns}$ DRAM table-walk stalls into the memory service process $S(t)$. Under bursty Markov-modulated arrivals (such as DeepSeek/Mixtral sparse MoE token routing), these stochastic service stalls compound exponentially in the queueing backlog, driving P99.99 tail latency to $184.2\text{~ns}$. Furthermore, rotating cryptographic epochs by pausing link traffic incurs a drain penalty $\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) > 0$. Theorem~\ref{thm:martingale} proves via Doob's supermartingale inequality that eliminating permission-cache misses ($m_{\mathrm{perm}} \equiv 0$, deterministic $\tau_{\mathrm{core}} = 4.0\text{~ns}$) collapses the P99.99 tail to a hard $8.0\text{~ns}$ cut-through wall, while a dual-active grace window ($\texttt{bank\_valid} = \texttt{2'b11}$) indexed per slot by $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$ achieves hitless epoch rotation ($\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) \equiv 0$) with zero dropped write beats.

\begin{theorem}[Martingale Stochastic Network Calculus Tail Bounds, Bellman Pre-Keying, \& Hitless Per-Slot Dual-Epoch Grace Window]
\label{thm:martingale}
Let sub-flit requests arrive via a Markov-Modulated Arrival Process $A(t)$ with transition matrix $P$, MGF matrix $M_\theta(u,v) = P_{u,v} \mathbb{E}[e^{\theta a_t} \mid J_t = v]$, spectral radius $\rho(M_\theta)$, and service process $S(t)$ (where stateful tables incur $\mathrm{Bernoulli}(m_{\mathrm{perm}})$ walk stalls $T_{\mathrm{walk}} \in [32, 68]\text{~ns}$, vs.\ $m_{\mathrm{perm}} \equiv 0$ and deterministic $\tau_{\mathrm{core}} = 4.0\text{~ns}$ for \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter}). Let $\theta^* > 0$ satisfy $\rho(M_{\theta^*}) \mathbb{E}[e^{-\theta^* S_1}] \le 1$ with left eigenvector $h_{\theta^*}$~\cite{poloczek2014martingale}, and let \texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x60\_bank[0:1]} operate under the 2-bit state machine $\texttt{bank\_valid[1:0]} \in \{\texttt{2'b01}, \texttt{2'b11}, \texttt{2'b10}\}$. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Non-Asymptotic Doob Supermartingale Tail Bound~\cite{poloczek2014martingale}:} $Z_t := h_{\theta^*}(J_t)\exp(\theta^*(A(t) - S(t)))$ is a non-negative supermartingale, and Doob's maximal inequality bounds the waiting-time tail at any $\tau > 0$ by:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:martingale_tail}
  \mathbb{P}\bigl(W_q > \tau\bigr) \le K_0 \exp\bigl(-\theta^*(m_{\mathrm{perm}}) c_{\mathrm{link}} \tau\bigr),\qquad
  K_0 := \frac{\max_j h_{\theta^*}(j)}{\min_j h_{\theta^*}(j)},
  \end{equation}
  reducing P99.99 tail latency on sparse MoE workloads from $184.2\text{~ns}$ to $8.0\text{~ns}$ (Table~\ref{tab:workloads} and Figure~\ref{fig:martingale_latency});
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Bellman Pre-Keying Threshold $u^*$:} Because $\Phi(u) = \binom{u}{w}/\binom{64}{w}$ has strictly positive second difference $\Delta^2 \Phi(u) = \binom{u}{w-2}/\binom{64}{w} > 0$, a single threshold $u^* \le u_{\max} - \lceil \lambda_{\mathrm{rev}} T_{\mathrm{stage}} w \rceil$ (or $d^* = 6 < 8$ in Vieta mode) completes background RoT key staging into the inactive bank before active-bank saturation; and
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Hitless Per-Slot Dual-Epoch Grace Window ($\mathrm{Path}(G_{\mathrm{active}}) \equiv 0$, Zero Write Drops):} Transitioning $\texttt{bank\_valid[1:0]}: \texttt{2'b01} \rightarrow \texttt{2'b11}$ (Dual-Active Grace Window) selects the bank per 128-bit slot via $b_i = \texttt{slot\_bank\_sel\_i}[i]$ (ratcheting both banks on any new revocation during $\texttt{2'b11}$) before retiring Epoch~0 ($\texttt{2'b11} \rightarrow \texttt{2'b10}$), eliminating in-flight \texttt{M2S\_RwD} silent write drops (Lean~4 \id{thm25_dual_epoch_grace_window}, Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2}, Row~16).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:martingale}]
Conditioning on $\mathcal{F}_t$ gives $\mathbb{E}\bigl[Z_{t+1} \mid \mathcal{F}_t\bigr] = Z_t\,\rho(M_{\theta^*})\,\mathbb{E}\bigl[e^{-\theta^* S_1}\bigr] \le Z_t$, and Doob's maximal inequality yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:martingale_tail}. Pascal's identity gives $\Delta^2 \Phi(u) = \binom{u}{w-2}/\binom{64}{w} > 0$, establishing $u^*$. During $\texttt{bank\_valid} = \texttt{2'b11}$, both banks are valid so in-flight $b_i=0$ and new $b_i=1$ slots pass without link stalls while dual-bank ratcheting preserves monotonic isolation (\texttt{thm25}).
\end{proof}
\MidConnesRule
\noindent\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}
\captionsetup{hypcap=false}
\centering
\IncludeSpectralPlate{fig3_martingale_latency.pdf}
\captionof{figure}{Disaggregated Sparse-MoE waiting-time CCDF $\mathbb{P}(W_q > \tau)$ and Doob supermartingale tail bound: deterministic $8.0\text{~ns}$ cut-through wall ($m_{\mathrm{perm}}\equiv 0$) vs.\ $34.8\text{~ns}$ CHERI/Morello and $184.2\text{~ns}$ stateful DRAM permission-table walk stalls at P99.99.\vspace{3pt}\\
\textbf{So What? (Architectural Consequence).}\; In synchronous all-to-all token dispatch for sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference, the slowest memory completion across all parallel experts dictates the step time of the entire GPU pod. Figure~\ref{fig:martingale_latency} shows that permission-cache thrashing in stateful DRAM-table firewalls inflates P99.99 tail latency by $23\times$ ($184.2\text{~ns}$ vs.\ $8.0\text{~ns}$), whereas \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} enforces a deterministic $m_{\mathrm{perm}} \equiv 0$ service process whose queueing tail terminates at a hard $8.0\text{~ns}$ vertical wall.}
\label{fig:martingale_latency}
\end{minipage}
\MidConnesRule
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; A $1.5625\%$ ($1/64$) DRAM permission-table carve-out and a $3.30\%$--$19.0\%$ memory-stall penalty may appear modest at the scale of a single DIMM, but at hyperscale AI cluster dimensions ($10,000$ racks $= 160\text{~PB}$ decimal / $156.25\text{~PiB}$ binary of pooled HBM/DDR5), those two taxes strand $2,500\text{~TB}$ of physical memory and burn tens of megawatts of continuous grid power: first, from periodic \texttt{tREFI} charge-restoration refresh currents across $2.5\times 10^6\text{~GB}$ of dedicated table DRAM; and second, from non-gated clock-tree capacitance ($C_{\mathrm{clk}}V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2 f$), HBM PHY PLLs, and FinFET subthreshold leakage across thousands of accelerators held in memory-wait stall states. Theorem~\ref{thm:thermo_env} formalizes the exact solid-state, grid-power, hydrological, and lifecycle-carbon conservation laws governing this 10,000-rack footprint.

\begin{theorem}[Thermodynamic, Grid-Power, Hydrological, and Lifecycle-Carbon Scaling Across Disaggregated Clusters]
\label{thm:thermo_env}
\label{thm:cluster_thermodynamics}
Consider a disaggregated AI/HPC cluster of $N_{\mathrm{racks}} = 10,000$ racks, each provisioned with $M_{\mathrm{rack,dec}} = 16\text{~TB}$ decimal ($16,000\text{~GB}$; fleet total $160\text{~PB}$) or $M_{\mathrm{rack,bin}} = 16\text{~TiB}$ binary ($16,384\text{~GiB}$; fleet total $156.25\text{~PiB}$) of pooled CXL DRAM/HBM at unit capital cost $c_{\mathrm{DRAM}} = \$15/\text{GB}$ (or $\$15/\text{GiB}$). Under a stateful permission-table architecture requiring $1\text{~B}$ of permission metadata per $64\text{~B}$ cacheline ($f_{\mathrm{meta}} = 1/64 = 1.5625\%$), JEDEC DDR5/HBM background refresh and CXL-buffer standby power $p_{\mathrm{DRAM}} = 0.45\text{~W/GB}$ at $V_{\mathrm{DD}} = 1.1\text{~V}$ (\texttt{tREFI} $= 3.9\text{~}\mu\text{s}$), fleet IT load $P_{\mathrm{IT,fleet}} = 400\text{~MW}$ ($40\text{~kW/rack}$) with non-gated accelerator wait-state power factor $\alpha_{\mathrm{wait}} = 0.40$ ($P_{\mathrm{rack,wait}} = 16.0\text{~kW/rack}$, spanning $[6.0, 20.0]\text{~kW/rack}$) across permission-miss stall fraction $\eta_{\mathrm{stall}} \in [3.30\%, 19.0\%]$, and \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} datapath power $P_{\mathrm{core}} = 35.0\text{~mW}$ per FPGA core (\AsicTotalPowerMw\ in 7nm ASAP7; $< 3\text{~kW}$ cluster-wide across 80,000 ports):
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Exact Reclaimed Memory Capacity and Capital Expenditure ($\Delta\mathrm{CapEx}$):} Eliminating the $f_{\mathrm{meta}} = 1/64$ table carve-out reclaims $\Delta C_{\mathrm{dec}} = 2,500\text{~TB}$ ($2.50 \times 10^6\text{~GB}$) decimal or $\Delta C_{\mathrm{bin}} = 2,560\text{~TiB}$ ($2.56 \times 10^6\text{~GiB}$) binary, yielding:
  \begin{align}
  \Delta\mathrm{CapEx}_{\mathrm{dec}} &= 2.50\times 10^6\text{~GB} \times \$15/\text{GB} = \$37.50\text{M}\quad(\Delta P_{\mathrm{DRAM,pass}} = 1.406\text{ / }1.575\text{ MW at PUE }1.25\text{ / }1.40), \label{eq:tco_dec}\\
  \Delta\mathrm{CapEx}_{\mathrm{bin}} &= 2.56\times 10^6\text{~GiB} \times \$15/\text{GiB} = \$38.40\text{M}\quad(\Delta P_{\mathrm{DRAM,pass}} = 1.440\text{ / }1.613\text{ MW at PUE }1.25\text{ / }1.40); \label{eq:tco_bin}
  \end{align}
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Combined Passive Refresh and Active Stall-Wait Grid Power ($\Delta P_{\mathrm{grid}}$) \& Annual Energy ($\Delta E_{\mathrm{yr}}$):} At $\mathrm{PUE} = 1.25$ (and LBNL U.S.\ fleet-average $\mathrm{PUE} = 1.40$~\cite{shehabi2024lbnl}), active memory-stall wait power $\Delta P_{\mathrm{stall,active}} = \mathrm{PUE} \cdot P_{\mathrm{IT,fleet}} \eta_{\mathrm{stall}} \alpha_{\mathrm{wait}}$ equals $6.60\text{~MW}$ at average stall rate $\eta_{\mathrm{stall}} = 3.30\%$ and $22.00$--$38.00\text{~MW}$ under sparse MoE / KV-cache thrashing ($\eta_{\mathrm{stall}} \in [11.0\%, 19.0\%]$). Summing passive DRAM refresh and active stall savings minus $<3\text{~kW}$ sanitizer power yields total continuous grid-power and annual energy savings ($8,760\text{~h/yr}$):
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:thermo_total_energy}
  \Delta P_{\mathrm{grid}} \in \bigl[8.01\text{~MW}\;(8.04\text{~MW bin}),\; 39.44\text{~MW}\bigr],\qquad
  \Delta E_{\mathrm{yr}} = 8,760\text{~h/yr} \times \Delta P_{\mathrm{grid}} \in \bigl[70.13\;(70.43\text{~bin}),\; 345.49\bigr]\text{~GWh/yr};
  \end{equation}
  \item[(iii)] \textbf{Hydrological Cooling-Water Conservation and Scope-2 / Scope-3 Carbon Reduction:} At the U.S.\ data-center blended direct+indirect Water Usage Effectiveness $\mathrm{WUE} = 1.80\text{~L/kWh}_{\mathrm{IT}}$ ($\Delta E_{\mathrm{IT,yr}} = \Delta E_{\mathrm{yr}}/\mathrm{PUE} \in [56.11\;(56.34\text{~bin}), 276.40]\text{~GWh}_{\mathrm{IT}}/\text{yr}$)~\cite{siddik2021water,shehabi2024lbnl}, EPA eGRID2024 U.S.\ national average grid carbon intensity $I_{\mathrm{CO_2,US}} = 0.341\text{~kg CO}_2\text{e/kWh}$ (and hyperscale balancing-authority intensity $I_{\mathrm{CO_2,coal/gas}} = 0.545\text{~kg CO}_2\text{e/kWh}$), and the Architectural Carbon Modeling Tool (\texttt{ACT}) embodied DRAM fabrication footprint $c_{\mathrm{emb}} \in [0.15, 0.60]\text{~kg CO}_2\text{e/GB}$~\cite{gupta2022act}, eliminating the permission-table carve-out and stall tax conserves:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:water_carbon_bounds}
  \begin{aligned}
  \Delta W_{\mathrm{H_2O}} &= \mathrm{WUE} \cdot \Delta E_{\mathrm{IT,yr}} \in \bigl[101.0\text{M}\;(101.4\text{M bin}),\; 497.5\text{M}\bigr]\text{~L/yr}\quad\bigl(26.7\text{M}\;[26.8\text{M bin}]\text{--}131.4\text{M U.S.\ gal/yr}\bigr),\\
  \Delta M_{\mathrm{Scope2}} &= I_{\mathrm{CO_2}} \cdot \Delta E_{\mathrm{yr}} \in \bigl[23,915\;(24,017\text{~bin}),\; 117,814\bigr]\text{~tCO}_2\text{e/yr}\;\text{(U.S.)}\;\text{to}\;\bigl[38,223\;(38,385\text{~bin}),\; 188,294\bigr]\text{~tCO}_2\text{e/yr},\\
  \Delta M_{\mathrm{Scope3}} &= c_{\mathrm{emb}} \cdot \Delta C \in \bigl[375\;(384\text{~bin}),\; 1,536\bigr]\text{~tCO}_2\text{e}\;\text{(embodied semiconductor fab carbon avoided)}.
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:thermo_env}]
Multiplying $N_{\mathrm{racks}} = 10^4$ by $f_{\mathrm{meta}} M_{\mathrm{rack}} = \frac{1}{64}(16,000\text{~GB}) = 250\text{~GB}$ (or $\frac{1}{64}(16,384\text{~GiB}) = 256\text{~GiB}$) gives $\Delta C_{\mathrm{dec}} = 2.50\times 10^6\text{~GB}$ and $\Delta C_{\mathrm{bin}} = 2.56\times 10^6\text{~GiB}$, establishing Eqs.~\eqref{eq:tco_dec}--\eqref{eq:tco_bin} at $\$15/\text{GB}$ and $p_{\mathrm{DRAM}} = 0.45\text{~W/GB}$ ($1.125\text{~MW}_{\mathrm{IT}} \times 1.25 = 1.40625\text{~MW}$ decimal; $1.152\text{~MW}_{\mathrm{IT}} \times 1.25 = 1.440\text{~MW}$ binary). Adding $\Delta P_{\mathrm{stall,active}} = 1.25 \times 400\text{~MW} \times 0.40 \times \eta_{\mathrm{stall}} \in [6.60, 38.00]\text{~MW}$ yields $\Delta P_{\mathrm{grid}} \in [8.006, 39.440]\text{~MW}$ and $\Delta E_{\mathrm{yr}} = 8,760 \Delta P_{\mathrm{grid}} \in [70.13, 345.49]\text{~GWh/yr}$ (or $70.43\text{~GWh/yr}$ binary lower bound). Multiplying $\Delta E_{\mathrm{IT,yr}} = \Delta E_{\mathrm{yr}}/1.25$ by $\mathrm{WUE} = 1.80\text{~L/kWh}_{\mathrm{IT}}$, $\Delta E_{\mathrm{yr}}$ by $I_{\mathrm{CO_2}} \in \{0.341, 0.545\}\text{~tCO}_2\text{e/MWh}$, and $\Delta C$ by $c_{\mathrm{emb}} \in [0.15, 0.60]\text{~kg CO}_2\text{e/GB}$ establishes Eq.~\eqref{eq:water_carbon_bounds}.
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch~---~Unconditional Carve-Out Reclamation vs.\ Workload-Dependent Active Stall Power).}\;In Theorem~\ref{thm:cluster_thermodynamics}, the 10,000-rack CapEx reclamation ($\text{\$37.50M--\$38.40M}$ for $2,500\text{~TB} / 2,560\text{~TiB}$), embodied Scope-3 semiconductor carbon avoidance ($375\text{--}1,536\text{~tCO}_2\text{e}$), and passive DDR5/HBM \texttt{tREFI} refresh savings ($1.41\text{--}1.61\text{~MW}$) hold unconditionally across all workloads whenever the $1/64$ DRAM permission-table carve-out is removed. By contrast, the active accelerator stall-wait power savings ($6.60\text{--}38.00\text{~MW}$) scale linearly with the permission-cache miss stall fraction $\eta_{\mathrm{stall}} \in [3.30\%, 19.0\%]$ and wait-state power factor $\alpha_{\mathrm{wait}}$: for static batch workloads whose permission working sets fit entirely inside on-die SRAM ($\eta_{\mathrm{stall}} \to 0$), cluster power savings converge to the $1.41\text{--}1.61\text{~MW}$ passive refresh floor.

\section{End-to-End Formal Verification, LUT SRAM Bi-Simulation, and Coprime Odd-Cycle Stage-Mixer Cryptanalysis}
\label{sec:formal}

\subsection{Zero-Leakage Canonical Egress and Post-Route \texorpdfstring{\texttt{LUT6\_2}/\texttt{LUT2}}{LUT6\_2 / LUT2} Netlist Bi-Simulation}
\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Two subtle verification gaps undermine conventional hardware security enclaves: first, if an enclave emits variable-format error syndromes or allows unlimited adaptive witness queries after a failed check, an attacker can extract internal state bit-by-bit; second, proving a high-level RTL behavioral model in a theorem prover does not prove that the vendor synthesis tool mapped those equations onto the correct physical FPGA lookup-table SRAM bits. Theorem~\ref{thm:egress} closes the multi-query side channel via a sticky lockout latch that bounds total unconditioned leakage across $M_{\mathrm{qry}}$ queries to $\le \log_2(M_{\mathrm{qry}}+1)$ bits, while Theorem~\ref{thm:lut_sram} bi-simulates the exact 64-bit AMD UG574 configuration SRAM hex frames (\texttt{64'h2A2A2A2A\_80808080} and \texttt{4'h2}) across all bit lanes in Lean~4.

\begin{theorem}[Zero Conditional Shannon Mutual Information \& Sticky Multi-Query Lockout of Canonical Egress]
\label{thm:egress}
Let $\Sigma \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ denote an authorized query goal (\texttt{BAR0 CSR 0x58}) and $X_{\mathrm{opt}}$ the internal upstream state. Emitting $\mathcal{N}(\Sigma, \pi) = \Sigma$ iff $\mathrm{Valid} = 1 \wedge \mathrm{Lockout} = 0$ and permanently latching $\mathrm{Lockout} \leftarrow 1$ (emitting $\mathbf{0}_{64}$) upon the first invalid witness guarantees across $M_{\mathrm{qry}} \ge 1$ queries:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:egress_mi}
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal{N}(\Sigma, \pi_1) &= \mathcal{N}(\Sigma, \pi_2),\qquad
I\bigl(X_{\mathrm{opt}}; \mathcal{N}(\Sigma_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}, \pi_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}) \;\big|\; \Sigma_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}, \mathrm{Valid}_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}\bigr) = 0,\\
I\bigl(X_{\mathrm{opt}}; \mathcal{N}(\Sigma_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}, \pi_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}) \;\big|\; \Sigma_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}\bigr) &\le \log_2(M_{\mathrm{qry}} + 1).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:egress}]
Given $(\Sigma_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}}, \mathrm{Valid}_{1:M_{\mathrm{qry}}})$, $\mathcal{N}(\Sigma_m, \pi_m)$ is deterministic ($H = 0$). Unconditioned, $\mathrm{Lockout}_m = \mathrm{Lockout}_{m-1} \vee \neg\mathrm{Valid}_m$ restricts $(\mathrm{Valid}_m \wedge \neg\mathrm{Lockout}_{m-1})_{m=1}^{M_{\mathrm{qry}}}$ to $\{1^k 0^{M_{\mathrm{qry}}-k} : 0 \le k \le M_{\mathrm{qry}}\}$ of cardinality $M_{\mathrm{qry}}+1$, bounding entropy by $\log_2(M_{\mathrm{qry}}+1)$ bits (Lean~4 \texttt{thm04}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Physical \texttt{xcvu47p} Configuration SRAM \texttt{LUT6\_2}/\texttt{LUT2} Translation Validation]
\label{thm:lut_sram}
Per AMD UG574, each Stage-1 pin-locked \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} ($\texttt{I5} = 1, \texttt{I4} = \texttt{I3} = 0, \texttt{INIT = 64'h2A2A2A2A\_80808080}$) and Stage-2 \texttt{LUT2} ($\texttt{INIT = 4'h2}$) with address $a_i = 4p_{t,i} + 2p_{s,i} + s_i \in \{0,\ldots,7\}$ satisfies across all 64 bit lanes:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:lut6_o5}
\begin{aligned}
\mathrm{LUT6\_O5}_{80}(p_{t,i}, p_{s,i}, s_i) &= \bigl(\texttt{8'h80} \gg a_i\bigr) \wedge 1 = s_i \wedge (p_{s,i} \wedge p_{t,i}),\\
\mathrm{LUT6\_O6}_{2\mathrm{A}}(p_{t,i}, p_{s,i}, s_i) &= \bigl(\texttt{8'h2A} \gg a_i\bigr) \wedge 1 = s_i \wedge \neg(p_{s,i} \wedge p_{t,i}),\\
\mathrm{LUT2}_{02}(v_i, f_i) &= \bigl(\texttt{4'h2} \gg (2v_i + f_i)\bigr) \wedge 1 = f_i \wedge \neg v_i.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:lut_sram}]
$\texttt{8'h80} = \texttt{1000\_0000}_2$ has a single $1$ at $a_i = 7$, $\texttt{8'h2A} = \texttt{0010\_1010}_2$ has $1$s at $a_i \in \{1, 3, 5\}$, and $\texttt{4'h2} = \texttt{0010}_2$ has its sole $1$ at $2v_i + f_i = 1$ (Lean~4 \texttt{thm05}, Table~\ref{tab:lean}, Row~4).
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Single-\texorpdfstring{\texttt{LUT4}}{LUT4} Coprime Odd-Cycle (\texorpdfstring{$\chi_{33} \times \chi_{31} \circ \mathcal{L}_5$}{chi\_33 x chi\_31 o L\_5}) Stage Permutation, 128-Bit Dual-Key Cubic \texorpdfstring{$\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$}{GF(2\^64)} MAC, and Zero-Syscall User-Space Capability Attenuation}
In Daemen's cellular-automaton design~\cite{daemen1995cipher}, $\chi_n(y)_i := y_i \oplus (\neg y_{i+1} \wedge y_{i+2})$ (indices mod~$n$) is a bijection iff $n$ is odd ($\gcd(n,2) = 1$). While Mode-B.2/C unsealers use a 2-round balanced Feistel swap $\mathrm{Enc}_{K_0,K_1}(L_0,R_0)$ on $\mathbb{F}_2^n \times \mathbb{F}_2^n$ (\texttt{thm11}), for the 64-bit MAC datapath we partition $64 = 33 + 31$ into two coprime odd cycle lengths ($\gcd(33,31) = 1$) and apply $\chi_{33} \times \chi_{31}$ in parallel followed by $\mathcal{L}_5$ in a \textbf{single \texttt{LUT4} logic level per stage}:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:coprime_chi}
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal{X}_{31,33}(y, K) := \mathcal{L}_5\Bigl(&\bigl(\chi_{33}(y[63:31]) \oplus K[63:31]\bigr)\\
&\parallel \bigl(\chi_{31}(y[30:0]) \oplus K[30:0]\bigr)\Bigr).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
Evaluating the inner Carter--Wegman polynomial~\cite{carter1979universal} at the non-Frobenius cube power $K_{\mathrm{uhash}}^3 = K_{\mathrm{uhash}}^2 \odot K_{\mathrm{uhash}}$ prevents $\mathbb{F}_2$-linear key-schedule weaknesses.

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Standard 64-bit cellular-automaton S-boxes such as $\chi_{64}$ fail to be invertible because $\gcd(64,2)=2 \neq 1$, whereas splitting 64 bits into two coprime odd cycles $64 = 33 + 31$ ($\gcd(33,31)=1$) restores global bijectivity in a single \texttt{LUT4} logic level per round. Furthermore, in disaggregated LLM serving where a Prefill GPU hands off a KV-cache page to an untrusted Decode GPU, invoking the OS kernel or Fabric Manager to mint a read-only capability incurs microsecond syscall overhead, yet a bare reversible permutation would allow the Decode GPU to invert the round function and recover the parent read/write token. Theorems~\ref{thm:mac} and~\ref{thm:coprime_chi} pair a non-Frobenius cubic $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$ Carter--Wegman MAC ($P_{\mathrm{coll}} \equiv 0$ intra-page, $<2^{-63}$ inter-page) with an 8-round Davies--Meyer feed-forward XOR ($\tau_{\mathrm{child}} = \mathcal{X}_{31,33}^{(8)}(\tau_{\mathrm{parent}}, K_{\mathrm{ctx}}) \oplus \tau_{\mathrm{parent}}$) that enables $<2\text{~ns}$, zero-syscall user-space capability attenuation while destroying backward invertibility.

\begin{theorem}[128-Bit Dual-Key Non-Frobenius Cubic $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$ Carter--Wegman MAC Security]
\label{thm:mac}
For any 4-KB page $\mathrm{HPA}_k[51:12] \in \mathcal{W}_n$ with $x_{\mathrm{hi},k} = [\mathrm{LD\_ID}_k \parallel \mathrm{Perms}_k \parallel \mathrm{SPID}_k \parallel \mathrm{HPA}_k[51:12]] \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ and $x_{\mathrm{lo},k} = c_{\mathrm{cohort},k}[63:0] \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$, the 4-cycle pipelined MAC kernel (\SharedCubicMacLUTs~CLB LUTs on \texttt{xcvu47p}) with $(K_{\mathrm{uhash}}, K_{\mathrm{whiten}}) \in (\mathbb{F}_2^{64} \setminus \{\mathbf{0}_{64}, \mathbf{1}_{\mathrm{GF}}\}) \times (\mathbb{F}_2^{64} \setminus \{\mathbf{0}_{64}\})$, $K'_{\mathrm{whiten}} = K_{\mathrm{whiten}} \oplus c_{\mathrm{dom}}$, and $K_{\mathrm{mix}} = \mathcal{X}_{K_{\mathrm{whiten}}}^{(4)}(K_{\mathrm{uhash}} \oplus c_{\mathrm{hi}})$ computes:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:mac}
\begin{aligned}
u_k &:= \mathcal{X}_{K_{\mathrm{whiten}}}^{(4)}(x_{\mathrm{hi},k} \oplus c_{\mathrm{hi}}), &
v_k &:= \mathcal{X}_{K'_{\mathrm{whiten}}}^{(4)}(x_{\mathrm{lo},k} \oplus c_{\mathrm{dom}}),\\
P_{\mathrm{CW},k} &:= \bigl(u_k \odot K_{\mathrm{uhash}}^3\bigr) \oplus \bigl(v_k \odot K_{\mathrm{uhash}}\bigr), &
\hat{\tau}_k &= \mathcal{H}_{K_{\mathrm{active}}}(x_{\mathrm{hi},k}, x_{\mathrm{lo},k}) := \mathcal{X}_{K_{\mathrm{mix}}}^{(8)}\bigl(\mathcal{L}_1(P_{\mathrm{CW},k})\bigr) \oplus K_{\mathrm{mix}}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
Then: (i)~each quadratic coordinate $Q_i(y) = y_i \oplus (\neg y_{i+1} \wedge y_{i+2})$ has zero DC bias $\hat{Q}_i(\mathbf{0}) = 0$ and plateaued Walsh--Hadamard spectrum $|\hat{Q}_i(\omega)| \in \{0, 2^{n-1}\}$; (ii)~distinct 128-bit tuples $(x_{\mathrm{hi},k}, x_{\mathrm{lo},k}) \neq (x_{\mathrm{hi},k'}, x_{\mathrm{lo},k'})$ collide with probability $P_{\mathrm{coll}} \equiv 0$ when $x_{\mathrm{hi},k} = x_{\mathrm{hi},k'}$ and $P_{\mathrm{coll}} \le 1/(2^{64}-2) < 2^{-63}$ over uniform $K_{\mathrm{uhash}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64} \setminus \{\mathbf{0}_{64}, \mathbf{1}_{\mathrm{GF}}\}$ when $x_{\mathrm{hi},k} \neq x_{\mathrm{hi},k'}$; and (iii)~cohort revocation in \texttt{CSR 0x60/0x68} executes in 1~cycle without key rotation.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:mac}]
In $Q_i(y) = y_i \oplus (1 \oplus y_{i+1})y_{i+2}$, $y_i$ enters linearly, forcing $\hat{Q}_i(\mathbf{0}) = 0$ and $|\hat{Q}_i(\omega)| \in \{0, 2^{n-1}\}$. Bijectivity of $\mathcal{X}^{(8)} \circ \mathcal{L}_1$ implies $\hat{\tau}_k = \hat{\tau}_{k'} \iff ((\Delta u \odot K_{\mathrm{uhash}}^2) \oplus \Delta v) \odot K_{\mathrm{uhash}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, which has 0~roots in $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})^\times$ when $\Delta u = \mathbf{0}_{64}, \Delta v \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$, and $\le 1$ root when $\Delta u \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}$ since $K \mapsto K^2$ is a bijection on $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Single-\texttt{LUT4} Coprime Odd-Cycle Bijection ($\chi_{33} \times \chi_{31}$), Group-Ring Roots of Rotation, 2-Share DOM Glitch Non-Completeness, \& Davies--Meyer User-Space Capability Attenuation]
\label{thm:coprime_chi}
Consider $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}(y, K)$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:coprime_chi}, $\mathcal{L}_a(m) = m \oplus \mathrm{RotL}_a(m) \oplus \mathrm{RotL}_{32}(m)$ (odd $a \in \{1,\ldots,31\}$), and the 2-share DOM~\cite{gross2016dom} decomposition $s = s^{(0)} \oplus s^{(1)}$, $p_{\mathrm{eff}} = p_{\mathrm{eff}}^{(0)} \oplus p_{\mathrm{eff}}^{(1)}$ across two fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} cells with fresh blinding bit $z \in \mathbb{F}_2$ and explicit cross-domain synchronizing flip-flop stage $\mathrm{Reg}(\cdot)$:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:dom_lut6}
q^{(0)} := \bigl(s^{(0)} \wedge p_{\mathrm{eff}}^{(0)}\bigr) \oplus \mathrm{Reg}\!\left(\bigl(s^{(0)} \wedge p_{\mathrm{eff}}^{(1)}\bigr) \oplus z\right),\qquad
q^{(1)} := \bigl(s^{(1)} \wedge p_{\mathrm{eff}}^{(1)}\bigr) \oplus \mathrm{Reg}\!\left(\bigl(s^{(1)} \wedge p_{\mathrm{eff}}^{(0)}\bigr) \oplus z\right).
\end{equation}
Furthermore, for disaggregated Prefill-to-Decode LLM KV-cache handoff~\cite{birgisson2014macaroons}, let a parent process holding $\tau_{\mathrm{parent}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ with permissions $\mathrm{Perms}_{\mathrm{parent}}$ derive a delegated child capability in user-space registers in $<2\text{~ns}$ (0~syscalls) via an 8-round Davies--Meyer one-way feed-forward compression:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:macaroon_att}
\mathrm{Perms}' := \mathrm{Perms}_{\mathrm{parent}} \wedge M_{\mathrm{att}} \le \mathrm{Perms}_{\mathrm{parent}},\qquad
K_{\mathrm{ctx}} := \bigl[\mathrm{Perms}' \parallel \mathrm{SPID}_{\mathrm{decode}} \parallel \mathrm{Epoch}_t\bigr],\qquad
\tau_{\mathrm{child}} := \mathcal{X}_{31,33}^{(8)}\bigl(\tau_{\mathrm{parent}},\, K_{\mathrm{ctx}}\bigr) \oplus \tau_{\mathrm{parent}}.
\end{equation}
Then: (i)~$\chi_{33}$ and $\chi_{31}$ are global bijections with inverse degrees $\deg(\chi_{33}^{-1}) = 17$ and $\deg(\chi_{31}^{-1}) = 16$~\cite{daemen1995cipher} executing in 1~\texttt{LUT4} level per stage; (ii)~for every odd $a$, $P_a(z)^2 = (1 + z^a + z^{32})^2 \equiv z^{2a} \pmod{z^{64}+1}$, so $\mathcal{L}_a(\mathcal{L}_a(m)) \equiv \mathrm{RotL}_{2a}(m)$ with $\mathcal{B}(\mathcal{L}_a) = 4$; (iii)~$q^{(0)} \oplus q^{(1)} = s \wedge p_{\mathrm{eff}}$ while each share $q^{(b)}$ is statistically independent of $(s, p_{\mathrm{eff}})$ across all $2^5$ states; and (iv)~user-space attenuation~\eqref{eq:macaroon_att} is strictly lattice-monotonic ($(\mathrm{Perms}' \wedge \neg\mathrm{Perms}_{\mathrm{parent}}) = \mathbf{0}$), and XORing the pre-permutation root $\tau_{\mathrm{parent}}$ via Davies--Meyer feed-forward destroys unkeyed backward invertibility (preventing an untrusted Decode GPU holding public $K_{\mathrm{ctx}}$ from inverting $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}^{(8)}$ to recover $\tau_{\mathrm{parent}}$), while Cycle~0 on the trusted memory controller---which already derives $\tau_{\mathrm{parent}}$ forward from $K_{\mathrm{active}}$---verifies $\tau_{\mathrm{in}} == \tau_{\mathrm{child}}$ purely in the forward direction in $<2\text{~ns}$ with zero syscalls (Lean~4 \texttt{thm23}, \texttt{thm24}, \texttt{thm29}, Table~\ref{tab:lean} Row~7 and Table~\ref{tab:lean_part2} Rows~16 and~20).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:coprime_chi}]
On odd cycle $n = 2m+1$ with $z_i = y_i \oplus (\neg y_{i+1} \wedge y_{i+2})$, $y_{i+2} = 1 \implies z_{i+1} = y_{i+1}$, so $(\neg y_{i+1} \wedge y_{i+2}) = (\neg z_{i+1} \wedge y_{i+2})$; iterating yields $y_i = z_i \oplus \bigoplus_{k=1}^m (z_{i+2k} \wedge \bigwedge_{j=1}^k \neg z_{i+2j-1})$ of degree $m+1 = (n+1)/2$. Claim~(ii) follows from $(1+z^a+z^{32})^2 \equiv z^{2a} \pmod{z^{64}+1}$, Claim~(iii) follows because $z$ blinds $s^{(b)} \wedge p_{\mathrm{eff}}^{(1-b)}$ prior to the flip-flop barrier $\mathrm{Reg}(\cdot)$, and Claim~(iv) follows because $(p \wedge m) \wedge \neg p = \mathbf{0}$ and stripping the feed-forward mask $\mathcal{X}_{31,33}^{(8)}(\tau_{\mathrm{parent}}, K_{\mathrm{ctx}}) = \tau_{\mathrm{child}} \oplus \tau_{\mathrm{parent}}$ prior to applying $(\mathcal{X}_{31,33}^{(8)})^{-1}$ requires prior knowledge of $\tau_{\mathrm{parent}}$ itself, whereas forward verification evaluates Eq.~\eqref{eq:macaroon_att} directly from $\tau_{\mathrm{parent}}$ (\texttt{thm29}).
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Shared-MAC Control-Plane Auditing, Credit Equalization, and Pipeline Composition}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; If an inline sanitizer releases CXL Link-Layer RX credits earlier on aborted slots (e.g., at Stage~1) than on authorized slots (at Stage~4), an external observer monitoring reverse-link credit return timestamps can infer whether co-tenant memory accesses succeeded or failed. Furthermore, during control-plane DAG certificate auditing, a naive XOR accumulator allows an attacker to cancel a vertex birth tag by submitting an even number of duplicate edge copies. Theorems~\ref{thm:dag}--\ref{thm:pipeline_comp} eliminate both vulnerabilities: multiplying each fan-out copy by distinct monomials $x^{j_{\mathrm{off}}} \in \mathrm{GF}(2^{64})^\times$ makes partial/even-copy cancellation algebraically impossible, while delaying all RX credit releases uniformly to Stage~4 ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$, $\mathrm{Var}(\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}}) \equiv 0\text{~ps}^2$) guarantees $0.000\text{~bits}$ of reverse credit-timing mutual information.

\begin{theorem}[Zero-BRAM Control-Plane DAG Auditing with 1-Cycle $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$ Monomial Copy Ratchet]
\label{thm:dag}
Reusing the shared \SharedCubicMacLUTs-LUT $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})$ MAC kernel during control-plane certificate auditing over $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64}) \cong \mathbb{F}_2[x]/(x^{64} + x^4 + x^3 + x + 1)$ to commit step $v$'s $d^+(v) \in \{1,\ldots,8\}$ fan-out copies in 1~clock cycle via
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:monomial_dag}
\Delta S_{\mathrm{birth}}(v) = \left(\bigoplus_{j_{\mathrm{off}}=0}^{d^+(v)-1} x^{j_{\mathrm{off}}}\right) \odot \tau_0(v)
\end{equation}
inside a 64-bit sliding window $B_{\mathrm{mask}} \in \mathbb{F}_2^{64}$ ($\mathrm{cw}(\mathcal{D}) \le 64$) guarantees: (i)~acyclicity via $t_{\mathrm{birth}} < t_{\mathrm{use}}$; (ii)~unconditional invertibility against same-vertex partial/even-copy cancellation since every non-zero polynomial $p_{v,S}(x)$ of degree $\le 7 < 64$ is a unit in $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})^\times$; and (iii)~multi-vertex forgery resistance under $\mathcal{X}_{K_{\mathrm{mix}}}^{(8)}$ PRF security (Lean~4 \texttt{thm07}, \texttt{thm14}, \texttt{thm18}).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:dag}]
Strict order $t_{\mathrm{birth}} < t_{\mathrm{use}}$ prevents cycles, and any $S \neq \{0,\ldots,d^+(v)-1\}$ leaves a non-zero residual $p_{v,S}(x)$ of degree $\le 7 < 64$, which is coprime to $x^{64}+x^4+x^3+x+1$ and invertible in $\mathrm{GF}(2^{64})^\times$ (\texttt{thm18}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Stage-4 Link-Credit Non-Interference and Reverse-Channel Decoupling]
\label{thm:credit}
Releasing CXL Link-Layer RX credits (\texttt{m\_pcie\_crd\_pulse\_c4\_o[k]}) at Stage~4 via \texttt{crd\_pipe\_q[0:3]} ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$, 3~cycles after Stage~1 for both $\mathrm{viol}_k = 0$ and $\mathrm{viol}_k = 1$) while gating $\texttt{WSTRB}_k = \texttt{16'h0000}$ guarantees $(\mathrm{crd\_req}_k \wedge \mathrm{viol}_k) \oplus (\mathrm{crd\_req}_k \wedge \neg\mathrm{viol}_k) \equiv \mathrm{crd\_req}_k$ ($\mathrm{Var}(\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}}) \equiv 0\text{~ps}^2$) and:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:credit_mi}
I\bigl(\mathrm{viol}_{1:t}; \mathcal{F}_t^{\mathrm{crd,egress}} \;\big|\; \Sigma_{1:t}, \mathrm{Valid}_{1:t}\bigr) \equiv 0\text{~bits}.
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:credit}]
In $(\mathbb{F}_2, \oplus, \wedge)$, for every slot $k \in \{0,1,2,3\}$:
\[
(\mathrm{crd\_req}_k \wedge \mathrm{viol}_k) \oplus (\mathrm{crd\_req}_k \wedge \neg\mathrm{viol}_k)
  = \mathrm{crd\_req}_k \wedge (\mathrm{viol}_k \oplus \neg\mathrm{viol}_k)
  = \mathrm{crd\_req}_k,
\]
so Stage-4 credit pulse emission is invariant to $\mathrm{viol}_k$ ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$ and $\mathrm{Var}(\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}}) \equiv 0\text{~ps}^2$). Combining this deterministic credit schedule with Theorem~\ref{thm:egress} via the chain rule of conditional mutual information establishes Eq.~\eqref{eq:credit_mi} (Lean~4 \texttt{thm08}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Compositional 4-Cycle Pipeline State--Transfer Correctness]
\label{thm:pipeline_comp}
Unfolding $\Phi_{\mathrm{pipe}} := \Phi_{\mathrm{S4}} \circ \Phi_{\mathrm{S3}} \circ \Phi_{\mathrm{S2}} \circ \Phi_{\mathrm{S1}}$ across all 4~slots for every cycle $t \ge 0$ and slot $i \in \{0,1,2,3\}$ yields:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:pipeline_transfer}
\Phi_{\mathrm{pipe},i}^{(t)} =
\begin{dcases}
\bigl(\mathcal{Q}_{c_i}(s_i^{(t)}), \texttt{WSTRB}_i^{(t)}, \mathrm{crd}_i^{(t)}\bigr), &\quad\text{if } \mathrm{viol}_i^{(t)}=0 \vee \mathrm{byp}_i^{(t)}=1,\\
\bigl(\texttt{CXL\_NOP}_i, \texttt{16'h0}, \mathrm{crd}_i^{(t)}\bigr), &\quad\text{if } \mathrm{viol}_i^{(t)}=1\text{ (\texttt{M2S\_RwD} hdr)},\\
\bigl(\texttt{128'h0}, \texttt{16'h0}, \mathrm{crd}_i^{(t)}\bigr), &\quad\text{if } \mathrm{viol}_i^{(t)}=1\text{ (\texttt{RwD}/\texttt{DRS} data)},\\
\bigl(\{\mathbf{0}, \mathrm{Tag}_i\}, \texttt{16'h0}, \mathrm{crd}_i^{(t)}\bigr), &\quad\text{if } \mathrm{viol}_i^{(t)}=1\text{ (\texttt{S2M\_DRS}/\texttt{NDR} hdr)},\\
\bigl(\{\mathbf{0}_{46}, \mathrm{Tag}_i\}, \texttt{16'h0}, \mathrm{crd}_i^{(t)}\bigr), &\quad\text{if } \mathrm{viol}_i^{(t)}=1\text{ (\texttt{M2S\_Req} hdr)},
\end{dcases}
\end{equation}
where $\mathrm{byp}_i^{(t)} := \mathrm{birsp}_i^{(t)} \vee \mathrm{snoop\_evict}_i^{(t)}$, with $\sigma_{\mathrm{cap}}^{(t+1)} \le \sigma_{\mathrm{cap}}^{(t)}$, zero unauthorized HBM writes, $100\%$ innocent slot survival, zero host MSHR hangs, and zero self-inflicted link NAKs.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:pipeline_comp}]
Unfolding the registered stage transfer maps $\Phi_{\mathrm{S1}},\ldots,\Phi_{\mathrm{S4}}$ across slots $i \in \{0,1,2,3\}$ together with Theorem~\ref{thm:lut_sram}, Theorem~\ref{thm:credit}, and Corollary~\ref{cor:unwinding} establishes Eq.~\eqref{eq:pipeline_transfer} (Lean~4 \texttt{thm06}, \texttt{thm08}, \texttt{thm09}, \texttt{thm16}, \texttt{thm19}, \texttt{thm29}).
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch~---~Zero-Axiom Digital Kernel Proofs vs.\ Physical Analog Matter).}\;Echoing von~Neumann and Landauer's warnings on Page~1~\cite{landauer1961irreversibility}, machine-checking \LeanTheoremTotal\ theorems in pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 (\texttt{0 sorry}, \texttt{0 axioms}) and proving per-cone CNF/LRAT Miter \texttt{UNSAT} guarantees that our synthesized digital logic gates obey the algebraic specification---not that the algebraic specification captures every analog failure mode of physical matter. Deep-submicron substrate coupling, post-route parasitic capacitance asymmetry ($C_{\mathrm{true}} \neq C_{\mathrm{comp}}$), and multi-spot laser fault injection beyond 2-share DOM (\texttt{thm24}) remain governed by physical silicon integration hygiene rather than theorem provers.

\section{Operator-to-Netlist Traceability, Inductive \texorpdfstring{\texttt{ZK-IC3}}{ZK-IC3} Equivalence, Composable Extensions, and \texorpdfstring{\texttt{SKILL.md}}{SKILL.md} Certifying Compiler (\texorpdfstring{\texttt{spectralc}}{spectralc})}
\label{sec:traceability}

\subsection{Operator-to-Netlist Traceability and Two-Tier DFARS \texorpdfstring{\texttt{ZK-LEC} / \texttt{ZK-IC3}}{ZK-LEC / ZK-IC3} Attestation}
Table~\ref{tab:traceability} maps each operator to its physical netlist primitive on \texttt{xcvu47p-fsvh2892-2L-e}. Whereas prior formal hardware verification frameworks such as MIT Kami~\cite{choi2017kami} verify high-level parametric RTL specifications in Coq assuming an open source-to-gate flow, under Pillar-4 OPSEC (which blocks \texttt{.sv}, \texttt{.v}, \texttt{.edf}, and \texttt{.dcp} files in Zone~2 (\id{02_Public_GitHub_Repo})), all synthesizable RTL and post-route netlists reside in Zone~1 (\id{01_Private_DFARS_Vault}) under DFARS 252.227-7018 and DEAR 952.227-14, while Zone~2 publishes \id{reports_public/ZK_LEC_MITER_RECEIPT.json} bridging our zero-axiom \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 proofs~\cite{demoura2021lean4} to the physical post-route gate netlist under a two-tier verification contract:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Tier 1 --- Exhaustive Per-Cone CNF/LRAT Miter UNSAT, 1-Step Inductive \texttt{IC3} Invariance, \& 65,536-Cycle Trace Attestation:} Anchored to the cryptographic commitment $h_{\mathrm{netlist}} = \mathrm{SHA\text{-}256}(\mathrm{EDIF}_{\mathrm{vault}})$, each registered combinational logic cone $k$ of fan-in width $w_k \le 24$ (Stage-1 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} sieve, Cycle-0 Vieta/Steiner/Time-Wheel evaluator, Stage-2 \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} MOESI and 4-tier mantissa clamps, and PHY-gated chord mux) is translated via Tseitin transformation into a miter formula $\mathcal{M}_k(x) := [\mathrm{EDIF}_{\mathrm{vault},k}(x) \neq \mathrm{Spec}_{\mathrm{Lean4},k}(x)]$ proved UNSAT across $100\%$ of its $2^{w_k}$ input states via a machine-checkable LRAT resolution proof $\pi_{\mathrm{LRAT},k}$, paired with 1-step inductive sequential invariance certificates $(\pi_{\mathrm{init}}, \pi_{\mathrm{ind}}, \pi_{\mathrm{bad}})$ and a 65,536-cycle sequential trace digest verifying multi-flit $Q_{\mathrm{RwD}}$ FIFO rollover and Per-\texttt{ARID} scrub-shim ordering without exposing any Zone-1~\texttt{.sv} or~\texttt{.edf} source in Zone~2.
  \item \textbf{Tier 2 --- Commit-and-Prove Inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} Relation ($\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{ZK\text{-}IC3}}$):} For zero-trust semiconductor IP licensing where even $\pi_{\mathrm{LRAT},k}$ clause topologies remain confidential, Theorem~\ref{thm:zk_ic3} extends combinational \texttt{ZK-LEC} to unbounded sequential horizons $t \in \mathbb{N}$.
\end{enumerate}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; In defense and commercial semiconductor IP licensing, a fundamental tension exists between intellectual-property protection and supply-chain assurance: releasing cleartext post-route SystemVerilog (\texttt{.sv}) or EDIF (\texttt{.edf}) netlists exposes proprietary physical floorplans to cloning, yet withholding the netlist forces the customer to trust that the synthesized silicon contains no sequential ``time-bomb'' hardware Trojans that trigger after $10^9$ clock cycles. Theorem~\ref{thm:zk_ic3} resolves this dilemma by formulating a commit-and-prove inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} relation $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{ZK\text{-}IC3}}$ anchored to the SHA-256 hash of the private post-route EDIF netlist: by proving 1-step inductive consecution of the state invariant $\mathcal{I}(S)$ alongside Miter UNSAT against the public Lean~4 specification, the verifier obtains mathematical certainty of infinite-horizon ($t \rightarrow \infty$) Trojan non-reachability while zero cleartext netlist gates cross the air-gap.

\begin{theorem}[\texttt{ZK-IC3} Infinite-Horizon Sequential Trojan Non-Reachability]
\label{thm:zk_ic3}
Let $C_{\mathrm{edf}}$ denote the SHA-256-committed Zone-1 post-route~\texttt{.edf} netlist with sequential FDCE register state $S = (P_{\mathrm{CSR}}, \mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}}, Q_{\mathrm{RwD}}, \mathrm{lock}_{\mathrm{eg}}, S_{\mathrm{dp}}) \in \mathbb{F}_2^{n_s}$, primary inputs $X \in \mathbb{F}_2^{n_x}$, Tseitin-compiled transition relation $T_{\mathrm{edf}}(S,X,S') := \mathrm{Tseitin}(C_{\mathrm{edf}})$, and public Lean~4 reference specification $C_{\mathrm{spec}}$ with abstraction map $\pi_{\mathrm{abs}}$ and miter safety violation predicate:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:bad_miter}
\mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}(S,X) := \bigl[\mathrm{Out}_{\mathrm{edf}}(S,X) \neq \mathrm{Out}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\pi_{\mathrm{abs}}(S),X)\bigr]
\vee \bigl[(P'_{\mathrm{CSR}} \wedge \neg P_{\mathrm{CSR}}) \neq \mathbf{0}\bigr].
\end{equation}
Define the explicit 1-step inductive invariant $\mathcal{I}(S)$ over the sequential control, 8-way replicated violation, and FIFO registers:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:ic3_invariant}
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal{I}(S) :\iff\;& \bigl(0 \le \mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}} \le 32\bigr)
\wedge \Bigl(\forall j \in \{\mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}}, \ldots, 31\},\; Q_{\mathrm{RwD}}[j] = 0\Bigr)\\
&\wedge \bigl(P_{\mathrm{CSR}} \preceq P_{\mathrm{CSR}}^{(0)}\bigr) \wedge \bigl(\pi_{\mathrm{abs}}(S_{\mathrm{edf}}) = S_{\mathrm{spec}}\bigr)
\wedge \bigwedge_{i=0}^3 \bigl(\texttt{s1\_slot\_viol\_rep\_q}[i] \in \{\texttt{8'h00}, \texttt{8'hFF}\}\bigr).
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
Consider the commit-and-prove NP-relation:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:zklec_np}
\begin{aligned}
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{ZK\text{-}IC3}} := \Bigl\{\bigl(&(h_{\mathrm{netlist}}, C_{\mathrm{spec}});\, (C_{\mathrm{edf}}, \mathcal{I}, \pi_{\mathrm{init}}, \pi_{\mathrm{ind}}, \pi_{\mathrm{bad}})\bigr) :\\
&\mathrm{SHA\text{-}256}(C_{\mathrm{edf}}) = h_{\mathrm{netlist}} \;\wedge\; T_{\mathrm{edf}} = \mathrm{Tseitin}(C_{\mathrm{edf}})\\
&\wedge\; \mathrm{VerifyLRAT}\bigl(\mathrm{Init}(S) \wedge \neg\mathcal{I}(S),\, \pi_{\mathrm{init}}\bigr) = 1\\
&\wedge\; \mathrm{VerifyLRAT}\bigl(\mathcal{I}(S) \wedge T_{\mathrm{edf}}(S,X,S') \wedge \neg\mathcal{I}(S'),\, \pi_{\mathrm{ind}}\bigr) = 1\\
&\wedge\; \mathrm{VerifyLRAT}\bigl(\mathcal{I}(S) \wedge \mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}(S,X),\, \pi_{\mathrm{bad}}\bigr) = 1\Bigr\}.
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
If a prover attests $(\pi_{\mathrm{init}}, \pi_{\mathrm{ind}}, \pi_{\mathrm{bad}})$ (locally via CNF/LRAT in Tier~1 or inside a VOLE-ZK/STARK circuit in Tier~2), then across all unbounded clock cycles $t \in \mathbb{N}$ ($t \rightarrow \infty$) and all input sequences $\{X^{(t)}\}_{t=0}^{\infty}$: (i)~$\mathcal{I}(S^{(t)})$ holds for all $t \ge 0$; (ii)~$\mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}(S^{(t)}, X^{(t)}) \equiv 0$, guaranteeing exact cycle-accurate observational equivalence and \textbf{zero reachable sequential ``time-bomb'' Trojan or $0 \rightarrow 1$ privilege-escalation states}; and (iii)~zero cleartext~\texttt{.sv} or~\texttt{.edf} netlist structure is disclosed across the DFARS air-gap.
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:zk_ic3}]
Soundness of propositional resolution in $\mathrm{VerifyLRAT}$ together with $T_{\mathrm{edf}} = \mathrm{Tseitin}(C_{\mathrm{edf}})$ guarantees that: (a)~$\pi_{\mathrm{init}}$ proves $\mathrm{Init}(S^{(0)}) \implies \mathcal{I}(S^{(0)})$ at cold reset ($\mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}}^{(0)} = 0, Q_{\mathrm{RwD}}^{(0)} = \mathbf{0}_{32}$); (b)~$\pi_{\mathrm{ind}}$ proves 1-step consecution $\mathcal{I}(S^{(t)}) \wedge T_{\mathrm{edf}}(S^{(t)}, X^{(t)}, S^{(t+1)}) \implies \mathcal{I}(S^{(t+1)})$ (preserved by non-destructive overflow clamping $\mathrm{cnt}_{\mathrm{RwD}}^{(t+1)} \le 32$, 8-way fanout replication, and feedback AND-ratcheting $P_{\mathrm{CSR}}^{(t+1)} = P_{\mathrm{CSR}}^{(t)} \wedge w \preceq P_{\mathrm{CSR}}^{(t)}$); and (c)~by mathematical induction on $t \in \mathbb{N}$, every reachable state $S^{(t)}$ satisfies $\mathcal{I}(S^{(t)})$, which by $\pi_{\mathrm{bad}}$ is disjoint from $\mathrm{Bad}_{\mathrm{miter}}$.
\end{proof}
\global\specresumefalse\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{Composable Zero-BRAM Extensions, Tropical-Holonomy Pod Federation, and WDDL Perimeter Dichotomy}

\BeginConnesPlate
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; When federating multiple sovereign AI pods across long-haul optical CXL/scale-up links, each pod administrator must enforce end-to-end capability intersection $\Gamma_{ABC} = T_A \wedge T_B \wedge T_C$ and size optical deskew credit buffers without disclosing internal switch topologies $|S|$ to neighboring pods. Furthermore, architects must decide where to deploy single-rail cut-through logic versus dual-rail precharge logic (WDDL). Theorems~\ref{thm:pod_gluing}--\ref{thm:extensions} and Proposition~\ref{prop:wddl_dichotomy} prove that exporting only the boundary-to-boundary Kleene-star closure $(D_{B,B}^*, T_{B,B}^*)$ over the capability-annihilated Tropical-Boolean dioid $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{trop}}$ conceals $100\%$ of interior switch nodes while sizing optical deskew buffers exactly via $B^* = \lceil(\mathrm{RTT}_{\max}+\Delta_{\mathrm{skew}})/t_{\mathrm{flit}}\rceil$, and establish the exact $50\%$ duty-cycle / $\ge 4\times$ switching-energy dichotomy separating intra-pod cut-through pools from cross-domain \texttt{RAIN} perimeter gateways.

\begin{theorem}[Zero-Topology-Disclosure Federated Pod Gluing \& Shadow IDE Key Filtration]
\label{thm:pod_gluing}
Across pods $G_A, G_B, G_C$ interconnected via gateways $\Sigma_{AB}, \Sigma_{BC}, \Sigma_{CA}$ with chord-ratcheted interior edges $\tilde{G}_{a,b}^{\mathrm{int}} := G_{a,b}^{\mathrm{int}} \wedge \Gamma_{a,b}$: (i)~over $(\mathbb{F}_2^{64}, \vee, \wedge)$, each pod exports only its boundary transfer summary $T_{u,v} = \tilde{G}_{u,v}^{\mathrm{dir}} \vee \bigvee_{s_1, s_2 \in S} (\tilde{G}_{u,s_1}^{\mathrm{int}} \wedge (\tilde{G}_{S,S}^{\mathrm{int}})_{s_1,s_2}^* \wedge \tilde{G}_{s_2,v}^{\mathrm{int}})$, eliminating $100\%$ of interior switch nodes while gating gateways by $\Gamma_\Sigma = T_A \wedge T_B$ and $\Gamma_{ABC} = T_A \wedge T_B \wedge T_C$; (ii)~shared boundary syndromes cancel via $(c_A \oplus c_\Sigma) \oplus (c_B \oplus c_\Sigma) = c_A \oplus c_B$; and (iii)~staging keys into the inactive bank via \texttt{CSR 0x10/0x18} leaves the active bank invariant (\texttt{thm12}, \texttt{thm13}).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:pod_gluing}]
Boolean absorption $a \vee (a \wedge b) = a$ absorbs walks of length $\ge |S|$ into simple paths of length $\le \max(0, |S|-1)$, and $c_\Sigma \oplus c_\Sigma = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ cancels shared boundary syndromes (\texttt{thm12}, \texttt{thm13}).
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Multi-Rail Tropical-Holonomy Pod Federation \& Optical Deskew Credit Sufficiency]
\label{thm:tropical_pod}
Lift each directed edge $e = (u,v)$ of a multi-pod interconnect $G = G_A \cup G_B \cup G_C$ to the capability-annihilated product dioid $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{trop}} := (\mathbb{R}_{\max,+} \cup \{-\infty\}) \times \mathcal{D}_{64}$ with edge weight $W_e = (d_e, \pi_e, \Gamma_e^{\mathrm{active}}(p_{\mathrm{phy}}))$, where $d_e > 0$ is the physical propagation delay, $\pi_e \in S_{64}$ is the switch HDM permutation, $\Gamma_e^{\mathrm{active}}(p_{\mathrm{phy}})$ is the PHY-gated $(T_0,T_1)$ orbit-clamped mask of Eq.~\eqref{eq:phy_failover}, and any walk $P$ whose cumulative capability mask vanishes ($\Gamma(P) = \mathbf{0}_{64}$) is annihilated to tropical delay~$-\infty$. Then:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Zero-Interior-Leakage Tropical-Boolean Closure:} Exporting only the boundary-to-boundary Kleene-star closure $(D_{B,B}^*, T_{B,B}^*)$ over $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{trop}}$ (where $D_{u,v}^* = \max_{P \in \mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{simple}}(u,v) : \Gamma(P) \neq \mathbf{0}_{64}} \sum_{e \in P} d_e$ over capability-admissible simple chord-clamped paths and $T_{u,v}^* = \bigsqcap_{w \in P_{T_{\mathrm{active}}}(u,v)} x_w$) leaks $0$ bits of interior switch count $|S|$ or interior topology while enforcing $\Gamma_{ABC} = T_A \wedge T_B \wedge T_C$ across $p_{\mathrm{phy}} \in \{0,1\}$ in $<4.0\text{~ns}$; and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Exact Multi-Lane Deskew Credit Buffer Bound ($B^*$):} Across parallel optical/retimer lanes with inter-lane skew $\Delta_{\mathrm{skew}} = \max_\ell d_\ell - \min_\ell d_\ell$, round-trip latency $\mathrm{RTT}_{\max}$, and flit period $t_{\mathrm{flit}} = 4.0\text{~ns}$, sizing the Stage-4 link-credit deskew buffer to
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:tropical_buffer}
  B^* := \left\lceil \frac{\mathrm{RTT}_{\max} + \Delta_{\mathrm{skew}}}{t_{\mathrm{flit}}} \right\rceil
  \end{equation}
  guarantees $100\%$ line-rate link utilization ($\eta = 1$) with zero credit-starvation bubbles and $0.000\text{~bits}$ of reverse credit-timing leakage ($\Delta t_{\mathrm{CRD}} \equiv 0$, Theorem~\ref{thm:credit}).
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:tropical_pod}]
Under orbit chord clamping $\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb}}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi}(i)), any cyclic walk traversing $e^*$ is dominated in capability by the simple tree path $P_{T_{\mathrm{active}}}(u,v)$ and prevented from looping by anti-hairpinning and TTL bounds, while paths with $\Gamma(P) = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ map to $-\infty$, so the boundary closure $(D_{B,B}^*, T_{B,B}^*)$ depends only on boundary port pairs $B \times B$, concealing $|S|$. By Little's law in $(\max,+)$ network calculus, the maximum in-flight flit count during round-trip propagation plus multi-lane deskew alignment is $(\mathrm{RTT}_{\max} + \Delta_{\mathrm{skew}})/t_{\mathrm{flit}}$, so $B^*$ credits in Eq.~\eqref{eq:tropical_buffer} prevent sender stalls while Stage-4 constant-delay release (\texttt{thm08}) keeps reverse credit timing data-independent.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[WDDL vs.\ Cut-Through Bandwidth--Power Dichotomy for Exascale Pools vs.\ \texttt{RAIN} Perimeter Gateways]
\label{prop:wddl_dichotomy}
Compare single-rail cut-through sanitization (Modes~A/B.2/C) against Wave Dynamic Differential Logic (WDDL / Mode~D 1-of-2 dual-rail precharge~\cite{gross2016dom}) over a $W$-bit bus at clock frequency $f_{\mathrm{clk}}$ and data activity factor $\alpha_{\mathrm{data}} \in (0, 1/2]$:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Mandatory $50\%$ Duty-Cycle Ceiling and $\ge 2\times$ Switching Energy Floor of WDDL:} Because WDDL alternates between an all-zero precharge phase $(t_i, f_i) = (0,0)$ and a 1-of-2 evaluation phase $(t_i, f_i) \in \{(1,0), (0,1)\}$ on every wire pair $i \in \{1,\ldots,W\}$, every bit lane executes exactly $1$ rising and $1$ falling transition per symbol ($\alpha_{\mathrm{WDDL}} \equiv 1$ toggle/symbol across $2W$ wires). Under matched capacitance $C_{\mathrm{true}} = C_{\mathrm{comp}} = C_0$, first-order data-dependent current variance vanishes ($\mathrm{Var}(\Delta I_{\mathrm{dyn}}^{\mathrm{WDDL}}) = 0$), but the $50\%$ internal flit-phase duty-cycle ceiling halves effective single-clock datapath throughput to $R_{\mathrm{WDDL}} = \frac{1}{2} W f_{\mathrm{clk}}$ and increases dynamic energy per useful bit by $\frac{E_{\mathrm{WDDL}}}{E_{\mathrm{cut}}} = \frac{2}{\alpha_{\mathrm{data}}} \ge 4\times$ (at $\alpha_{\mathrm{data}} \le 0.5$); and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Pareto-Optimal Architectural Partitioning:} Inside physically access-controlled intra-cluster DOE \texttt{AmSC}/ASCR HBM pools, single-pass cut-through (Modes~A/B.2/C) is uniquely Pareto-optimal ($100\%$ line rate, $0\%$ bandwidth tax, \PostRouteDynPowerMW\ FPGA / \AsicTotalPowerMw\ ASIC power), whereas Mode~D (combining 2-share DOM \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} masking \texttt{thm24}, complementary 1-of-2 fault trees \texttt{thm16}, and Vivado \texttt{HD.ISOLATED} SLR fence moats) is deployed strictly at cross-domain NNSA \texttt{RAIN} perimeter gateways~\cite{ncdsmo2020rain} where physical side-channel resistance outweighs the $2\times$ wire/precharge tax.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:wddl_dichotomy}]
In WDDL, each $(t_i, f_i)$ pair transitions $(0,0) \rightarrow (x_i, \neg x_i) \rightarrow (0,0)$ over two clock phases, consuming $2 \times \frac{1}{2} C_0 V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2 = C_0 V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2$ per symbol independent of $x_i \in \{0,1\}$ ($\mathrm{Var}(\Delta I_{\mathrm{dyn}}) = 0$ when $C_{\mathrm{true}} = C_{\mathrm{comp}}$) while transmitting 1~bit every 2~phases ($R_{\mathrm{WDDL}} = \frac{1}{2} W f_{\mathrm{clk}}$), vs.\ $\frac{1}{2}\alpha_{\mathrm{data}} C_0 V_{\mathrm{DD}}^2$ and $R_{\mathrm{cut}} = W f_{\mathrm{clk}}$ for single-rail cut-through.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Modulo-$2^k$ Ripple-Carry Telescoping, 4-Channel 64-Cacheline OOO DMA Ratchet, \& Dual-Rail Fault Trap]
\label{thm:extensions}
Around the core enclave boundary: (i)~over a 4-hop collective ring, $\delta^1(\delta^0 h \oplus \mathrm{skew}) = \mathrm{skew}$ and recursive carry $c_{i+1}(a,b) = (a_i \wedge b_i) \oplus ((a_i \oplus b_i) \wedge c_i)$ ($c_0 = 0$) in $\mathbb{Z}/2^k\mathbb{Z}$ satisfies exact arbitrary-width telescoping $\mathrm{sub}_k(\mathrm{add}_k(\mathrm{acc}, \Delta), \Delta) = \mathrm{acc}_k$ for all $k \in \mathbb{N}$ in 0~BRAM/DSPs (\texttt{thm15}); (ii)~arming 4-channel OOO DMA state $(\mathrm{tag}_{q,c}, \mathrm{seq}_{q,c}, \mathrm{live}_{q,c}) \leftarrow (\mathrm{page}, \mathrm{seq}_{q,c} + \Delta + 1, \mathbf{1}_{64})$ via \texttt{CSR 0x78} only when $\mathrm{live}_{q,c} == \mathbf{0}_{64}$ enforces $\mathrm{seq}_{\mathrm{new},c} > \mathrm{seq}_{q,c}$ and clears $\mathrm{step}(\mathrm{live}_{q,c}, \mathrm{idx})_j := \mathrm{live}_{q,c,j} \wedge \neg\mathrm{eqb}(j, \mathrm{idx})$ on each arriving line $\mathrm{idx} = \mathrm{HPA}[11:6] \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$ to trap any replay beat immediately (\texttt{thm14}); and (iii)~two \texttt{DONT\_TOUCH} reduction trees over $\texttt{O5}, \texttt{O6}$, and \texttt{CSR 0x70} form a complementary 1-of-2 code passing only \texttt{2'b10} while trapping \texttt{2'b00} and \texttt{2'b11} faults in \texttt{CSR 0x48} (\texttt{thm16}).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:extensions}]
Inducting on $i \ge 0$ via \texttt{@Nat.rec} with $b_i = c_i$ gives $d_i = a_i$ and $b_{i+1} = c_{i+1}$ (\texttt{thm15}); Claims~(ii)--(iii) follow from $\mathrm{step}(\mathrm{live}_{q,c},\mathrm{idx})_{\mathrm{idx}} = \mathrm{false}$ and $\texttt{O5} \oplus \texttt{O6} = r_i$ (\texttt{thm14}, \texttt{thm16}).
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\subsection{DOE Genesis \texorpdfstring{\texttt{SKILL.md}}{SKILL.md} / ModCon Policy Homomorphism and Submodular \texorpdfstring{\texttt{AG(2,8)}}{AG(2,8)} Certifying Compilation (\texorpdfstring{\texttt{spectralc}}{spectralc})}
\label{sec:compiler}
In autonomous scientific computing environments---including the DOE Genesis Mission (\id{AI-ModCon/genesis-skills} \texttt{SKILL.md} Markdown YAML frontmatter specifying \texttt{tools}, \id{globus-compute} endpoints, and \id{amsc-data-movement-api} scopes~\cite{doe2025genesis}), Argonne Model-Contract (\texttt{ModCon}) \textit{Agent/Data/Model Cards}, Flux/Slurm HPC job prolog/epilog allocators, Bio-Genesis HIPAA/genomic enclave manifests, and \texttt{SEC Rule 15c3-5} pre-trade risk limits---high-level declarative policies must compile deterministically into hardware-enforced \texttt{BAR0} register images at $+0$ extra silicon gates. We define the lattice homomorphism $\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}} : \mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SKILL}} \rightarrow \mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{BAR0}}$ mapping each declarative agent manifest $\mathcal{M} \in \mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{SKILL}}$ into the 16-CSR \texttt{BAR0} state space:

\BeginConnesPlate
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:modcon_map}
\begin{aligned}
\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}\bigl(\mathcal{M}_1 \sqcap \mathcal{M}_2\bigr) &= \Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}(\mathcal{M}_1) \sqcap_{\mathrm{BAR0}} \Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}(\mathcal{M}_2),\\
\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}(\mathcal{M}) &= \Bigl(\underbrace{P_{\mathrm{coarse}}, P_{\mathrm{fine}}^{[0:1]}, \mathrm{SPID}_{\mathrm{live}}}_{\texttt{\scriptsize CSR 0x00, 0x60, 0x70}}\;\;,\;\;
\underbrace{p_{t,\mathrm{wheel}}[\tau_{\mathrm{TTL}}]}_{\texttt{\scriptsize CSR 0x68 (thm26)}}\;\;,\;\;
\underbrace{c_{\mathrm{MOESI}}, c_{\mathrm{mant}}}_{\texttt{\scriptsize CSR 0x78 (thm22,27)}}\;\;,\;\;
\underbrace{\Sigma_{\mathrm{prov}}}_{\texttt{\scriptsize CSR 0x58 (thm04)}}\Bigr),
\end{aligned}
\end{equation}
\noindent where: (1)~dataset HPA apertures and Globus/AmSC transfer scopes map to coarse/fine masks in \texttt{CSR 0x00/0x60} and tenant bitmaps in \texttt{CSR 0x70}; (2)~autonomous laboratory actuator dead-man TTL leases map to 1-hot bucket masks $\texttt{64'd1} \ll \tau_{\mathrm{TTL}}$ in the \texttt{CSR 0x68} 64-bucket time-wheel (\texttt{thm26}); (3)~Model Card weight-sharing tiers map to Stage-2 fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} MOESI and 4-tier mantissa ceilings $c_i \in \{\texttt{00},\texttt{01},\texttt{10},\texttt{11}\}$ in \texttt{CSR 0x78} (\texttt{thm22}, \texttt{thm27}); and (4)~scientific derivation provenance goals map to the canonical egress digest register \texttt{CSR 0x58} (\texttt{thm04}).

\medskip
\noindent\textbf{Motivation.}\; Assigning autonomous agent lifecycles to $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ affine lines to minimize bit-overlap collisions is NP-hard, and selecting a minimum-weight cycle basis in a multi-hop fabric graph is APX-hard. If the correctness of hardware isolation depended on trusting a complex offline optimization solver inside the compiler, any compiler bug could silently widen tenant privileges. Theorems~\ref{thm:compiler} and~\ref{thm:submodular_modcon} decouple optimization from soundness using McConnell--Mehlhorn--N\"{a}her certifying compilation~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying}: \texttt{spectralc} maximizes a monotone submodular bit-dispersion potential $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$ subject to a per-slope partition matroid $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{slope}}$ ($|S \cap \mathcal{P}_b| \le 7$, preventing 8-line intra-slope wipeout) in $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ time, while the trusted Lean~4 checker (\texttt{thm18}) verifies monotonic mask containment, tree acyclicity, and chord clamping fail-closed in linear $O(|V| + |E|)$ graph time.

\begin{theorem}[Polynomial-Time Soundness of Certifying \texttt{BAR0} Compilation (\texttt{spectralc})~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying}]
\label{thm:compiler}
While offline MSFCB spanning-forest selection (APX-hard) and $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ conflict-hypergraph slope coloring (NP-hard) run outside the TCB, if the Lean~4 certificate checker (\texttt{thm18}, \texttt{thm28}) verifies in $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ time that: (1)~$(M_{\mathrm{new}}[i] \wedge \neg M_{\mathrm{curr}}[i]) = \mathrm{false}$ (and $\mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t)}) \subseteq \mathrm{Roots}(\Lambda^{(t+1)})$); (2)~both primary and secondary failover forests $T_0, T_1 \subset E$ ($|T_b| = |V| - \beta_0$) are acyclic~\cite{tutte1961problem,nashwilliams1961edge}, $\alpha_{u \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_u) = \alpha_{v \trianglelefteq e}^*(x_v)$ on $T_b$, and $H_{C_b(e^*)}(\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb},(b)}) = \Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb},(b)}$ with $(\mathrm{chord}_{e^*}^{(b)}[i] \wedge \neg\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb},(b)}[i]) = \mathrm{false}$ for $b \in \{0,1\}$; and (3)~$\mathrm{wt}(c) = w$ and $\mathrm{wt}(c \wedge c') \le \lambda$ for all $c \neq c' \in \mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}$; then $(r \wedge M_{\mathrm{new}}) \wedge \neg M_{\mathrm{curr}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, $(r \wedge \mathrm{chord}_{e^*}^{(b)}) \wedge \neg\Gamma_{e^*}^{\mathrm{orb},(b)} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$, and $P_{\mathrm{alias}}(R) \equiv 0$ up to $R_{\mathrm{exact}}$ (\texttt{thm18}, \texttt{thm20}, \texttt{thm21}, \texttt{thm28}).
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:compiler}]
Bitwise case analysis on $r[i], M_{\mathrm{new}}[i] \in \{\mathrm{false}, \mathrm{true}\}$ and Theorems~\ref{thm:johnson}(ii), \ref{thm:vieta}, \ref{thm:agreement}, and~\ref{thm:sheaf_moesi} establish $100\%$ soundness in $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ operations~\cite{mcconnell2011certifying}.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Submodular Parallel-Slope Cohort Packing \& Kirchhoff Cycle-Basis Compilation for \texttt{SKILL.md} Manifests]
\label{thm:submodular_modcon}
In \texttt{spectralc}, sub-agents that co-revoke atomically within a single Flux/Slurm batch job share a single cohort codeword, whereas $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{ag}}$ ($|\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{ag}}| = N_{\mathrm{ag}}$) indexes concurrent \emph{independent} \texttt{SKILL.md} agent lifecycles with activity/revocation-risk dispersion weights $\omega_c \in (0,1]$ across hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = (\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{ag}}, \mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{rev}})$. Compiling $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{ag}}$ via $\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}$ onto the 72 lines of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$---partitioned into $m_s = 9$ parallel-slope classes $\mathcal{P}_0, \ldots, \mathcal{P}_8$ of $8$ pairwise bit-disjoint lines each ($\lambda = 0$ intra-slope, $\lambda = 1$ inter-slope, Theorem~\ref{thm:modec}(i)) subject to the per-slope \textbf{partition matroid constraint} $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{slope}} := \{S \subseteq \mathrm{AG}(2,8) : |S \cap \mathcal{P}_b| \le 7\;\forall b \in \{0,\ldots,8\}\}$ (strictly preventing the 8-line intra-slope wipeout $P_{\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$)---while compiling multi-hop fabric routing onto spanning forest $T_{\mathrm{forest}} \subset E$ guarantees:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{NP-Hardness of Exact Slope Partitioning \& Submodular Bit-Dispersion Potential $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$:} Partitioning $\mathcal{H}$ across $\mathcal{P}_0,\ldots,\mathcal{P}_8$ to minimize expected bit-lane overlap collisions is NP-hard (by reduction from hypergraph coloring), and unweighted minimum cycle-basis selection is APX-hard. Define the \textbf{bit-dispersion potential} $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$ and complementary bit-overlap collision penalty $\Omega_{\mathrm{coll}}(S)$ by:
  \begin{equation}
  \label{eq:submodular_dispersion}
  G_{\mathcal{H}}(S) := \sum_{j=0}^{63} \Biggl(1 - \prod_{c \in S : j \in \mathrm{supp}(c)} \bigl(1 - \omega_c\bigr)\Biggr),\qquad
  \Omega_{\mathrm{coll}}(S) := 8 \sum_{c \in S} \omega_c - G_{\mathcal{H}}(S) \ge 0.
  \end{equation}
  Then $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$ is non-negative, monotone, and \textbf{submodular} ($\Delta G_{\mathcal{H}}(c \mid A) \ge \Delta G_{\mathcal{H}}(c \mid B)$ for $A \subseteq B$), and maximizing $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$ for a fixed cohort weight sum $\sum_{c \in S}\omega_c$ is algebraically equivalent to minimizing $\Omega_{\mathrm{coll}}(S)$: for any pair $\{c_1,c_2\}$, bit-disjoint intra-slope lines ($\lambda = 0$) achieve the additive maximum $G_{\mathcal{H}}(\{c_1,c_2\}) = 8\omega_1 + 8\omega_2$ ($\Omega_{\mathrm{coll}} = 0$, e.g., $16\omega$ for equal weights), strictly dominating inter-slope lines ($\lambda = 1$) which score $G_{\mathcal{H}}(\{c_1,c_2\}) = 8\omega_1 + 8\omega_2 - \omega_1\omega_2$ ($\Omega_{\mathrm{coll}} = \omega_1\omega_2 > 0$, i.e., $16\omega - \omega^2$). Over the partition matroid $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{slope}}$, maximizing $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$ admits a $(1 - 1/e) \approx 63.21\%$ randomized continuous-greedy guarantee~\cite{calinescu2011maximizing} and a $1/2$ deterministic greedy guarantee (and $(1 - 1/e)$ deterministic greedy within cardinality-bounded batches $|S| \le 7$) in $O(N_{\mathrm{ag}} |\mathrm{AG}(2,8)|)$ time; and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Kirchhoff Effective-Resistance Cycle Basis \& $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ Certifying Complexity:} Selecting $T_{\mathrm{forest}}$ via Spielman--Srivastava effective-resistance leverage scores $\ell_e = w_e^\Gamma (\delta_a - \delta_b)^T S_\Gamma^{-1}(\delta_a - \delta_b)$ minimizes expected chord-clamping boundary power (Theorem~\ref{thm:power}(i)). Regardless of the offline approximation heuristic used by \texttt{spectralc}, the Lean~4 certificate checker (\texttt{thm18}, Theorem~\ref{thm:compiler}) verifies tree acyclicity and chord-mask dominance in $O(|V| + |E|)$ graph time and pairwise cohort overlap in $O(|\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ time, validating $100\%$ of emitted \texttt{ML-DSA-87}-signed \texttt{BAR0} bundles fail-closed in deterministic $O(|V| + |E| + |\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{assigned}}|^2)$ total time and $O(|V| + |E|)$ space.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:submodular_modcon}]
For each bit coordinate $j \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$, $g_j(S) = 1 - \prod_{c \in S : j \in \mathrm{supp}(c)} (1 - \omega_c)$ has marginal gain on $A \subseteq B$:
\[
g_j(A \cup \{c\}) - g_j(A) = \omega_c \prod_{a \in A : j \in \mathrm{supp}(a)} (1 - \omega_a)
\ge \omega_c \prod_{b \in B : j \in \mathrm{supp}(b)} (1 - \omega_b)
= g_j(B \cup \{c\}) - g_j(B),
\]
for all $j \in \mathrm{supp}(c)$ since $1 - \omega_a \in [0,1)$. Summing over $j \in \{0,\ldots,63\}$ preserves monotonicity and submodularity, and since $\sum_{j=0}^{63} \sum_{c \in S : j \in \mathrm{supp}(c)} \omega_c = 8\sum_{c \in S} \omega_c \ge G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$, maximizing $G_{\mathcal{H}}(S)$ minimizes $\Omega_{\mathrm{coll}}(S)$. Within any parallel slope $\mathcal{P}_b$, $|S \cap \mathcal{P}_b| \le 7$ prevents the 8-line wipeout $P_{\mathrm{fine}} = \mathbf{0}_{64}$ while achieving $\lambda = 0$ ($\Omega_{\mathrm{coll}} = 0$). Submodular maximization bounds over partition matroids~\cite{calinescu2011maximizing,mcconnell2011certifying} and Theorems~\ref{thm:power} and~\ref{thm:compiler} complete the proof.
\end{proof}

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Scope \& Price Paid (No Free Lunch~---~Pod/Job Lifecycle Compilation \& Tier-1 Local LRAT vs.\ Sub-Microsecond Kernel Churn \& Tier-2 Prover Compute).}\;Decoupling NP-hard $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ slope packing and Kirchhoff cycle-basis optimization from linear-time fail-closed certificate checking (\texttt{thm18}, $1.36\text{~ms}$ warm admission gate) is engineered for container, batch-job, and autonomous-agent lifecycles (Kubernetes pods, Flux/Slurm allocations, and DOE \texttt{SKILL.md} tasks); it is not designed to replace sub-microsecond intra-process OS kernel \texttt{mmap}/\texttt{munmap} virtual-memory churn. Similarly, while Tier-1 CNF/LRAT Miter \texttt{UNSAT} and 1-step inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} certificates verify in milliseconds on a standard CPU, wrapping full-shell multi-SLR netlists into zero-knowledge Tier-2 VOLE-ZK/STARK proofs requires offline GPU prover acceleration (Section~\ref{sec:roadmap}, Milestone~2).
