\subsection{Multi-Track Empirical Silicon, Open-Source 7nm ASAP7 Predictive PDK ASIC, and TRL-8 Kubernetes Synthesis}
\label{sec:empirical_synthesis}
Figures~\ref{fig:vieta_scaling}--\ref{fig:k8s_tco} synthesize the four empirical reduction-to-practice tracks sealed in \ReleaseVersion\ (Table~\ref{tab:provenance}):
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Track A --- Open-Source 7nm FinFET \texttt{ASAP7} Predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7} Standard-Cell CMOS Sign-Off (Figure~\ref{fig:silicon_ppa}):} Using \id{OpenROAD-flow-scripts} with parasitic RC extraction (\texttt{OpenRCX} SPEF) and static timing analysis (\texttt{OpenSTA}) on the open-source 7nm FinFET ASAP7 predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7}, the standalone 2-stage sanitizer core (\id{cxl_subflit_slot_sanitizer_core}) synthesizes to \AsicCoreCells\ standard cells (\AsicCoreGe\ NAND2 Gate Equivalents, \AsicCoreAreaMm\ silicon area, $0$ SRAM macros). The placed-and-routed full 4-stage enclave top (\id{cxl_subflit_sanitizer_enclave_top}) closes at \AsicStandardCells\ routed standard cells in \AsicEnclaveAreaUm\ (\AsicEnclaveAreaMm) active area inside a $180.0 \times 180.0\text{~}\mu\text{m}$ 1,460-pin die ring with $0$ SRAM macros, $0$ TritonRoute DRC violations, \AsicSetupWnsPs\ setup WNS, \AsicStageOneTwoDelayPs\ (\AsicFourStageDelayNs) critical path (\AsicFmaxMhz\ / $0.693\text{~GHz}$ $F_{\max} = 2.77\times$ timing margin over the $250.0\text{~MHz}$ CXL clock), and \AsicTotalPowerMw\ total power (\id{reports_public/TRL6_ASIC_7NM_ASAP7_SIGNOFF.json}).
  \item \textbf{Track B.1 --- Live 16nm AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} BIST, Warm-FLR, \& 19,990-Trace Welch TVLA Silicon (Figure~\ref{fig:tvla_dither}):} Inside an account-locked private AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} enclave (public \texttt{loadPermission} disabled) running AFI \texttt{\AwsAgfiId} (\texttt{afi-07282de69eecfd279}, $+1.842\text{~ns}$ post-route WNS at $250.0\text{~MHz}$ / $128.0\text{~Gbps}$), the hardware BIST engine exercised \AwsBistSlots{} sub-flit slots ($400,000$ rogue attacks surgically scrubbed), confirming $\texttt{WSTRB} = \texttt{0x0000FFFF0000FFFF}$, $\texttt{leak\_trap} = 0$, live Stage-2 BF16 mantissa clamping ($\texttt{0x3F803F80}$), and warm PCIe FLR ratchet persistence ($\texttt{CSR[0x70]} = \texttt{0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEB}$). Across 19,990 live hardware traces evaluated under ISO/IEC~17825 Welch's $t$-test, the 2-share DOM \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} enclave achieved $\max|t| = 0.0 < 4.5$ (\id{reports_public/STAGE2_AWS_F2_SILICON_RECEIPT.json}).
  \item \textbf{Track B.2 --- Live 16~GB HBM2 Inline Datapath DMA Silicon on AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} (Figures~\ref{fig:hbm_goodput} and~\ref{fig:silicon_ppa}):} Inside the same account-locked private AWS F2 enclave running Production HBM2 DMA AFI \texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId} (\texttt{afi-0f2ddd7dc87d81193}, PCIe BDF \texttt{0000:34:00.0}, $+0.512\text{~ns}$ post-route WNS) with $16\text{~GB}$ on-package HBM2 calibrated (\texttt{0x0300 = 0x00000006}) and mapped at \texttt{PF0-BAR4} (\texttt{0x1000000000}), the inline sanitizer intercepted \AwsLiveBursts{} 512-bit bursts ($50,128$ HBM flits, \AwsLiveSlots{} inline sub-flit slots), passing $100\%$ of allied tenant HBM2 writes (\texttt{0x5555555555555555}) while preserving $100\%$ of pre-seeded HBM2 victim sentinel words (\texttt{0xAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA}) against revoked-tenant DMA overwrites ($\texttt{leak\_trap} = 0$, $0$ PCIe CTOs, $0$ link NAKs $\texttt{CSR[0x40]} = 0$, $\texttt{CSR[0x48]} = 1$, and live HBM2 BF16 readback clamping $\texttt{0x3F8E3F8E} \rightarrow \texttt{0x3F803F80}$; \id{reports_public/TRL7_AWS_F2_HBM2_DMA_RECEIPT.json}).
  \item \textbf{Track C --- TRL-8 CNCF \texttt{k3s} Kubernetes Admission Webhook HIL (\texttt{spectralc} \ReleaseVersion, Figure~\ref{fig:k8s_tco}):} In a live CNCF \texttt{k3s} cluster running the \texttt{SpectralMemoryPolicy} CRD and cluster-internal \texttt{validate.spectral.io} webhook service FQDN backed by \texttt{spectralc} \ReleaseVersion, a valid multi-tenant revocation manifest (\id{coalition-revoke-tenants-2-4}) was formally verified by the cold Lean~4 kernel in $5,934.19\text{~ms}$, signed with FIPS-204 \texttt{ML-DSA-87} (\id{mldsa87-fips204-det-0e060a832a52b7fab9243a9b2d7d036d}), and committed onto \texttt{CSR[0x70] = 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEB}, whereas a rogue $0 \rightarrow 1$ bit-widening escalation manifest (\texttt{delta = 0x14}) was rejected with HTTP \texttt{403 Forbidden} in $1.36\text{~ms}$---well inside the $200\text{~ms}$ webhook SLA (\id{reports_public/TRL8_K8S_OPERATOR_HIL_RECEIPT.json}).
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Distributed Exascale and Silicon-in-the-Loop Validation Roadmap}
\label{sec:roadmap}
All baseline architectural, formal, and silicon verification milestones are closed and sealed in \ReleaseVersion\ (Table~\ref{tab:provenance}): the \CorePhysicalLUTs-LUT 2-stage sanitizer core, the \ScrubShimPhysicalLUTs-LUT Per-\texttt{ARID} scrub responder shim, the 16-CSR \texttt{BAR0} ABI, all \LeanTheoremTotal\ zero-axiom Lean~4 theorems (\texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm29}), placed-and-routed open-source 7nm FinFET ASAP7 predictive PDK (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t})~\cite{clark2016asap7} standard-cell CMOS sign-off (\AsicCoreCells\ core cells / \AsicStandardCells\ routed enclave cells at \AsicFmaxMhz), live 16nm AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} Dual-AFI execution ($16\text{~GB}$ HBM2 DMA \texttt{\AwsHbmAgfiId} across \AwsLiveBursts{} bursts and 19,990-trace Welch TVLA $\max|t|=0.0 < 4.5$ on \texttt{\AwsAgfiId}), and TRL-8 CNCF \texttt{k3s} Kubernetes admission webhook HIL ($1.36\text{~ms}$ tamper rejection). Building on this foundation, four multi-rack exascale deployment milestones scope our Phase~II national-laboratory roadmap for DOE \texttt{AmSC}/ASCR facilities: (1)~\textbf{10,000-Node Online \texttt{SKILL.md} / Flux Competitive Scheduling:} scaling the $\Psi_{\mathrm{ModCon}}$ submodular $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ compiler (Theorem~\ref{thm:submodular_modcon}) from single-cluster \texttt{k3s} admission control to dynamic online arrival/departure streams of $10^5$ concurrent autonomous agent tasks under Flux/Slurm; (2)~\textbf{Full-Shell VOLE-ZK \texttt{ZK-IC3} Prover Acceleration:} extending Tier-1 local CNF/LRAT verification of $\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{ZK\text{-}IC3}}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:zk_ic3}) to GPU-accelerated VOLE-ZK proof generation across full-chip multi-SLR FPGA shells; (3)~\textbf{Multi-Rack \texttt{gem5} / \texttt{SST} (\texttt{CXL-ClusterSim}) Co-Simulation:} calibrating multi-hop \texttt{S2M\_BISnp} shootdown contention (Proposition~\ref{prop:bisnp_shootdown}) and Tropical $(\max,+)$ deskew buffering (Theorem~\ref{thm:tropical_pod}) under production national-laboratory scientific workflow traces; and (4)~\textbf{Multi-Vendor CXL~3.1/4.0 Hard-IP Interposer Tapeout \& Heavy-Ion Beamline SEU Characterization:} extending the 19,990-trace AWS F2 \texttt{TVLA} baseline to heavy-ion cyclotron beamline fault-injection campaigns on 7nm/3nm HBM3e/HBM4 base-die test chips and Intel Agilex~7 CXL-attached silicon across Modes~A--D.

\section*{Research Methodology, Falsification Flow, and Role of AI Tooling}
To ensure complete methodological transparency and reproducibility, we document the exact division of labor between human architectural conception, large language model (LLM) assistance, and deterministic formal/physical falsification oracles:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Human Architectural Conception \& Reduction to Practice:} All foundational hardware architectures, algebraic constructions, and microarchitectural mechanisms in this monograph---including direct-sum Boolean sub-flit projection ($\Pi_P \oplus \Delta_P = \mathrm{Id}$) mapped onto fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} \texttt{O5}/\texttt{O6} primitives, multiscale $\mathcal{X}_{n+1} = \mathcal{X}_n \oplus \mathcal{W}_n$ address splitting with $\mathbb{F}_8$ Steiner $S(2,8,64)$ and Cycle-0 $\mathbb{F}_{2^8}[Z]$ Vieta polynomial-ideal ratcheting, spanning-forest $S_{64}$ orbit chord clamping with PHY-gated dual-forest failover, Stage-2 $+0$-LUT 4-tier mantissa clamping, dual-subflit split-coset CRC48/CRC64 tunneling, and the Per-\texttt{ARID} ordered AXI4 read-scrub responder shim---were conceived, architected, and reduced to practice by the human author.
  \item \textbf{Role of Large Language Models as Adversarial Sparring Partners:} Frontier LLMs were utilized during manuscript preparation and verification harness development as high-throughput mathematical sparring partners for: (a)~cross-examining proof obligations against classical coding-theory, graph-theory, and stochastic network-calculus literature; (b)~accelerating repetitive Lean~4 term-mode proof scripts, Python trace-simulation harnesses, and LaTeX typographic formatting; and (c)~conducting adversarial ``Reviewer~\#2'' stress-tests to surface boundary cases (such as the $d_{\mathrm{rev}}^{(t)} = 8$ Vieta zero-input guard $\mathrm{cid}_{\mathrm{lo},i} \neq 0_{\mathbb{F}_{2^8}}$, snoop-solicited \texttt{DirtyEvict} vs.\ unsolicited \texttt{MemWr} coherency separation, and counter-seeded dither non-reuse).
  \item \textbf{Deterministic Popperian Falsification Gauntlet (Zero Trust in Probabilistic Outputs):} Because generative models are probabilistic token predictors that frequently hallucinate algebraic constants, finite-field inverses, LUT configuration hex masks, and hardware timing closure, \textbf{zero mathematical, cryptographic, or silicon claims in this work rest on LLM authority}. Every conjecture, equation, \texttt{INIT} bitmask, and RTL state transition was subjected to five deterministic, machine-checked falsification oracles (Table~\ref{tab:provenance}): (i)~the pure \texttt{Init.Prelude} Lean~4 kernel (\LeanTheoremTotal\ theorems \texttt{thm00}--\texttt{thm29} checked with \texttt{0 sorry} and \texttt{0 axioms}); (ii)~exhaustive finite-field and truth-table symbolic sweeps in Python (\id{sim/benchmark_workload_and_martingale.py}); (iii)~65,536-cycle sequential RTL simulation paired with exhaustive per-cone CNF/LRAT Miter UNSAT equivalence ($\mathcal{M}_k(x) \equiv 0$ across $100\%$ of $2^{w_k}$ input states) and 1-step inductive \texttt{ZK-IC3} consecution (\id{ZK_LEC_MITER_RECEIPT.json}); (iv)~AMD Vivado~\VivadoToolVersion\ (\texttt{xcvu47p}) and \texttt{OpenROAD} (\texttt{asap7sc7p5t}) post-route static timing and DRC sign-off (\PostRouteDRCViolations\ DRC violations); and (v)~live hardware execution across \AwsBistSlots{} BIST slots, 19,990 Welch TVLA traces ($\max|t| = 0.0$), and \AwsLiveBursts{} inline 16~GB HBM2 DMA bursts on physical AWS EC2 \texttt{f2.6xlarge} silicon. Any candidate identity or circuit transformation that failed any of these five deterministic oracles was immediately refuted and discarded.
\end{enumerate}

\section{Algebraic and Coding-Theoretic Lemmas}
\label{sec:appendix}

\BeginConnesPlate
\begin{lemma}[Order-Preserving Bitvector Embedding, Pointed Flat Tenant Meet, \& Dynamic Epoch Transition Masking]
\label{lem:embedding}
For any finite security policy meet-semilattice $(P, \preceq, \wedge_P)$: (a)~the downset map $\iota(x)_p = \mathbb{I}(p \preceq x)$ satisfies $\iota(x \wedge_P y) = \iota(x) \wedge \iota(y)$; (b)~the pointed flat meet $\mathrm{ID}_1 \wedge_{\mathrm{flat}} \mathrm{ID}_2 := \mathrm{ID}_1 \cdot \mathbb{I}(\mathrm{ID}_1 = \mathrm{ID}_2)$ on $D_\perp = D \cup \{0\}$ prevents cross-tenant synthesis ($\mathrm{ID}_1 \neq \mathrm{ID}_2 \implies \mathrm{ID}_1 \wedge_{\mathrm{flat}} \mathrm{ID}_2 = 0$); and (c)~latching $\Gamma_{e,\mathrm{trans}} = \Gamma_e^{(t)} \sqcap \Gamma_e^{(t+1)}$ across $G^{(t)} \rightarrow G^{(t+1)}$ preserves $\mathrm{Fix}(\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{cons}}^0)$.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:embedding}]
Immediate from $p \preceq x \wedge_P y \iff (p \preceq x \wedge p \preceq y)$, orthogonality in $D_\perp$, and $\Gamma_{e,\mathrm{trans}} \preceq \Gamma_e^{(t)}, \Gamma_e^{(t+1)}$.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Affine Seed Cancellation, Exact Modulo-3 Alignment, \& Parallel Matrix Folding]
\label{lem:folding}
For any physical generator over $\mathrm{GF}(2^8)$ with $S_{\mathrm{phys}}(M) = S_{\mathrm{lin}}(M) \oplus c_0$, $S_{\mathrm{phys}}(M \oplus \Delta M) = S_{\mathrm{phys}}(M) \oplus S_{\mathrm{lin}}(\Delta M)$. In a 256-byte CXL flit whose 250 message/CRC bytes $i \in \{0,\ldots,249\}$ partition by $m = i \bmod 3 \in \{0,1,2\}$ into sub-blocks of lengths $(84, 83, 83)$ (and full RS codeword lengths $(86, 85, 85)$ across bytes $0,\ldots,255$ including 2 parity bytes per interleave), byte $i \in \{0,\ldots,249\}$ has exponent $d_m(i) = \lfloor (249-i)/3 \rfloor + 2$, parity byte $250 + 3p + r$ belongs to $m = (r+1) \bmod 3$, and over $i \in \{16,\ldots,235\}$, $\tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)}$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:h_tilde_def} satisfies:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:matrix_folding}
\left(\bigoplus_{\substack{i=16 \\ i \equiv m \pmod 3}}^{235} H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)} \delta_i\right)
  \oplus \left(\bigoplus_{\substack{j=0 \\ (242+j) \equiv m \pmod 3}}^7 H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(242+j)} \Delta C_j^{\mathrm{total}}\right)
= \left(\bigoplus_{i=16}^{235} \tilde{H}_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(i)} \delta_i\right)
  \oplus \left(\bigoplus_{\substack{j=0 \\ (242+j) \equiv m \pmod 3}}^7 H_{\mathrm{FEC},m}^{(242+j)} \tau_{\mathrm{out},j}\right),
\end{equation}
reducing $\mathbb{F}_2$ XOR-tree depth from 16 serial stages to 10--11 parallel stages.
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:folding}]
Since $250 \equiv 1 \pmod 3$, parity byte $250 + 3p + r$ has residue $(1+r) \bmod 3$. Substituting $\Delta C_j^{\mathrm{total}}$ from Eq.~\eqref{eq:delta_crc} into the left-hand side of Eq.~\eqref{eq:matrix_folding} and interchanging the finite $\mathbb{F}_2$-linear summations yields the right-hand side.
\end{proof}

\begin{lemma}[Concatenated Coset Subcode Distance \& Vandermonde Non-Aliasing Bound]
\label{lem:bch}
For the $[8,4,5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ Reed--Solomon coset subcode $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{tag}} = \mathrm{im}(G_{\mathrm{tag}})$ ($H_{\mathrm{tag}} \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^{4 \times 8}$): (i)~any channel error $N \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^{256}$ with $\le 1$ byte error per modulo-3 interleave is uniquely corrected by the outer RS-FEC decoder prior to CRC evaluation ($0\%$ FEC margin loss); and (ii)~when $g_{\mathrm{CRC}}(x) = \prod_{k=1}^8 (x + \alpha^k)$ with $G_{\mathrm{tag}}$ spanning $\{\alpha^5,\ldots,\alpha^8\}$, $H_{\mathrm{tag}}H_{\mathrm{CRC}}$ checks $\{\alpha^1,\alpha^2,\alpha^3,\alpha^4\}$ to form a $[250, 246, d_{\mathrm{super}} = 5]_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)}$ BCH supercode that detects $100\%$ of weight-3 outer RS-FEC miscorrections ($\mathrm{wt}(E_{\mathrm{mis}}) \le 4$; $2^{-32}$ burst bound under binary CRC-64).
\end{lemma}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:bch}]
$S_{\mathrm{FEC}}(F') = \mathbf{0}$ and $d_{\min}(\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{FEC}}) = 3$ corrects $t = 1$ symbol per interleave. Any 4 columns $j_1 < j_2 < j_3 < j_4 \in \{0,\ldots,249\}$ of $H_{\mathrm{super}} := H_{\mathrm{tag}}H_{\mathrm{CRC}}$ at consecutive roots $\{\alpha^1,\alpha^2,\alpha^3,\alpha^4\}$ of primitive element $\alpha \in \mathrm{GF}(2^8)^\times$ ($\mathrm{ord}(\alpha) = 255 > 250$) form a generalized Vandermonde matrix $V = [\alpha^{r \cdot j_c}]_{1 \le r, c \le 4}$ with determinant:
\begin{equation}
\label{eq:vandermonde_bch}
\det(V) = \left(\prod_{c=1}^4 \alpha^{j_c}\right) \prod_{1 \le a < b \le 4} \bigl(\alpha^{j_b} \oplus \alpha^{j_a}\bigr) \neq 0_{\mathrm{GF}(2^8)},
\end{equation}
since $0 \le j_a < j_b < 250 < 255 \implies \alpha^{j_b} \neq \alpha^{j_a}$. Hence every 4 columns of $H_{\mathrm{super}}$ are linearly independent over $\mathrm{GF}(2^8)$, forcing $d_{\mathrm{super}} \ge 5$~\cite{macwilliams1977theory}.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[Optimality of $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ at 64 Bits \& Hermitian Curve $\mathcal{H}_q$ Hasse--Weil Bus-Widening Gain]
\label{prop:hermitian_ag}
Compare genus-0 Reed--Solomon / affine-plane constructions against algebraic-geometry (AG) one-point codes over the Hermitian curve $\mathcal{H}_q : y^q + y = x^{q+1}$ of genus $g = q(q-1)/2$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ under 1-hot symbol-to-bitmap embedding:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item[(i)] \textbf{Exact Optimality of Genus-0 $\mathrm{AG}(2,8) \cong S(2,8,64)$ at $v = 64$ Bits:} At the frozen 64-bit \texttt{BAR0} width ($w = 8, \lambda = 1$), $\mathrm{AG}(2,8)$ achieves the Johnson bound $A(64,14,8) = \lfloor \frac{64}{8}\lfloor \frac{63}{7}\rfloor \rfloor = 72$ with exact equality; no higher-genus curve can exceed 72 codewords at $v=64, w=8, \lambda=1$; and
  \item[(ii)] \textbf{Hermitian Hasse--Weil Excess on Widened $256\text{-Bit}$ and Projected $1,024\text{-Bit}$ Buses (\mbox{CXL~3.0--4.0} (and projected \mbox{CXL~5.0})~\cite{cxl2025spec} / \mbox{UCIe~2.0--3.0}~\cite{ucie2025spec30}):} On a widened $v = 256\text{-bit}$ capability bitmap partitioned into $n_{\mathrm{sym}} = 27$ 1-hot 9-bit symbol lanes over $\mathbb{F}_9 = \mathbb{F}_{3^2}$ ($q = 3$, using $27 \times 9 = 243 \le 256$ bits and retaining $13$ guard/prefix bits), a genus-0 extended Reed--Solomon code over $\mathbb{F}_9$ is limited to $q^2 = 9$ evaluation points, whereas the Hermitian curve $\mathcal{H}_3 : y^3 + y = x^4$ over $\mathbb{F}_9$ ($g = 3$) attains the Hasse--Weil bound of $q^3 = 27$ affine rational points ($N(\mathcal{H}_3) = 28$ including $P_\infty$), expanding the evaluation length by $3\times$ ($w = 27$). Similarly, on projected $1,024\text{-bit}$ next-generation CXL / scale-up capability-bus extensions ($64 \times 16 = 1,024$ bits under 1-hot $\mathbb{F}_{16}$ lane embedding, or $64 \times 15 = 960 \le 1,024$ bits of constant Hamming weight $512$ when concatenated with a binary $[15,4,8]_2$ simplex inner code), the Hermitian curve $\mathcal{H}_4 : y^4 + y = x^5$ over $\mathbb{F}_{16} = \mathbb{F}_{4^2}$ ($q = 4, g = 6$) attains $q^3 = 64$ affine rational points ($4\times$ the genus-0 Reed--Solomon length $q^2 = 16$), yielding $q^{2 k_{\mathrm{AG}}}$ disjunct cohorts with overlap $\lambda \le \deg(G)$ for $R_{\mathrm{exact}} = \lfloor (q^3 - 1)/\deg(G) \rfloor$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proposition}
\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:hermitian_ag}]
Claim~(i) follows directly from Theorem~\ref{thm:multiscale}(ii). For Claim~(ii), for every $x \in \mathbb{F}_{q^2}$, the norm $x^{q+1} = \mathrm{Norm}_{\mathbb{F}_{q^2}/\mathbb{F}_q}(x) \in \mathbb{F}_q$, and the separable $\mathbb{F}_q$-linear trace map $\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{F}_{q^2}/\mathbb{F}_q}(y) = y^q + y$ is $q$-to-1 onto $\mathbb{F}_q$, yielding $q^2 \cdot q = q^3$ affine rational points ($27$ points requiring $27 \times 9 = 243 \le 256$ bits for $q=3$, and $64$ points requiring $64 \times 16 = 1,024$ bits for $q=4$) plus $P_\infty$, achieving the Hasse--Weil bound $q^2 + 1 + 2g q = q^3 + 1$.
\end{proof}
\EndConnesPlate

\section{Normative SystemVerilog Assertions (\texorpdfstring{\texttt{SVA 1}--\texttt{SVA 10}}{SVA 1--SVA 10})}
\label{app:sva}

Listing~\ref{fig:sva} presents the complete normative IEEE~1800-2017 / P3164 SystemVerilog Assertions (\texttt{SVA 1}--\texttt{SVA 10}) in upright portrait format, verifying cold-POR monotonic ratcheting across dual-banked Boolean and Cycle-0 Vieta/Time-Wheel/Tensor modes, 4-slot surgical sanitization, Stage-4 credit conservation, snoop \texttt{DirtyEvict} bypass, fractured \mbox{\texttt{LUT6\_2}} \texttt{CXL.cache} MOESI and 4-tier mantissa clamping, hitless per-slot dual-epoch grace windows, Per-\texttt{ARID} ordered AXI4 scrub-shim \texttt{ARVALID} suppression, and 4-channel OOO DMA replay annihilation.

\begin{lstlisting}[caption={Normative IEEE 1800-2017 / P3164 SystemVerilog Assertions (SVA 1--SVA 10) for \textsc{Spectral Flit-Filter} (v1.0.0).},label={fig:sva},basicstyle=\ttfamily\scriptsize,columns=fullflexible,keepspaces=true,upquote=true,literate={~}{{$\sim$}}1]
// SVA 1: Unconditional Cold-POR Monotonic BAR0 Ratchet & Warm-FLR Lockdown (0x00/0x08/0x60_bank/0x68/0x70)
property p_monotonic_csr_ratchet_no_escalation_across_flr;
  @(posedge clk_bar_axi) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n || !rot_epoch_rekey_rst_n)
  $past(por_cold_rst_n && rot_epoch_rekey_rst_n)
  |-> (((csr_0x00_spatial_q  & ~$past(csr_0x00_spatial_q))  == 64'h0) &&
       ((csr_0x08_temporal_q & ~$past(csr_0x08_temporal_q)) == 64'h0) &&
       (!vieta_mode_en_q
         ? (((csr_0x60_bank_q[0] & ~$past(csr_0x60_bank_q[0])) == 64'h0) &&
            ((csr_0x60_bank_q[1] & ~$past(csr_0x60_bank_q[1])) == 64'h0))
         : ((vieta_deg_bank_q[0] >= $past(vieta_deg_bank_q[0])) &&
            (vieta_deg_bank_q[1] >= $past(vieta_deg_bank_q[1])))) &&
       ((csr_0x68_fine_tm_q  & ~$past(csr_0x68_fine_tm_q))  == 64'h0) &&
       ((csr_0x70_spid_live_q & ~$past(csr_0x70_spid_live_q)) == 64'h0));
endproperty
assert property (p_monotonic_csr_ratchet_no_escalation_across_flr);

// Per-Slot Assertions (Generated Across Slots s in {0..3}): SVA 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 10
generate
  for (genvar s = 0; s < 4; s = s + 1) begin : g_sva_slots
    // SVA 2: Stage-1 to Stage-4 (##3) Surgical NOP, Credit Conservation & WSTRB Gate
    property p_surgical_nop_and_credit_conservation;
      @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
      (s1_flit_valid_q && s1_slot_viol_vec_q[s] && s1_crd_qual_q[s])
      |-> ##3 ((m_pcie_crd_pulse_c4_o[s] == 1'b1) && (m_hbm_wstrb_c4_o[16*s +: 16] == 16'h0000));
    endproperty
    assert property (p_surgical_nop_and_credit_conservation);

    // SVA 3: 100% Co-Packed Innocent Neighbor Slot Survival
    property p_innocent_copacked_slot_survival;
      @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
      (s1_flit_valid_q && !s1_slot_viol_vec_q[s] && (s1_slot_viol_rep_q[s] == 8'h00))
      |=> ((m_flit_payload_q[128*s+16 +: 112] == $past(s1_flit_payload_q[128*s+16 +: 112])) &&
           ((s == 0 && $past(s1_flit_phase_id_q == 2'b00))
             ? (m_flit_payload_q[3:0] == $past(s1_flit_payload_q[3:0]))
             : (m_flit_payload_q[128*s +: 16] == $past(s1_flit_payload_q[128*s +: 16]))));
    endproperty
    assert property (p_innocent_copacked_slot_survival);

    // SVA 4: Unconditional M2S_BIRsp & Snoop DirtyEvict Progress
    property p_bi_snoop_and_dirty_evict_deadlock_free_bypass;
      @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
      (s_flit_valid_i && (slot_is_bi_rsp_i[s] || slot_is_snoop_evict_i[s]) &&
       !slot_is_rwd_data_i[s] && !slot_is_drs_data_i[s])
      |=> (s1_slot_viol_vec_q[s] == 1'b0);
    endproperty
    assert property (p_bi_snoop_and_dirty_evict_deadlock_free_bypass);

    // SVA 6: Cycle-0 Vieta/Time-Wheel/Tensor & Prefix-Guarded SPID Check
    property p_stateless_4kb_cohort_revocation_1cycle;
      @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
      (s_flit_valid_i && slot_is_cmd_hdr_i[s] && !slot_is_bi_rsp_i[s] && !slot_is_snoop_evict_i[s] &&
       ((s_slot_cap_vec_i[64*s +: 64] == 64'h0) ||
        (s_slot_spid_i[14*s +: 14] == 14'h0) ||
        (s_slot_spid_i[14*s+6 +: 8] != port_spid_prefix_q) ||
        !csr_0x70_spid_live_q[s_slot_spid_i[14*s +: 6]] ||
        !bank_valid_q[slot_bank_sel_i[s]] ||
        (slot_is_fine_cohort_i[s]
          ? (!slot_coarse_arena_ok_i[s] ||
             ((s_slot_cap_vec_i[64*s +: 64] & p_fine_eff_bank_w[slot_bank_sel_i[s]]) == 64'h0))
          : ((s_slot_cap_vec_i[64*s +: 64] & ~p_coarse_eff_w[63:0]) != 64'h0))))
      |=> (s1_slot_viol_vec_q[s] == 1'b1);
    endproperty
    assert property (p_stateless_4kb_cohort_revocation_1cycle);

    // SVA 8: Fractured LUT6_2 MOESI & 4-Tier S2M_DRS Mantissa Clamping
    property p_cxl_cache_moesi_and_mantissa_lut6_2_clamp;
      @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
      (s1_flit_valid_q && s1_slot_is_cache_req_q[s] && (s1_moesi_ceiling_q[s] == 2'b01))
      |=> (m_slot_moesi_state_o[2*s +: 2] <= 2'b01);
    endproperty
    assert property (p_cxl_cache_moesi_and_mantissa_lut6_2_clamp);

    property p_s2m_drs_4tier_mantissa_lut6_2_clamp;
      @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
      (s1_flit_valid_q && s1_slot_is_drs_data_q[s] && (s1_moesi_ceiling_q[s] <= 2'b10))
      |=> (m_flit_payload_q[128*s +: 4] == 4'h0);
    endproperty
    assert property (p_s2m_drs_4tier_mantissa_lut6_2_clamp);

    // SVA 10: Hitless Per-Slot Dual-Epoch Grace-Window Isolation (bank_valid_q == 2'b11)
    property p_dual_epoch_grace_window_zero_drop;
      @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
      (s_flit_valid_i && (bank_valid_q == 2'b11) && slot_is_cmd_hdr_i[s] &&
       slot_is_fine_cohort_i[s] && !slot_is_rwd_data_i[s] && !slot_is_drs_data_i[s] &&
       !slot_is_bi_rsp_i[s] && slot_coarse_arena_ok_i[s] &&
       ((s_slot_cap_vec_i[64*s +: 64] & p_fine_eff_bank_w[slot_bank_sel_i[s]]) != 64'h0) &&
       (s_slot_spid_i[14*s +: 14] != 14'h0) &&
       (s_slot_spid_i[14*s+6 +: 8] == port_spid_prefix_q) &&
       csr_0x70_spid_live_q[s_slot_spid_i[14*s +: 6]])
      |=> (s1_slot_viol_vec_q[s] == 1'b0);
    endproperty
    assert property (p_dual_epoch_grace_window_zero_drop);
  end
endgenerate

// === Enclave-Level Assertions: SVA 5, 7, 9 ===
// SVA 5: Canonical Egress Lockout (CSR 0x58)
property p_canonical_egress_zero_leakage;
  @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
  (!egress_proof_valid_i || egress_lockout_q) |=> (m_canonical_egress_digest_o == 64'h0);
endproperty
assert property (p_canonical_egress_zero_leakage);

// SVA 7: 4-Channel 64-Cacheline OOO DMA Replay Ratchet
property p_ooo_64cacheline_dma_replay_annihilation;
  @(posedge clk_i) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
  (beat_fire && dma_line_live[chan] && !auth_new_burst_desc_i)
  |=> (dma_live_mask_q[$past(chan)][$past(hpa_line_idx)] == 1'b0);
endproperty
assert property (p_ooo_64cacheline_dma_replay_annihilation);

// SVA 9: Per-ARID Scrub Shim ARVALID Suppression & Same-ARID Inflight Ordering Gate
property p_axi4_scrub_shim_arvalid_and_arid_order;
  @(posedge clk_f2_250mhz) disable iff (!por_cold_rst_n)
  (s_axi_arvalid_i && m_axi_aruser_scrub_o)
  |-> ((m_hbm_axi_arvalid_o == 1'b0) &&
       ((arid_hbm_inflight_cnt_q[s_axi_arid_i[3:0]] > 2'd0) ? (m_axi_scrub_rvalid_o == 1'b0) : 1'b1));
endproperty
assert property (p_axi4_scrub_shim_arvalid_and_arid_order);
\end{lstlisting}
